`CONTRIBUTING.md`:

```md
# CONTRIBUTING

## 提交规范

标题一律 `type: 描述` 形式：

- `type` 用英文小写：`feat` / `fix` / `chore` / `docs` / `refactor` / `test` / `ci` / `perf`
- 描述用中文，一句话说清「改了什么」，不写「修改了一些问题」这种空话
- **不使用括号**（全角 `（）` 与半角 `()` 都不出现），因此**不写 `type(scope):` 形式**
- 标题里不要堆多个改动点；一个提交只做一件事，拆不开就说明提交粒度错了

```
✅ feat: 应用内交互与详情卡
✅ fix: 非法 verdict 不再计入 clean
✅ chore: 初始化仓库
❌ feat(ui): 应用内交互与详情卡        ← 有 scope
❌ fix(core): 修复一个 bug              ← 有 scope 且描述空洞
```

**提交前先确认标题**：动手前把拟好的标题发出来，确认后再执行 `git commit`。

## 首个提交

仓库的第一个提交固定是 `chore: 初始化仓库`，只放工程骨架：
`.gitignore`、`.gitattributes`、`LICENSE`、空的 `README.md`。
功能与文档一律放在后续提交里 —— 否则 `git log` 的第一屏就是一大坨，读不出这个仓库是怎么长出来的。

## 提交粒度

按**目录边界**切，一个提交只碰一个子系统（`canary/` / `cli/` / `docs/` / `tools/`）。
原因是这个仓库由多个并行工作面推进，按目录切既好 review 也好 revert。

## 改动前必读

| 你要改 | 先读 |
| --- | --- |
| 检测向量 | `docs/04-契约.md`（冻结契约）+ `docs/02-探针目录.md` |
| 评分逻辑 | `docs/03-评分模型.md`（`cli/litmus_score.py` 是权威实现） |
| 交互 / 报告 | `docs/07-交互与报告规范.md`（I1–I10 / R1–R8 是验收指标） |
| 构建链 | `canary/build.ps1`（无 gradle，`aapt2 + javac + d8 + clang++ + apksigner`） |

## 提交前必须全绿

```powershell
python tools\lint_vectors.py          # 契约门禁（tier C 不许判 TAMPERED）
python tools\gen_vector_docs.py --check  # VectorDocs.java 与 vectors.json 同步
python tools\coverage.py              # 覆盖度审计（看缺口有没有扩大）
python cli\litmus.py selftest          # 137 项断言
pwsh -File canary\build.ps1 -NoNative # 至少在无 native 路径下能构建
```

## 代码风格

- **零第三方依赖**是硬约束：`import` 只允许 `android.*` / `java.*` / `org.json` / `io.litmus.*`
- UI 全部代码构建（不写 layout xml），视觉 token 见 `docs/07` §5
- 注释写**为什么**，不写「这里遍历列表」这种复述代码的注释
- native 只做机械取证，判定一律在 Java 侧（改判据不该重编 `.so`）

```

`README.md`:

```md
# Litmus · 试金石

<p align="center">
  <b>把「过检测」变成一个可复现的分数</b><br/>
  <i>Patch the canary, then weigh what's left.</i>
</p>

<p align="center">
  <a href="#"><img alt="Platform" src="https://img.shields.io/badge/Android-8.0%20~%2016-3DDC84"></a>
  <a href="#"><img alt="Vectors" src="https://img.shields.io/badge/Vectors-53%20catalog%20%2F%2045%20implemented-6C5CE7"></a>
  <a href="#"><img alt="Deps" src="https://img.shields.io/badge/Dependencies-0-2EA043"></a>
  <a href="LICENSE"><img alt="License" src="https://img.shields.io/badge/License-MIT-2F81F7"></a>
</p>

> 试金石不改变金子，只告诉你成色还剩多少。我们对你的补丁做的是同一件事。

---

## 是什么

我们发布一个普通 Android 应用（靶包 `canary`），你随便修补它——重打包、换签名、注入 Frida、上 LSPosed、塞 Zygisk 模块、丢进 VirtualApp、跑在模拟器里。检测内核跑 **53 条向量**，最后给出：

| 输出 | 含义 |
| --- | --- |
| **Litmus Score**（0–100） | 越高 = 我们越看不出来 |
| **盲区 Blind Spot** | 你确实改了包，但某一整类一条都没抓到 → **我们的洞** |
| **误报 False Positive** | 包没动却被冤枉 → 这个环境里那条检测不可信 |

| 总览 | 应用内 HTML 报告 |
| --- | --- |
| ![总览](docs/images/ui-home.png) | ![报告](docs/images/ui-html-report.png) |

## 快速开始

```powershell
python cli/litmus.py doctor                                   # 环境自检
pwsh -File canary/build.ps1 -Install -Serial <设备序列号>       # 构建并安装靶包
python cli/litmus.py run --label C1-clean                     # 记基线
pwsh -File tools/make-repack.ps1                              # 模拟「用户重打包」
python cli/litmus.py run --label C2-repack --baseline runs/C1-clean-*/run.json
python cli/litmus.py matrix --runs runs                        # 跨版本对比
```

需要：JDK 11+、Android SDK（build-tools + 一台 arm64/x86_64 设备）。**不需要 gradle**，构建链是 `aapt2 + javac + d8 + clang++ + apksigner`。

## 为什么不是又一个 Momo

| | Momo / Ruru 类 | Litmus |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 输入 | 你的设备 | **你修补过的包** + 设备，两个平面 |
| 输出 | 一串红绿灯 | 分数 + 类别分 + **盲区 + 误报**，稳定 JSON |
| 可复现 | 靠人截图 | `run.json` 可 diff、可进 CI |
| 误报 | 无基线概念 | **基线归一**：只算你额外引入的脏 |
| 能力边界 | 闭源、不可证伪 | 盲区矩阵强制暴露「哪类根本没抓到」 |

## 通吃靠不变量，不靠特征

每条向量必须能写出一句「你做了任何注入都会破坏的等式」：

| 你做的手脚 | 一定被破坏的等式 |
| --- | --- |
| inline hook（Dobby / ShadowHook / LSPlant） | 函数入口**运行时字节 ≠ 磁盘字节** |
| PLT/GOT 劫持 | GOT 槽落点不在合法模块的 `r-x` 区间 |
| Frida 注入 | 匿名可执行段 / `TracerPid≠0` / memfd 映射 |
| 重打包、换签名 | 签名链 / APK 哈希 / ZipEntry 指纹 |
| syscall hook | 裸 `syscall` 与 libc 返回值不一致 |

**硬约束**：`tier C`（特征匹配）永远不许单独判「被坐实」，只能「可疑」，由 `tools/lint_vectors.py` 在 CI 里守着。

## 三平面

```
SELF    进程内：看得深，但可被 hook 伪造
DEVICE  设备面：看得广，但误报高（只能出「可疑」）
CLI     进程外：只有 APK 文件 + 构建期期望值 —— 包改不了这层，所以它是分数权威
```

检测器自己就跑在被修补的包里，所以凡是能在进程内被伪造的结论，都不能单独作为最终判据。

## 现状

| 已验证 | 未验证 |
| --- | --- |
| 重打包 / 换签名：5 条 A 级向量坐实，包完整性 100 → 36.1 | **注入类向量从未做过阳性对照**（没有一次真实的 Frida/LSPosed 检出记录） |
| 干净包：0 盲区 0 误报，inline 扫描 4056 个入口 0 命中 | Java 层 hook 的直接证据（`rt.art.entrypoint` 未实现） |
| CLI ≤ Java：137 项断言 + 评分公式逐字段对拍 | 内核态 hook、硬件断点、TEE 证明（见 `docs/08`） |

结论先说清楚：**现在能抓「改包」，还不能声称能抓「注入」。** 详见 [docs/08-覆盖度评估.md](docs/08-覆盖度评估.md)。

## 参考与依据

设计取舍不是拍脑袋，依据如下。

**平台机制（我们依赖的事实）**

- [APK Signature Scheme v2](https://source.android.com/docs/security/features/apksigning/v2) / [v3](https://source.android.com/docs/security/features/apksigning/v3) — 签名覆盖整包字节，`apkContentsSigners` 与 lineage 的语义
- [Android 10 隐私变更](https://developer.android.com/about/versions/10/privacy/changes) — 跨 uid 读 `/proc/<pid>/` 被收回，这是「检测内核必须内嵌」的根本原因
- [Android SELinux](https://source.android.com/docs/security/features/selinux) — `untrusted_app` 对 `/proc/self/mem`、`/proc/net` 的 neverallow
- [ART runtime](https://source.android.com/docs/core/runtime) — JIT code cache 与 `memfd:jit-*` 是干净机的常客，也是我们过滤误报的依据
- [Play Integrity API](https://developer.android.com/google/play/integrity) — 硬件级证明；我们没做，但它是「本地方案挡不住重放」的对照物

**标准与测试方法**

- [OWASP MASVS](https://mas.owasp.org/MASVS/) — RESILIENCE 类（防篡改、防逆向、反调试、防 hook）就是本项目的靶心
- [OWASP MASTG](https://mas.owasp.org/MASTG/) — 对应测试项与判定口径
- [MITRE ATT&CK Mobile](https://attack.mitre.org/matrices/mobile/) — Defense Evasion 战术，如 T1406 Obfuscated Files or Information

**我们对着什么做检测**

- [Frida](https://frida.re/docs/home/) — ptrace 注入 / gadget 内存加载两条路径
- [LSPlant](https://github.com/LSPosed/LSPlant) — ART 层 hook 入口改写
- [Dobby](https://github.com/jmpews/Dobby) / [ShadowHook](https://github.com/bytedance/android-inline-hook) — inline hook 与 PLT hook 的实现方式
- [Magisk](https://github.com/topjohnwu/Magisk) / [KernelSU](https://github.com/tiann/KernelSU) — 提权面与模块注入

**学术测量**

- Zhou & Jiang, *Dissecting Android Malware: Characterization and Evolution*, IEEE S&P 2012 — 重打包在恶意样本中的占比，是「为什么要做包完整性」的经典依据
- OWASP MASVS-RESILIENCE 的威胁模型条目 — 对抗性逆向的分级假设

## 合法使用

本项目用于验证**你自己应用 / 你自己补丁方案**的抗篡改能力。靶包是我们自己发布的、不含第三方代码的测试应用。
禁止用于绕过第三方真实保护或未授权修改他人应用。检测结果只落在本机，无任何上报。

## License

MIT · 见 [LICENSE](LICENSE)。

`canary/AndroidManifest.xml`:

```xml
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
<!--
  Litmus 靶包（canary）。契约见 docs/04-契约.md。

  注意 appComponentFactory 这一行是**故意的占位属性**：
  部分手工打包器对「清单里没有就新增属性、有就替换值”」两种分支处理不一致，
  新增属性在某些设备的 PackageParser 上会被忽略，导致注入桩不生效。
  这里预置一个系统自带的空实现，逼打包器走「替换值」分支。
  值本身不参与任何逻辑（LitmusApp 才是真正的入口）。
-->
<manifest xmlns:android="http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android"
    package="io.litmus.canary"
    android:versionCode="1"
    android:versionName="0.1.0">

    <uses-sdk
        android:minSdkVersion="26"
        android:targetSdkVersion="35" />

    <!-- 用于扫框架包名 / 多开容器 / 模拟器相关包；tier C 弱信号专用 -->
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.QUERY_ALL_PACKAGES" />

    <!--
      INTERNET 只用于 **loopback** 探测（127.0.0.1:27042/27043 的 frida-server 默认端口）。
      Android 建 socket 一律要求该权限，即使目标是自己。
      本应用没有任何外发请求：全仓库不存在 http 客户端、上报地址或统计 SDK。
    -->
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.INTERNET" />

    <application
        android:name=".LitmusApp"
        android:allowBackup="false"
        android:appComponentFactory="android.app.AppComponentFactory"
        android:extractNativeLibs="true"
        android:label="@string/app_name"
        android:supportsRtl="true"
        android:theme="@android:style/Theme.DeviceDefault.Light.NoActionBar">

        <activity
            android:name=".MainActivity"
            android:configChanges="orientation|screenSize|keyboardHidden"
            android:exported="true"
            android:label="@string/app_name">
            <intent-filter>
                <action android:name="android.intent.action.MAIN" />
                <category android:name="android.intent.category.LAUNCHER" />
            </intent-filter>
        </activity>

        <!--
          应用内 HTML 报告预览。exported=true 是有意的：
          验收与 CI 都要能用 `adb shell am start -n io.litmus.canary/.ReportViewer` 直接拉起，
          而它只渲染本应用自己生成的报告（不含任何凭据），暴露面等于零。
        -->
        <activity
            android:name=".ReportViewer"
            android:configChanges="orientation|screenSize|keyboardHidden"
            android:exported="true"
            android:label="@string/app_name" />
    </application>
</manifest>

```

`canary/assets/litmus-build.properties`:

```properties
# 构建期由 canary/build.ps1 覆盖。这个文件是「开发态默认值」，
# 让源码树不构建也能被 IDE / javac 读懂。
litmus.version=0.1.0-dev
litmus.flavor=dev
litmus.buildUtc=1970-01-01T00:00:00Z
litmus.kitId=dev
litmus.hasNative=false
litmus.expectedCertSha256=
litmus.expectedLegacyCertSha256=
litmus.expectedCertHistory=[]
litmus.expectedSignerCount=1

```

`canary/build.ps1`:

```ps1
<#
.SYNOPSIS
    Litmus 靶包（canary）手工构建脚本：aapt2 + javac + d8 + clang++ + zipalign + apksigner，不依赖 gradle。

.DESCRIPTION
    产出：
      out/litmus-canary-<ver>.apk        靶包
      out/build-info.json               构建元数据（apkSha256 / certSha256 / zipFingerprint）
      out/keys/litmus.p12                自举的签名密钥（首次构建生成，之后复用）

    为什么不用 gradle：目标机没有 gradle/kotlinc，且安全工具的构建链越短越可信。
    native 为什么直接调 clang++ 而不是走 cmake：本机沙箱里 ninja/make 执行规则会挂死
    （`couldn't create signal pipe`），而 clang++ 单命令编译 0.5s 就完事。
    canary/jni/CMakeLists.txt 保留，给 IDE / 其它平台用。

    签名自举：首次构建用 keytool 生成 PKCS12；证书 SHA-256 在**签名前**就能算出来，
    因此可以烘进 assets/litmus-build.properties 作为「原始证书期望值」。
    APK 自身的 SHA-256 / Zip 指纹产生于签名之后，烘不进自己 —— 由 cli 平面比对。

.PARAMETER OutDir
    输出目录，默认 canary/out。

.PARAMETER Serial
    adb 设备序列号；不传则要求恰好一台在线设备。

.PARAMETER Install
    构建完安装到设备（覆盖安装）。

.PARAMETER Uninstall
    先卸载再装（清数据，用于干净基线）。

.PARAMETER NoNative
    跳过 native 探针（liblitmusprobe.so），Java 侧 native 向量会降级成 UNKNOWN。

.PARAMETER Debuggable
    构建 debug 版（清单加 android:debuggable，env.debuggable 会命中 TAMPERED）。

.PARAMETER Screenshot
    安装启动后截图到 out/ui-home.png。

.PARAMETER Logcat
    构建安装后 dump 最近的 LitmusReport 日志。

.EXAMPLE
    pwsh -File canary/build.ps1 -Install -Screenshot -Logcat -Serial emulator-5554
#>
[CmdletBinding()]
param(
    [string]$OutDir = '',
    [string]$Serial = '',
    [switch]$Install,
    [switch]$Uninstall,
    [switch]$NoNative,
    [switch]$Debuggable,
    [switch]$Screenshot,
    [switch]$Logcat,
    [switch]$KeepWork,
    [int]$MinSdk = 26,
    [int]$TargetSdk = 35,
    [string]$CompileSdk = '',
    [string]$Abis = 'arm64-v8a,x86_64',
    [string]$Version = '0.1.0'
)

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'

$CanaryDir = $PSScriptRoot
$RepoRoot = Split-Path $CanaryDir -Parent
if (-not $OutDir) { $OutDir = Join-Path $CanaryDir 'out' }
$Work = Join-Path $OutDir 'work'
$GenDir = Join-Path $Work 'gen'
$ClassesDir = Join-Path $Work 'classes'
$DexDir = Join-Path $Work 'dex'
$AssetsWork = Join-Path $Work 'assets'
$NatOut = Join-Path $Work 'native'

$script:stepNo = 0
function Write-Step([string]$msg) {
    $script:stepNo++
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host ("[{0:d2}] {1}" -f $script:stepNo, $msg) -ForegroundColor Cyan
}
function Write-Ok([string]$msg) { Write-Host "     $msg" -ForegroundColor DarkGray }
function Write-Warn2([string]$msg) { Write-Host "     ! $msg" -ForegroundColor Yellow }
function Fail([string]$msg) { throw "[litmus-build] $msg" }
function Write-Utf8([string]$path, [string]$text) {
    $dir = Split-Path $path -Parent
    if ($dir -and -not (Test-Path $dir)) { New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $dir | Out-Null }
    [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($path, $text, (New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)))
}
function Get-Sha256Hex([string]$path) { return (Get-FileHash -Algorithm SHA256 -Path $path).Hash.ToLowerInvariant() }
function New-Dir([string]$path) { if (-not (Test-Path $path)) { New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $path | Out-Null } }

# ================================================================ 01 环境
Write-Step '定位 JDK / Android SDK / build-tools'

# 工具链全部靠环境自动探测：公开仓库里不写死任何盘符
$JdkHome = if ($env:JAVA_HOME) { $env:JAVA_HOME } else {
    $javacCmd = Get-Command javac -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    if ($javacCmd) { Split-Path (Split-Path $javacCmd.Source -Parent) -Parent } else { '' }
}
$SdkRoot = if ($env:ANDROID_SDK_ROOT) { $env:ANDROID_SDK_ROOT }
           elseif ($env:ANDROID_HOME) { $env:ANDROID_HOME }
           else { '' }
if (-not $SdkRoot -or -not (Test-Path $SdkRoot)) {
    Fail '找不到 Android SDK：请设置 ANDROID_SDK_ROOT 或 ANDROID_HOME'
}
if (-not $JdkHome -or -not (Test-Path (Join-Path $JdkHome 'bin\javac.exe'))) {
    Fail '找不到 JDK（需要 11+）：请设置 JAVA_HOME'
}

$BuildTools = if ($env:ANDROID_BUILD_TOOLS) { $env:ANDROID_BUILD_TOOLS } else {
    $bt = Get-ChildItem (Join-Path $SdkRoot 'build-tools') -Directory -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
          Sort-Object { [version]($_.Name -replace '[^0-9\.].*$','') } -Descending | Select-Object -First 1
    if ($bt) { $bt.FullName } else { '' }
}
if (-not $BuildTools) { Fail "在 $SdkRoot\build-tools 下找不到任何 build-tools" }

# 未指定编译平台时取已安装的最高版本
if (-not $CompileSdk) {
    $plat = Get-ChildItem (Join-Path $SdkRoot 'platforms') -Directory -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
            Where-Object { $_.Name -match '^android-\d' } |
            Sort-Object { [int](($_.Name -replace '^android-','') -split '\.')[0] } -Descending |
            Select-Object -First 1
    if (-not $plat) { Fail "在 $SdkRoot\platforms 下找不到 android-* 平台" }
    $CompileSdk = $plat.Name
}
$NdkRoot = if ($env:ANDROID_NDK) { $env:ANDROID_NDK } else { Join-Path $SdkRoot 'ndk\28.2.13676358' }

$JavacExe = Join-Path $JdkHome 'bin\javac.exe'
$KeytoolExe = Join-Path $JdkHome 'bin\keytool.exe'
$Aapt2 = Join-Path $BuildTools 'aapt2.exe'
$D8 = Join-Path $BuildTools 'd8.bat'
$ZipAlign = Join-Path $BuildTools 'zipalign.exe'
$ApkSigner = Join-Path $BuildTools 'apksigner.bat'
$AndroidJar = Join-Path $SdkRoot "platforms\$CompileSdk\android.jar"
$Adb = Join-Path $SdkRoot 'platform-tools\adb.exe'

foreach ($t in @(
        @{ n = 'javac.exe'; p = $JavacExe }, @{ n = 'keytool.exe'; p = $KeytoolExe },
        @{ n = 'aapt2.exe'; p = $Aapt2 }, @{ n = 'd8.bat'; p = $D8 },
        @{ n = 'zipalign.exe'; p = $ZipAlign }, @{ n = 'apksigner.bat'; p = $ApkSigner },
        @{ n = 'android.jar'; p = $AndroidJar },
        @{ n = 'AndroidManifest.xml'; p = (Join-Path $CanaryDir 'AndroidManifest.xml') })) {
    if (-not (Test-Path $t.p)) { Fail "找不到 $($t.n)：$($t.p)" }
}
$env:JAVA_HOME = $JdkHome
$env:PATH = (Join-Path $JdkHome 'bin') + ';' + $env:PATH

Write-Ok "JDK         = $JdkHome"
Write-Ok "build-tools = $BuildTools"
Write-Ok "android.jar = $AndroidJar"
Write-Ok "ndk         = $NdkRoot"

if (-not $KeepWork -and (Test-Path $Work)) { Remove-Item $Work -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue }
foreach ($d in @($Work, $GenDir, $ClassesDir, $DexDir, $AssetsWork, $NatOut)) { New-Dir $d }

# ================================================================ 02 签名密钥自举
Write-Step '签名密钥自举（keytool → PKCS12）'

$KeysDir = Join-Path $OutDir 'keys'
New-Dir $KeysDir
$Keystore = Join-Path $KeysDir 'litmus.p12'
$CertDer = Join-Path $KeysDir 'litmus-cert.der'
$KsPass = 'litmus-pw'   # PKCS12 要求口令 >= 6 位

if (-not (Test-Path $Keystore)) {
    Write-Ok '首次构建，生成 PKCS12 密钥（RSA2048 / 10000 天）'
    & $KeytoolExe -genkeypair -keystore $Keystore -storetype PKCS12 `
        -storepass $KsPass -keypass $KsPass -alias litmus `
        -keyalg RSA -keysize 2048 -validity 10000 `
        -dname "CN=Litmus, O=Litmus, C=CN" -noprompt 2>&1 | ForEach-Object { Write-Host "     $_" }
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { Fail "keytool 生成密钥失败 (exit $LASTEXITCODE)" }
}
# 每次构建都重新导出：keystore 被换过时旧 cert.der 会让烘进去的期望值全错
if (Test-Path $CertDer) { Remove-Item $CertDer -Force }
& $KeytoolExe -exportcert -keystore $Keystore -storepass $KsPass -alias litmus -file $CertDer 2>&1 | Out-Null
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { Fail "keytool 导出证书失败 (exit $LASTEXITCODE)" }

$CertSha256 = Get-Sha256Hex $CertDer
$KeyId = $CertSha256.Substring(0, 12)
Write-Ok "keystore    = $Keystore"
Write-Ok "certSha256  = $CertSha256"

# ================================================================ 03 assets（烘期望值）
Write-Step '生成 assets/litmus-build.properties（烘入证书期望值）'

$buildUtc = (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString('yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ssZ')
$hasNative = (-not $NoNative)
$flavor = if ($Debuggable) { 'debug' } else { 'release' }
$props = @(
    "# 构建期由 canary/build.ps1 生成，勿手工修改。"
    "litmus.version=$Version"
    "litmus.flavor=$flavor"
    "litmus.buildUtc=$buildUtc"
    "litmus.kitId=$KeyId"
    "litmus.hasNative=$($hasNative.ToString().ToLowerInvariant())"
    "litmus.expectedCertSha256=$CertSha256"
    "litmus.expectedLegacyCertSha256=$CertSha256"
    "litmus.expectedCertHistory=[`"$CertSha256`"]"
    "litmus.expectedSignerCount=1"
) -join "`n"

$srcAssets = Join-Path $CanaryDir 'assets'
if (Test-Path $srcAssets) { Copy-Item (Join-Path $srcAssets '*') $AssetsWork -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue }
Write-Utf8 (Join-Path $AssetsWork 'litmus-build.properties') ($props + "`n")
Write-Ok "assets 工作副本 = $AssetsWork"

# ================================================================ 04 资源编译 + link
Write-Step 'aapt2 compile + link'

$ResDir = Join-Path $CanaryDir 'res'
$ResZip = Join-Path $Work 'res.zip'
$BaseApk = Join-Path $Work 'base.apk'
if (Test-Path $ResZip) { Remove-Item $ResZip -Force }
if (Test-Path $BaseApk) { Remove-Item $BaseApk -Force }

& $Aapt2 compile --dir $ResDir -o $ResZip 2>&1 | Out-Null
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { Fail "aapt2 compile 失败 (exit $LASTEXITCODE)" }

$linkArgs = @(
    'link', '-o', $BaseApk,
    '--manifest', (Join-Path $CanaryDir 'AndroidManifest.xml'),
    '-I', $AndroidJar,
    '-A', $AssetsWork,
    '--java', $GenDir,
    '--min-sdk-version', "$MinSdk",
    '--target-sdk-version', "$TargetSdk",
    '--version-code', '1',
    '--version-name', $Version,
    '--auto-add-overlay',
    # targetSdk>=30 要求 resources.arsc 不压缩，否则 Android 11+ 装不上
    '-0', 'arsc'
)
if ($Debuggable) { $linkArgs += '--debug-mode' }
$linkArgs += $ResZip

& $Aapt2 @linkArgs 2>&1 | Out-Null
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { Fail "aapt2 link 失败 (exit $LASTEXITCODE)" }
Write-Ok ("base.apk = {0:N0} bytes" -f (Get-Item $BaseApk).Length)

# ================================================================ 05 javac
Write-Step 'javac（src + R.java → classes）'

$sources = @()
$sources += (Get-ChildItem (Join-Path $CanaryDir 'src') -Recurse -Filter *.java | ForEach-Object { $_.FullName })
if (Test-Path $GenDir) { $sources += (Get-ChildItem $GenDir -Recurse -Filter *.java | ForEach-Object { $_.FullName }) }
if ($sources.Count -eq 0) { Fail '没有 Java 源码' }

$argFile = Join-Path $Work 'sources.txt'
Write-Utf8 $argFile (($sources | ForEach-Object { '"' + ($_ -replace '\\', '/') + '"' }) -join "`n")
Write-Ok "$($sources.Count) 个 Java 文件"

& $JavacExe -encoding UTF-8 --release 11 -nowarn -Xlint:-options -cp $AndroidJar -d $ClassesDir "@$argFile"
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { Fail "javac 失败 (exit $LASTEXITCODE)" }
$classFiles = @(Get-ChildItem $ClassesDir -Recurse -Filter *.class | ForEach-Object { $_.FullName })
Write-Ok "$($classFiles.Count) 个 class"

# ================================================================ 06 d8
Write-Step 'd8（classes → classes.dex）'

# 逐条传 .class 会撞 Windows 命令行长度上限（76 个 class 就炸），先打成一个 jar 再喂给 d8
$JarExe = Join-Path $JdkHome 'bin\jar.exe'
$ClassesJar = Join-Path $Work 'classes.jar'
if (Test-Path $ClassesJar) { Remove-Item $ClassesJar -Force }
& $JarExe cf $ClassesJar -C $ClassesDir . 2>&1 | Out-Null
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { Fail "jar 打包 classes 失败 (exit $LASTEXITCODE)" }

& $D8 --release --min-api $MinSdk --lib $AndroidJar --output $DexDir $ClassesJar 2>&1 | Out-Null
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { Fail "d8 失败 (exit $LASTEXITCODE)" }
$DexFile = Join-Path $DexDir 'classes.dex'
if (-not (Test-Path $DexFile)) { Fail "d8 没有产出 classes.dex：$DexDir" }
Write-Ok ("classes.dex = {0:N0} bytes" -f (Get-Item $DexFile).Length)

# ================================================================ 07 native（直接 clang++）
Write-Step ("native 探针（{0}）" -f $(if ($NoNative) { '已跳过' } else { $Abis }))

$soMap = @{}
if (-not $NoNative) {
    $clangxx = Join-Path $NdkRoot 'toolchains\llvm\prebuilt\windows-x86_64\bin\clang++.exe'
    $jniDir = Join-Path $CanaryDir 'jni'
    $cppFiles = @(Get-ChildItem $jniDir -Filter *.cpp | ForEach-Object { $_.FullName })
    if (-not (Test-Path $clangxx) -or $cppFiles.Count -eq 0) {
        Write-Warn2 "找不到 clang++（$clangxx）或 jni/*.cpp，降级为纯 Java"
        $hasNative = $false
    } else {
        foreach ($abi in ($Abis -split ',')) {
            $abi = $abi.Trim()
            $trip = switch ($abi) {
                'arm64-v8a' { 'aarch64-linux-android' }
                'x86_64' { 'x86_64-linux-android' }
                'armeabi-v7a' { 'armv7a-linux-androideabi' }
                default { $null }
            }
            if (-not $trip) { Write-Warn2 "不认识的 ABI: $abi，跳过"; continue }
            $abiOut = Join-Path $NatOut $abi
            New-Dir $abiOut
            $so = Join-Path $abiOut 'liblitmusprobe.so'
            if (Test-Path $so) { Remove-Item $so -Force }
            $cargs = @(
                "--target=$trip$MinSdk",
                # -Os + -s：体积优先。JNI 走 .dynsym，strip 掉 .symtab 不影响 dlsym
                '-std=c++17', '-Os', '-s', '-fPIC', '-shared', '-fno-exceptions', '-fno-rtti',
                # 必须静态链接 libc++：否则 .so 会 NEEDED libc++_shared.so，而 APK 里没有那个库，
                # dlopen 直接失败，报的却是含糊的 UnsatisfiedLinkError: no error!
                '-static-libstdc++',
                '-fvisibility=hidden', '-ffunction-sections', '-fdata-sections',
                '-Wl,--gc-sections', '-Wl,--strip-all',
                '-Wall', '-Wextra', '-Wno-unused-parameter',
                '-DNDEBUG', '-o', $so
            ) + $cppFiles + @('-llog', '-ldl')
            & $clangxx @cargs 2>&1 | ForEach-Object { Write-Host "     $_" }
            if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0 -or -not (Test-Path $so)) {
                Write-Warn2 "$abi 编译失败，跳过该 ABI"
                continue
            }
            $soMap[$abi] = $so
            Write-Ok ("{0,-12} liblitmusprobe.so = {1:N0} bytes" -f $abi, (Get-Item $so).Length)
        }
        if ($soMap.Count -eq 0) {
            Write-Warn2 '所有 ABI 都没编出 native → hasNative=false（native 向量会出 UNKNOWN）'
            $hasNative = $false
        }
    }
}

# hasNative 最终值回写 assets（必须在 04 之前决定，所以这里重写一次 assets）
if ($hasNative) {
    # placeholder: props 里的值已经是对的
} else {
    Write-Utf8 (Join-Path $AssetsWork 'litmus-build.properties') `
        (($props -replace 'litmus.hasNative=true', 'litmus.hasNative=false') + "`n")
    # assets 变了必须重新 link，否则 APK 里还是旧值
    Write-Ok '重新 link（assets 中 hasNative 变更）'
    if (Test-Path $BaseApk) { Remove-Item $BaseApk -Force }
    & $Aapt2 @linkArgs 2>&1 | Out-Null
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { Fail "aapt2 link 二次执行失败 (exit $LASTEXITCODE)" }
}

# ================================================================ 08 组包（新建 zip，逐条拷贝）
Write-Step '组包（base.apk 条目 + classes.dex + lib/*.so）'

Add-Type -AssemblyName System.IO.Compression.FileSystem
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.IO.Compression

$Unsigned = Join-Path $Work 'unsigned.apk'
if (Test-Path $Unsigned) { Remove-Item $Unsigned -Force }

$srcZip = [System.IO.Compression.ZipFile]::OpenRead($BaseApk)
$dstStream = [System.IO.File]::Open($Unsigned, [System.IO.FileMode]::CreateNew)
$dstZip = New-Object System.IO.Compression.ZipArchive($dstStream, [System.IO.Compression.ZipArchiveMode]::Create)
try {
    # 1) 原样搬 base.apk 的条目（arsc 用 Stored，其余 Optimal）
    foreach ($e in $srcZip.Entries) {
        $level = if ($e.FullName -eq 'resources.arsc') {
            [System.IO.Compression.CompressionLevel]::NoCompression
        } else {
            [System.IO.Compression.CompressionLevel]::Optimal
        }
        $ne = $dstZip.CreateEntry($e.FullName, $level)
        if ($e.Length -gt 0) {
            $is = $e.Open()
            $os = $ne.Open()
            try { $is.CopyTo($os) } finally { $os.Dispose(); $is.Dispose() }
        }
    }
    # 2) classes.dex
    $de = $dstZip.CreateEntry('classes.dex', [System.IO.Compression.CompressionLevel]::Optimal)
    $ds = $de.Open(); $df = [System.IO.File]::OpenRead($DexFile)
    try { $df.CopyTo($ds) } finally { $df.Dispose(); $ds.Dispose() }
    # 3) lib/<abi>/liblitmusprobe.so —— 不压缩，便于 zipalign -p 做页对齐
    foreach ($abi in $soMap.Keys) {
        $ne = $dstZip.CreateEntry("lib/$abi/liblitmusprobe.so", [System.IO.Compression.CompressionLevel]::NoCompression)
        $os = $ne.Open(); $fsrc = [System.IO.File]::OpenRead($soMap[$abi])
        try { $fsrc.CopyTo($os) } finally { $fsrc.Dispose(); $os.Dispose() }
    }
} finally {
    $dstZip.Dispose(); $dstStream.Dispose(); $srcZip.Dispose()
}
Write-Ok ("unsigned.apk = {0:N0} bytes" -f (Get-Item $Unsigned).Length)

# ================================================================ 09 zipalign + 签名
Write-Step 'zipalign + apksigner'

$Aligned = Join-Path $Work 'aligned.apk'
if (Test-Path $Aligned) { Remove-Item $Aligned -Force }
& $ZipAlign -p -f 4 $Unsigned $Aligned 2>&1 | Out-Null
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { Fail "zipalign 失败 (exit $LASTEXITCODE)" }

$OutApk = Join-Path $OutDir "litmus-canary-$Version.apk"
if (Test-Path $OutApk) { Remove-Item $OutApk -Force }

& $ApkSigner sign `
    --ks $Keystore --ks-key-alias litmus `
    --ks-pass "pass:$KsPass" --key-pass "pass:$KsPass" `
    --min-sdk-version $MinSdk `
    --v1-signing-enabled false --v2-signing-enabled true `
    --v3-signing-enabled true --v4-signing-enabled false `
    --out $OutApk $Aligned 2>&1 | ForEach-Object { Write-Host "     $_" }
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { Fail "apksigner sign 失败 (exit $LASTEXITCODE)" }

$verify = & $ApkSigner verify --print-certs --min-sdk-version $MinSdk $OutApk 2>&1
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { Fail ("apksigner verify 失败：`n" + ($verify -join "`n")) }
$signerLine = ($verify | Where-Object { $_ -match 'Signer #1 certificate SHA-256 digest' } | Select-Object -First 1)
Write-Ok "$signerLine"

# ================================================================ 10 build-info
Write-Step '生成 build-info.json'

$apkSha256 = Get-Sha256Hex $OutApk
$zipFp = ''
$zipfpPy = Join-Path $RepoRoot 'tools\zipfp.py'
if (Test-Path $zipfpPy) {
    $zipFp = ((& python $zipfpPy $OutApk 2>&1 | Select-Object -Last 1) -as [string]).Trim()
} else {
    Write-Warn2 '找不到 tools/zipfp.py，zipFingerprint 留空'
}
$apkSize = (Get-Item $OutApk).Length

$info = [ordered]@{
    schema         = 'litmus.build/1'
    apk            = (Resolve-Path $OutApk).Path
    apkSha256      = $apkSha256
    certSha256     = $CertSha256
    zipFingerprint = $zipFp
    version        = $Version
    flavor         = $flavor
    builtAt        = $buildUtc
    minSdk         = $MinSdk
    targetSdk      = $TargetSdk
    abis           = @($soMap.Keys | Sort-Object)
    sizeBytes      = $apkSize
}
Write-Utf8 (Join-Path $OutDir 'build-info.json') (($info | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 4) + "`n")

Write-Ok ("APK       = {0}" -f $OutApk)
Write-Ok ("size      = {0:N0} bytes" -f $apkSize)
Write-Ok ("apkSha256 = {0}" -f $apkSha256)
Write-Ok ("zipFp     = {0}" -f $zipFp)
Write-Ok ("abis      = {0}" -f $(if ($soMap.Count) { ($soMap.Keys | Sort-Object) -join ',' } else { 'none' }))

# ================================================================ 11 设备动作
if (-not ($Install -or $Uninstall -or $Screenshot -or $Logcat)) {
    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "构建完成。上设备：pwsh -File canary/build.ps1 -Install -Logcat -Serial emulator-5554" -ForegroundColor Green
    exit 0
}

Write-Step '设备操作（adb）'
if (-not (Test-Path $Adb)) { Fail "找不到 adb：$Adb" }

$adbArgs = @()
if ($Serial) {
    $adbArgs = @('-s', $Serial)
} else {
    $devices = @(& $Adb devices | Select-Object -Skip 1 | Where-Object { $_ -match '\sdevice$' })
    if ($devices.Count -ne 1) { Fail "在线设备 $($devices.Count) 台，请用 -Serial 指定" }
    $Serial = ($devices[0] -split '\s+')[0]
    $adbArgs = @('-s', $Serial)
}
Write-Ok "device = $Serial"

if ($Uninstall) {
    & $Adb @adbArgs uninstall io.litmus.canary 2>&1 | Out-Null
    Write-Ok '已卸载（清数据）'
}
if ($Install -or $Uninstall) {
    & $Adb @adbArgs logcat -c 2>&1 | Out-Null
    $r = & $Adb @adbArgs install -r -t $OutApk 2>&1
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { Fail ("adb install 失败：`n" + ($r -join "`n")) }
    Write-Ok (($r -join ' ').Trim())
    & $Adb @adbArgs shell am force-stop io.litmus.canary 2>&1 | Out-Null
    $startOut = & $Adb @adbArgs shell am start -W -n io.litmus.canary/.MainActivity 2>&1
    Write-Ok ($startOut -join ' ')
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 4
}
if ($Screenshot) {
    $png = Join-Path $OutDir 'ui-home.png'
    if (Test-Path $png) { Remove-Item $png -Force }
    # exec-out 是二进制流，PowerShell 的 > 会按文本落盘把 PNG 写坏，必须交给 cmd.exe
    & cmd /c "`"$Adb`" $($adbArgs -join ' ') exec-out screencap -p > `"$png`""
    if (Test-Path $png) { Write-Ok ("截图 = {0} ({1:N0} bytes)" -f $png, (Get-Item $png).Length) }
    else { Write-Warn2 '截图失败' }
}
if ($Logcat) {
    $lines = & $Adb @adbArgs logcat -d -s LitmusReport 2>&1 | Select-String -Pattern 'LITMUS_REPORT' | Select-Object -Last 3
    foreach ($l in $lines) {
        $s = $l.ToString()
        Write-Ok $(if ($s.Length -gt 900) { $s.Substring(0, 900) + ' …' } else { $s })
    }
}

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "完成。" -ForegroundColor Green

```

`canary/jni/CMakeLists.txt`:

```txt
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.22.1)
project(litmusprobe CXX)

# 构建：canary/build.ps1 会对每个 ABI 各调一次，产物 liblitmusprobe.so
# 手工：
#   cmake -S . -B ../build/native/arm64-v8a \
#         -DCMAKE_TOOLCHAIN_FILE=$env:ANDROID_NDK/build/cmake/android.toolchain.cmake \
#         -DANDROID_ABI=arm64-v8a -DANDROID_PLATFORM=android-26 \
#         -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release
#   cmake --build ../build/native/arm64-v8a

add_library(litmusprobe SHARED
        litmus_probe.cpp
        elf_scan.cpp)

target_compile_features(litmusprobe PRIVATE cxx_std_17)
target_compile_options(litmusprobe PRIVATE
        -Wall -Wextra -Wno-unused-parameter
        -fvisibility=hidden
        -fno-exceptions -fno-rtti)

find_library(LOG_LIB log)
# dladdr() 用来定位自己这个 .so（gotCheck 需要自己的 load bias）
find_library(DL_LIB dl)
target_link_libraries(litmusprobe ${LOG_LIB} ${DL_LIB})

set_target_properties(litmusprobe PROPERTIES
        CXX_VISIBILITY_PRESET hidden
        VISIBILITY_INLINES_HIDDEN ON
        OUTPUT_NAME "litmusprobe")

```

`canary/jni/elf_scan.cpp`:

```cpp
// Litmus 检测内核 · 内存侧不变量扫描（与框架无关）
//
// 见 elf_scan.h 的设计说明。本文件不 include <elf.h>：Android 上要同时处理
// 32/64 位 ELF，而宿主编译期的 ElfW 只对应本进程 ABI，所以按 ELF 规范手写偏移。

#include "elf_scan.h"

#include <dlfcn.h>
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <sys/mman.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <sys/sysmacros.h>
#include <time.h>
#include <unistd.h>

#include <algorithm>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>
#include <map>

namespace litmus {
namespace {

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ 硬上限
constexpr int kMaxLibs = 40;            // 最多扫 40 个 .so
constexpr int kMaxSymsPerLib = 200;     // 每个 .so 最多比对 200 个函数入口
constexpr uint64_t kCompareBytes = 16;  // 函数入口比对窗口
constexpr uint64_t kMaxElfBytes = 512ull * 1024 * 1024;
constexpr uint64_t kMaxDynsym = 200000;  // dynsym 条目数的合理性上限
constexpr int kMaxPltEntries = 512;
constexpr size_t kMaxSamples = 8;

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ ELF 常量
constexpr uint8_t kElfClass32 = 1;
constexpr uint8_t kElfClass64 = 2;

constexpr uint32_t kPtLoad = 1;
constexpr uint32_t kPtDynamic = 2;
constexpr uint32_t kPfX = 1;

constexpr uint8_t kSttFunc = 2;

constexpr uint32_t kShtDynsym = 11;

constexpr uint64_t kDtNull = 0;
constexpr uint64_t kDtPltrelsz = 2;
constexpr uint64_t kDtHash = 4;
constexpr uint64_t kDtStrtab = 5;
constexpr uint64_t kDtSymtab = 6;
constexpr uint64_t kDtStrsz = 10;
constexpr uint64_t kDtSyment = 11;
constexpr uint64_t kDtPltrel = 20;
constexpr uint64_t kDtJmprel = 23;
constexpr uint64_t kDtGnuHash = 0x6ffffef5u;
constexpr uint64_t kDtRela = 7;

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ 小工具

long long nowMs() {
    struct timespec ts;
    clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC, &ts);
    return static_cast<long long>(ts.tv_sec) * 1000LL + ts.tv_nsec / 1000000LL;
}

// Android 全是小端；这里直接按小端读，parse() 里校验 EI_DATA。
inline uint16_t rd16(const uint8_t *p) {
    uint16_t v = 0;
    memcpy(&v, p, sizeof(v));
    return v;
}

inline uint32_t rd32(const uint8_t *p) {
    uint32_t v = 0;
    memcpy(&v, p, sizeof(v));
    return v;
}

inline uint64_t rd64(const uint8_t *p) {
    uint64_t v = 0;
    memcpy(&v, p, sizeof(v));
    return v;
}

bool endsWith(const std::string &s, const char *suf) {
    const size_t n = strlen(suf);
    return s.size() >= n && s.compare(s.size() - n, n, suf) == 0;
}

std::string stripDeleted(const std::string &p) {
    const char *kSuf = " (deleted)";
    if (endsWith(p, kSuf)) return p.substr(0, p.size() - strlen(kSuf));
    return p;
}

std::string baseNameOf(const std::string &p) {
    const size_t slash = p.rfind('/');
    return (slash == std::string::npos) ? p : p.substr(slash + 1);
}

uint64_t pageSize() {
    static const uint64_t ps = []() -> uint64_t {
        long v = sysconf(_SC_PAGESIZE);
        return (v > 0) ? static_cast<uint64_t>(v) : static_cast<uint64_t>(4096);
    }();
    return ps;
}

uint64_t alignDown(uint64_t v) {
    const uint64_t ps = pageSize();
    return v & ~(ps - 1);
}

bool contains(const MapRegion &r, uint64_t addr, uint64_t len) {
    if (addr < r.start || addr > r.end) return false;
    return len <= r.end - addr;
}

const MapRegion *findReadable(const std::vector<const MapRegion *> &regs, uint64_t addr,
                              uint64_t len) {
    for (const MapRegion *r : regs) {
        if (r->readable && contains(*r, addr, len)) return r;
    }
    return nullptr;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ ELF 只读视图

struct PhdrView {
    uint32_t type = 0;
    uint32_t flags = 0;
    uint64_t off = 0;
    uint64_t vaddr = 0;
    uint64_t filesz = 0;
    uint64_t memsz = 0;
};

class MappedElf {
  public:
    MappedElf() = default;
    ~MappedElf() { close(); }
    MappedElf(const MappedElf &) = delete;
    MappedElf &operator=(const MappedElf &) = delete;

    // 只读 mmap 磁盘文件；不写、不 mprotect。
    bool open(const std::string &path) {
        close();
        int fd = ::open(path.c_str(), O_RDONLY | O_CLOEXEC);
        if (fd < 0) return false;
        struct stat st;
        if (fstat(fd, &st) != 0 || st.st_size <= 0 ||
            static_cast<uint64_t>(st.st_size) > kMaxElfBytes) {
            ::close(fd);
            return false;
        }
        const uint64_t sz = static_cast<uint64_t>(st.st_size);
        void *p = ::mmap(nullptr, static_cast<size_t>(sz), PROT_READ, MAP_PRIVATE, fd, 0);
        ::close(fd);  // mmap 之后 fd 可以立刻关掉，别占着
        if (p == MAP_FAILED) return false;
        base_ = static_cast<const uint8_t *>(p);
        size_ = sz;
        dev_ = (static_cast<uint64_t>(major(st.st_dev)) << 32) |
               static_cast<uint64_t>(minor(st.st_dev));
        ino_ = static_cast<uint64_t>(st.st_ino);
        return true;
    }

    void close() {
        if (base_ != nullptr) {
            ::munmap(const_cast<uint8_t *>(base_), static_cast<size_t>(size_));
            base_ = nullptr;
        }
        size_ = 0;
        cls_ = 0;
        dev_ = 0;
        ino_ = 0;
        loads_.clear();
        dyns_.clear();
    }

    // 解析 ELF 头 + program headers（PT_LOAD / PT_DYNAMIC）。
    bool parse() {
        if (base_ == nullptr || size_ < 64) return false;
        if (base_[0] != 0x7f || base_[1] != 'E' || base_[2] != 'L' || base_[3] != 'F') return false;
        cls_ = base_[4];
        if (cls_ != kElfClass32 && cls_ != kElfClass64) return false;
        if (base_[5] != 1) return false;  // 只支持 ELFDATA2LSB
        const bool is64 = (cls_ == kElfClass64);
        if (!is64 && size_ < 52) return false;

        etype_ = rd16(base_ + 16);
        machine_ = rd16(base_ + 18);
        const uint64_t phoff = is64 ? rd64(base_ + 32) : rd32(base_ + 28);
        const uint16_t phentsize = rd16(base_ + (is64 ? 54 : 42));
        const uint16_t phnum = rd16(base_ + (is64 ? 56 : 44));
        shoff_ = is64 ? rd64(base_ + 40) : rd32(base_ + 32);
        shentsize_ = rd16(base_ + (is64 ? 58 : 46));
        const uint16_t shnum = rd16(base_ + (is64 ? 60 : 48));
        shnum_ = (shnum == 0xffff) ? 0 : shnum;  // 扩展节数的库极少见，直接放弃

        const uint16_t minEnt = is64 ? 56 : 32;
        if (phnum == 0 || phnum > 512 || phentsize < minEnt) return false;
        if (phoff == 0 || phoff > size_) return false;
        if (static_cast<uint64_t>(phnum) * phentsize > size_ - phoff) return false;

        for (uint16_t i = 0; i < phnum; i++) {
            const uint8_t *p = base_ + phoff + static_cast<uint64_t>(i) * phentsize;
            PhdrView d;
            d.type = rd32(p);
            if (is64) {
                d.flags = rd32(p + 4);
                d.off = rd64(p + 8);
                d.vaddr = rd64(p + 16);
                d.filesz = rd64(p + 32);
                d.memsz = rd64(p + 40);
            } else {
                d.off = rd32(p + 4);
                d.vaddr = rd32(p + 8);
                d.filesz = rd32(p + 16);
                d.memsz = rd32(p + 20);
                d.flags = rd32(p + 24);
            }
            if (d.type == kPtLoad && d.off < size_) loads_.push_back(d);
            if (d.type == kPtDynamic) dyns_.push_back(d);
        }
        return !loads_.empty();
    }

    bool is64() const { return cls_ == kElfClass64; }
    uint16_t machine() const { return machine_; }
    uint16_t etype() const { return etype_; }
    const uint8_t *base() const { return base_; }
    uint64_t size() const { return size_; }
    uint64_t dev() const { return dev_; }
    uint64_t ino() const { return ino_; }
    const std::vector<PhdrView> &loads() const { return loads_; }
    const std::vector<PhdrView> &dyns() const { return dyns_; }

    // vaddr -> 文件内偏移（只覆盖 PT_LOAD 的 p_filesz 之内）。
    bool vaddrToOff(uint64_t vaddr, uint64_t &off) const {
        for (const PhdrView &s : loads_) {
            if (s.filesz == 0 || vaddr < s.vaddr) continue;
            const uint64_t delta = vaddr - s.vaddr;
            if (delta >= s.filesz) continue;
            const uint64_t o = s.off + delta;
            if (o >= size_) return false;
            off = o;
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    // 返回包含 vaddr 的可执行 PT_LOAD；没有则 nullptr。
    const PhdrView *codeSegmentOf(uint64_t vaddr) const {
        for (const PhdrView &s : loads_) {
            if (s.filesz == 0 || vaddr < s.vaddr) continue;
            if (vaddr - s.vaddr >= s.filesz) continue;
            if ((s.flags & kPfX) != 0) return &s;
        }
        return nullptr;
    }

    // 取第 idx 个节头；DT_HASH/DT_GNU_HASH 都缺失时用来兜底数 dynsym。
    bool section(uint32_t idx, uint32_t &typeOut, uint64_t &offOut, uint64_t &sizeOut,
                 uint64_t &entsizeOut) const {
        if (shoff_ == 0 || shnum_ == 0 || idx >= shnum_) return false;
        const uint16_t minEnt = is64() ? 64 : 40;
        if (shentsize_ < minEnt) return false;
        const uint64_t o = shoff_ + static_cast<uint64_t>(idx) * shentsize_;
        if (o > size_ || shentsize_ > size_ - o) return false;
        const uint8_t *p = base_ + o;
        typeOut = rd32(p + 4);
        if (is64()) {
            offOut = rd64(p + 24);
            sizeOut = rd64(p + 32);
            entsizeOut = rd64(p + 56);
        } else {
            offOut = rd32(p + 16);
            sizeOut = rd32(p + 20);
            entsizeOut = rd32(p + 36);
        }
        return offOut < size_;
    }

  private:
    const uint8_t *base_ = nullptr;
    uint64_t size_ = 0;
    uint8_t cls_ = 0;
    uint16_t etype_ = 0;
    uint16_t machine_ = 0;
    uint64_t shoff_ = 0;
    uint16_t shentsize_ = 0;
    uint16_t shnum_ = 0;
    uint64_t dev_ = 0;
    uint64_t ino_ = 0;
    std::vector<PhdrView> loads_;
    std::vector<PhdrView> dyns_;
};

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ .dynamic

struct DynInfo {
    uint64_t symtab = 0;
    uint64_t strtab = 0;
    uint64_t strsz = 0;
    uint64_t syment = 0;
    uint64_t hash = 0;
    uint64_t gnuhash = 0;
    uint64_t jmprel = 0;
    uint64_t pltrelsz = 0;
    uint64_t pltrel = 0;
};

bool readDynamic(const MappedElf &f, DynInfo &di) {
    const uint64_t ent = f.is64() ? 16 : 8;
    for (const PhdrView &seg : f.dyns()) {
        if (seg.filesz == 0 || seg.off >= f.size()) continue;
        const uint64_t avail = std::min<uint64_t>(seg.filesz, f.size() - seg.off);
        const uint64_t n = std::min<uint64_t>(avail / ent, 4096);
        for (uint64_t i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            const uint8_t *p = f.base() + seg.off + i * ent;
            const uint64_t tag = f.is64() ? rd64(p) : rd32(p);
            const uint64_t val = f.is64() ? rd64(p + 8) : rd32(p + 4);
            if (tag == kDtNull) break;
            switch (tag) {
                case kDtSymtab: di.symtab = val; break;
                case kDtStrtab: di.strtab = val; break;
                case kDtStrsz: di.strsz = val; break;
                case kDtSyment: di.syment = val; break;
                case kDtHash: di.hash = val; break;
                case kDtGnuHash: di.gnuhash = val; break;
                case kDtJmprel: di.jmprel = val; break;
                case kDtPltrelsz: di.pltrelsz = val; break;
                case kDtPltrel: di.pltrel = val; break;
                default: break;
            }
        }
    }
    return di.symtab != 0;
}

// DT_GNU_HASH：nbuckets/symoffset/bloom_size/bloom_shift + bloom + buckets + chains
uint64_t gnuHashSymCount(const MappedElf &f, uint64_t hashVaddr) {
    uint64_t off = 0;
    if (!f.vaddrToOff(hashVaddr, off)) return 0;
    if (off + 16 > f.size()) return 0;
    const uint8_t *h = f.base() + off;
    const uint32_t nbuckets = rd32(h);
    const uint32_t symoffset = rd32(h + 4);
    const uint32_t bloomSize = rd32(h + 8);
    if (nbuckets == 0 || nbuckets > (1u << 20) || bloomSize > (1u << 20)) return 0;

    const uint64_t wordBytes = f.is64() ? 8 : 4;
    const uint64_t bucketsOff = off + 16 + static_cast<uint64_t>(bloomSize) * wordBytes;
    const uint64_t bucketsBytes = static_cast<uint64_t>(nbuckets) * 4;
    if (bucketsOff > f.size() || bucketsBytes > f.size() - bucketsOff) return 0;

    const uint8_t *buckets = f.base() + bucketsOff;
    uint32_t last = 0;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < nbuckets; i++) {
        const uint32_t v = rd32(buckets + i * 4);
        if (v > last) last = v;
    }
    if (last < symoffset) return symoffset;
    const uint64_t chainBase = bucketsOff + bucketsBytes;
    uint64_t idx = last;
    for (uint64_t guard = 0; guard < kMaxDynsym; guard++, idx++) {
        if (idx < symoffset) return 0;
        const uint64_t pos = chainBase + (idx - symoffset) * 4;
        if (pos + 4 > f.size()) return 0;
        if ((rd32(f.base() + pos) & 1u) != 0) return idx + 1;
    }
    return 0;
}

// dynsym 条目数：DT_HASH → DT_GNU_HASH → 节头 SHT_DYNSYM → 距离估算。
uint64_t dynsymCount(const MappedElf &f, const DynInfo &di) {
    if (di.hash != 0) {
        uint64_t off = 0;
        if (f.vaddrToOff(di.hash, off) && off + 8 <= f.size()) {
            const uint32_t nchain = rd32(f.base() + off + 4);
            if (nchain > 0 && nchain <= kMaxDynsym) return nchain;
        }
    }
    if (di.gnuhash != 0) {
        const uint64_t n = gnuHashSymCount(f, di.gnuhash);
        if (n > 0 && n <= kMaxDynsym) return n;
    }
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < 64; i++) {
        uint32_t type = 0;
        uint64_t off = 0, size = 0, ent = 0;
        if (!f.section(i, type, off, size, ent)) continue;
        if (type == kShtDynsym && ent > 0) {
            const uint64_t n = size / ent;
            if (n > 0 && n <= kMaxDynsym) return n;
        }
    }
    uint64_t symOff = 0, strOff = 0;
    const uint64_t ent = di.syment ? di.syment : (f.is64() ? 24 : 16);
    if (ent >= 16 && f.vaddrToOff(di.symtab, symOff) && f.vaddrToOff(di.strtab, strOff) &&
        strOff > symOff) {
        const uint64_t n = (strOff - symOff) / ent;
        if (n > 0 && n <= kMaxDynsym) return n;
    }
    return 0;
}

// 读符号名（dynstr 里的 NUL 结尾串），失败返回空串。
std::string symNameAt(const MappedElf &f, uint64_t strtabOff, uint64_t strsz, uint32_t nameOff) {
    if (nameOff == 0) return std::string();
    const uint64_t pos = strtabOff + nameOff;
    if (pos >= f.size()) return std::string();
    uint64_t maxLen = 96;
    if (strsz > nameOff) maxLen = std::min<uint64_t>(maxLen, strsz - nameOff);
    if (maxLen > f.size() - pos) maxLen = f.size() - pos;
    if (maxLen == 0) return std::string();
    const char *s = reinterpret_cast<const char *>(f.base() + pos);
    size_t n = 0;
    while (n < maxLen && s[n] != '\0') n++;
    return std::string(s, n);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ 跳板形态

// 只看入口头 16 字节。命中「经典跳板」只是佐证：字节不一致本身才是依据。
bool looksLikeTrampoline(const uint8_t *m) {
#if defined(__aarch64__) || defined(__arm__)
    const uint32_t i0 = rd32(m);
    const uint32_t i1 = rd32(m + 4);
    const uint32_t i2 = rd32(m + 8);
    // LDR X16, #8 ; BR X16   /   LDR X17, #8 ; BR X17
    if ((i0 == 0x58000050u && i1 == 0xD61F0200u) || (i0 == 0x58000051u && i1 == 0xD61F0220u))
        return true;
    // ADRP X16, ... ; ADD X16, X16, #.. ; BR X16
    if ((i0 & 0x9F00001Fu) == 0x90000010u && (i1 & 0xFFC003FFu) == 0x91000210u && i2 == 0xD61F0200u)
        return true;
    // ADRP X16, ... ; LDR X16, [X16, #..] ; BR X16
    if ((i0 & 0x9F00001Fu) == 0x90000010u && (i1 & 0xFFC003FFu) == 0xF9400210u && i2 == 0xD61F0200u)
        return true;
    // 首条就是 B <imm26>（veneer / thunk，也是常见 hook 形态）
    if ((i0 & 0xFC000000u) == 0x14000000u) return true;
    // 首条就是 BR Xn
    if ((i0 & 0xFFFFFC1Fu) == 0xD61F0000u) return true;
    return false;
#elif defined(__x86_64__) || defined(__i386__)
    if (m[0] == 0xE9) return true;                                   // jmp rel32
    if (m[0] == 0xFF && (m[1] == 0x25 || m[1] == 0xE0 || m[1] == 0xE6)) return true;  // jmp *[..]
    if (m[0] == 0xCC) return true;                                   // int3
    if (m[0] == 0x68 && m[5] == 0xC3) return true;                    // push imm32 ; ret
    if (m[0] == 0x48 && m[1] == 0xB8 && m[10] == 0xFF && m[11] == 0xE0)
        return true;  // movabs rax,imm ; jmp rax
    return false;
#else
    (void)m;
    return false;
#endif
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ inline hook 扫描

struct LibGroup {
    std::string path;  // 已去掉 " (deleted)"
    std::vector<const MapRegion *> regs;
    bool hasExec = false;
    bool deleted = false;
    uint64_t dev = 0;  // 来自 maps，用来确认磁盘文件就是被映射的那一个 inode
    uint64_t ino = 0;
    int score = 0;
};

int libScore(const std::string &path) {
    const std::string base = baseNameOf(path);
    int s = 0;
    if (base == "liblitmusprobe.so") s += 100;  // 自己的库最先扫
    if (path.compare(0, 10, "/data/app/") == 0) s += 60;
    else if (path.compare(0, 6, "/data/") == 0) s += 45;
    else if (path.find("/data/") != std::string::npos) s += 35;
    else if (path.compare(0, 8, "/system/") == 0 || path.compare(0, 6, "/apex/") == 0) s += 10;
    static const char *const kHot[] = {"libc.so",        "libm.so",
                                       "libdl.so",       "libart.so",
                                       "liblog.so",      "libandroid_runtime.so",
                                       "libnativehelper.so", "libc++_shared.so"};
    for (const char *h : kHot) {
        if (base == h) {
            s += 50;
            break;
        }
    }
    return s;
}

void pushSample(std::vector<std::string> &v, const std::string &s) {
    if (v.size() < kMaxSamples) v.push_back(s.size() > 120 ? s.substr(0, 120) : s);
}

// 比对单个 .so 的函数入口。真扫了（took>0）返回 true。
bool scanLib(const LibGroup &g, InlineScanResult &res, long long deadline) {
    MappedElf f;
    if (!f.open(g.path) || !f.parse()) {
        res.skipped++;
        return false;
    }
    // 磁盘上这个路径必须还是被 mmap 的那一个文件（包更新/overlay 会让磁盘版本变掉）
    if (g.ino != 0 && f.ino() != 0 && (g.ino != f.ino() || (g.dev != 0 && g.dev != f.dev()))) {
        res.skipped++;
        return false;
    }
    DynInfo di;
    if (!readDynamic(f, di)) {
        res.skipped++;
        return false;
    }
    const uint64_t nsyms = dynsymCount(f, di);
    uint64_t symOff = 0;
    if (nsyms < 2 || !f.vaddrToOff(di.symtab, symOff)) {
        res.skipped++;
        return false;
    }
    uint64_t strOff = 0;
    f.vaddrToOff(di.strtab, strOff);
    const uint64_t syment = di.syment ? di.syment : (f.is64() ? 24 : 16);
    if (syment < 16) {
        res.skipped++;
        return false;
    }

    const std::string baseName = baseNameOf(g.path);
    const uint64_t limit = std::min<uint64_t>(nsyms, kMaxDynsym);
    int took = 0;
    for (uint64_t i = 1; i < limit && took < kMaxSymsPerLib; i++) {
        if ((i & 15) == 0 && nowMs() > deadline) {
            res.truncated = 1;
            break;
        }
        const uint64_t entryOff = symOff + i * syment;
        if (entryOff > f.size() || syment > f.size() - entryOff) break;
        const uint8_t *e = f.base() + entryOff;
        const uint32_t stName = rd32(e);
        const uint8_t stInfo = f.is64() ? e[4] : e[12];
        if ((stInfo & 0x0fu) != kSttFunc) continue;  // 只要 STT_FUNC
        const uint64_t stValue = f.is64() ? rd64(e + 8) : rd32(e + 4);
        if (stValue == 0) continue;  // 未定义符号

        const PhdrView *seg = f.codeSegmentOf(stValue);
        if (seg == nullptr) continue;
        const uint64_t segDelta = stValue - seg->vaddr;
        if (kCompareBytes > seg->filesz || segDelta > seg->filesz - kCompareBytes) continue;
        const uint64_t fileOff = seg->off + segDelta;
        if (fileOff >= f.size() || kCompareBytes > f.size() - fileOff) continue;

        // 运行时地址 = load_bias + st_value；load_bias 由该段在 maps 里的 (offset,start) 反推
        const uint64_t segVaddrAligned = alignDown(seg->vaddr);
        const uint64_t segOffAligned = alignDown(seg->off);
        const uint8_t *fileBytes = f.base() + fileOff;
        bool compared = false;
        bool equal = false;
        bool haveDiff = false;
        bool tramp = false;
        int diffAt = 0;
        for (const MapRegion *r : g.regs) {
            if (r->offset != segOffAligned) continue;
            if (r->start < segVaddrAligned) continue;
            const uint64_t bias = r->start - segVaddrAligned;
            const uint64_t runtime = bias + stValue;
            if (findReadable(g.regs, runtime, kCompareBytes) == nullptr) continue;
            const uint8_t *mem = reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t *>(runtime);
            compared = true;
            if (memcmp(mem, fileBytes, static_cast<size_t>(kCompareBytes)) == 0) {
                equal = true;
                break;  // 同一 .so 被加载两份时，任一份一致就算干净
            }
            if (haveDiff) continue;
            haveDiff = true;
            for (uint64_t k = 0; k < kCompareBytes; k++) {
                if (mem[k] != fileBytes[k]) {
                    diffAt = static_cast<int>(k);
                    break;
                }
            }
            tramp = looksLikeTrampoline(mem);
        }
        if (!compared) continue;
        took++;
        res.scanned++;
        if (equal) continue;

        res.hits++;
        if (tramp) res.tramp++;
        const std::string name = symNameAt(f, strOff, di.strsz, stName);
        char tail[32];
        snprintf(tail, sizeof(tail), " +0x%x", diffAt);
        pushSample(res.samples, baseName + "!" + (name.empty() ? "?" : name) + tail);
    }
    if (took > 0) res.libs++;
    return took > 0;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ GOT/PLT 落点

bool hasExecRegionCovering(const std::vector<MapRegion> &regions, uint64_t addr) {
    for (const MapRegion &r : regions) {
        if (!r.executable) continue;
        if (addr >= r.start && addr < r.end) return true;
    }
    return false;
}

// 取一个本 .so 内的代码地址，交给 dladdr 定位自身。
void selfAnchorNoop() {}

void *selfCodeAddress() {
    return reinterpret_cast<void *>(reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(&selfAnchorNoop));
}

}  // namespace

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ 对外入口

InlineScanResult scanInlineHooks(const std::vector<MapRegion> &regions, long long budgetMs) {
    const long long t0 = nowMs();
    const long long deadline = t0 + (budgetMs > 0 ? budgetMs : 1);
    InlineScanResult res;

    // 1) 按路径归并 maps 记录，只留 file-backed .so
    std::map<std::string, LibGroup> groups;
    for (const MapRegion &r : regions) {
        if (r.path.empty() || r.path[0] == '[') continue;
        const std::string clean = stripDeleted(r.path);
        if (!endsWith(clean, ".so")) continue;
        LibGroup &g = groups[clean];
        g.path = clean;
        g.regs.push_back(&r);
        if (r.executable) g.hasExec = true;
        if (r.deleted) g.deleted = true;
        if (g.ino == 0 && r.ino != 0) {
            g.ino = r.ino;
            g.dev = r.dev;
        }
    }

    // 2) 排序：自己的库 > 应用私有库 > 高频系统库 > 其它（同分按路径，保证可复现）
    std::vector<LibGroup *> order;
    order.reserve(groups.size());
    for (auto &kv : groups) {
        LibGroup &g = kv.second;
        if (!g.hasExec) continue;  // 没有可执行段就没有函数入口
        g.score = libScore(g.path);
        order.push_back(&g);
    }
    std::stable_sort(order.begin(), order.end(), [](const LibGroup *a, const LibGroup *b) {
        if (a->score != b->score) return a->score > b->score;
        return a->path < b->path;
    });

    // 3) 逐个扫，硬上限 40 个 .so
    const int total = static_cast<int>(order.size());
    const int libCap = std::min(total, kMaxLibs);
    for (int i = 0; i < libCap; i++) {
        if (nowMs() > deadline) {
            res.truncated = 1;
            break;
        }
        const LibGroup &g = *order[static_cast<size_t>(i)];
        if (g.deleted) {  // 磁盘文件未必还是被映射的那一份，比了没意义
            res.skipped++;
            continue;
        }
        scanLib(g, res, deadline);
    }
    if (total > libCap) res.truncated = 1;
    res.ms = nowMs() - t0;
    return res;
}

GotScanResult checkGotPlt(const std::vector<MapRegion> &regions, long long budgetMs) {
    const long long t0 = nowMs();
    const long long deadline = t0 + (budgetMs > 0 ? budgetMs : 1);
    GotScanResult res;

    // 1) 找到自己这个 so 的磁盘路径与 load bias
    std::string path;
    uint64_t bias = 0;
    bool haveBias = false;

    Dl_info info;
    memset(&info, 0, sizeof(info));
    if (dladdr(selfCodeAddress(), &info) != 0 && info.dli_fname != nullptr &&
        info.dli_fbase != nullptr) {
        path = info.dli_fname;
        bias = reinterpret_cast<uint64_t>(info.dli_fbase);
        haveBias = true;
    }
    if (path.empty()) {  // 兜底：按 basename 在 maps 里找
        for (const MapRegion &r : regions) {
            if (r.path.empty() || r.path[0] == '[') continue;
            if (baseNameOf(stripDeleted(r.path)) != "liblitmusprobe.so") continue;
            path = stripDeleted(r.path);
            break;
        }
    }
    MappedElf f;
    bool opened = !path.empty() && f.open(path) && f.parse();
    if (!opened) {  // dladdr 给的路径打不开：退回按 basename 在 maps 里找
        for (const MapRegion &r : regions) {
            if (r.path.empty() || r.path[0] == '[') continue;
            const std::string clean = stripDeleted(r.path);
            if (baseNameOf(clean) != "liblitmusprobe.so") continue;
            if (clean == path) continue;
            path = clean;
            haveBias = false;  // maps 路径的 bias 需要重新按段偏移推导
            opened = f.open(path) && f.parse();
            break;
        }
    }
    if (!opened) return res;
    DynInfo di;
    if (!readDynamic(f, di) || di.jmprel == 0 || di.pltrelsz == 0) return res;

    // 2) 本 so 在 maps 里的段，用于槽位可读性检查 / 兜底算 load bias
    const std::string selfBase = baseNameOf(path);
    std::vector<const MapRegion *> libRegs;
    for (const MapRegion &r : regions) {
        if (r.path.empty() || r.path[0] == '[') continue;
        const std::string clean = stripDeleted(r.path);
        if (baseNameOf(clean) != selfBase) continue;
        libRegs.push_back(&r);
    }
    if (!haveBias) {
        for (const PhdrView &seg : f.loads()) {
            const uint64_t segVaddrAligned = alignDown(seg.vaddr);
            const uint64_t segOffAligned = alignDown(seg.off);
            for (const MapRegion *r : libRegs) {
                if (r->offset != segOffAligned || r->start < segVaddrAligned) continue;
                bias = r->start - segVaddrAligned;
                haveBias = true;
                break;
            }
            if (haveBias) break;
        }
    }
    if (!haveBias) return res;

    // 3) 遍历 .rela.plt
    uint64_t jmpOff = 0;
    if (!f.vaddrToOff(di.jmprel, jmpOff)) return res;
    const bool isRela = (di.pltrel == kDtRela);
    const uint64_t entSize = isRela ? (f.is64() ? 24 : 12) : (f.is64() ? 16 : 8);
    const uint64_t avail = f.size() > jmpOff ? f.size() - jmpOff : 0;
    uint64_t count = std::min<uint64_t>(di.pltrelsz / entSize, avail / entSize);
    if (count > static_cast<uint64_t>(kMaxPltEntries)) count = kMaxPltEntries;
    res.plt = static_cast<int>(count);

    uint64_t strOff = 0;
    f.vaddrToOff(di.strtab, strOff);
    uint64_t symOff = 0;
    const bool haveSyms = f.vaddrToOff(di.symtab, symOff);
    const uint64_t symEnt = di.syment ? di.syment : (f.is64() ? 24 : 16);

    for (uint64_t i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        if ((i & 7) == 0 && nowMs() > deadline) break;
        const uint8_t *e = f.base() + jmpOff + i * entSize;
        const uint64_t rOffset = f.is64() ? rd64(e) : rd32(e);
        const uint64_t rInfo = f.is64() ? rd64(e + 8) : rd32(e + 4);
        const uint64_t slot = bias + rOffset;
        if (findReadable(libRegs, slot, 8) == nullptr) continue;
        uint64_t target = 0;
        memcpy(&target, reinterpret_cast<const void *>(slot), sizeof(target));
        res.scanned++;
        if (target == 0) {  // 弱符号未决议，不判定
            res.nulls++;
            continue;
        }
        if (hasExecRegionCovering(regions, target)) continue;
        res.bad++;
        std::string name;
        if (haveSyms && symEnt >= 16) {
            const uint64_t symIdx = f.is64() ? (rInfo >> 32) : (rInfo >> 8);
            const uint64_t so = symOff + symIdx * symEnt;
            if (so <= f.size() && symEnt <= f.size() - so) {
                name = symNameAt(f, strOff, di.strsz, rd32(f.base() + so));
            }
        }
        char buf[112];
        snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "%s!%s -> 0x%llx", selfBase.c_str(),
                 name.empty() ? "?" : name.c_str(),
                 static_cast<unsigned long long>(target & 0xffffffffffffull));
        pushSample(res.samples, buf);
    }
    res.ms = nowMs() - t0;
    return res;
}

}  // namespace litmus

```

`canary/jni/elf_scan.h`:

```h
// Litmus 检测内核 · 内存侧不变量扫描（与框架无关）
//
// 契约：docs/04-契约.md §4.5 的 inlineHook / gotCheck 两个子对象。
// 本模块只产出「事实」（扫了多少、命中多少、样本），不做判定。
//
// 设计要点：
//   1. 不找任何框架特征。只问「运行时字节 vs 磁盘字节」是否一致 —— 这是 tier A 的不变量。
//   2. 只读：open+mmap(PROT_READ) 磁盘文件，memcpy 读自己进程的内存；不 ptrace、不写别人。
//   3. 所有循环都有硬上限 + 单调时钟预算，超时立刻带着已有结果返回。
//   4. 不用异常（构建带 -fno-exceptions）；任何失败都退化为「少扫一个库」。

#ifndef LITMUS_ELF_SCAN_H
#define LITMUS_ELF_SCAN_H

#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace litmus {

// 一条 /proc/self/maps 记录（只留扫描需要的字段）。
struct MapRegion {
    uint64_t start = 0;
    uint64_t end = 0;
    uint64_t offset = 0;
    uint64_t dev = 0;  // makedev(major, minor)，用来和磁盘文件的 st_dev 对齐
    uint64_t ino = 0;  // 用来确认「磁盘上这个路径还是被映射的那一个 inode」
    std::string perms;  // 原样保留（"r-xp"），样本里要用
    bool readable = false;
    bool writable = false;
    bool executable = false;
    bool deleted = false;  // 路径带 " (deleted)" 后缀
    std::string path;      // 原样保留，可能带 " (deleted)"
};

// inline hook 扫描结果（对应 JSON 的 inlineHook）
struct InlineScanResult {
    int libs = 0;       // 真正比对了符号的 .so 个数
    int scanned = 0;    // 实际比对了多少个函数入口
    int hits = 0;       // 内存字节 != 磁盘字节的函数入口数
    int tramp = 0;      // 其中命中「经典跳板」形态的个数（佐证，不作为唯一依据）
    int skipped = 0;    // 打开/解析失败的 .so 个数
    int truncated = 0;  // 1 = 撞到时间/数量上限，结果不完整
    long long ms = 0;
    std::vector<std::string> samples;  // 最多 8 条，每条 <= 120 字符
};

// GOT/PLT 落点检查结果（对应 JSON 的 gotCheck）
struct GotScanResult {
    int plt = 0;      // .rela.plt 里的条目总数
    int scanned = 0;  // 实际检查过的槽位数
    int bad = 0;      // 落点不在任何可执行段的槽位数
    int nulls = 0;    // 值为 0 的槽位数（弱符号未决议，不判定）
    long long ms = 0;
    std::vector<std::string> samples;
};

// 扫描 regions 里所有 file-backed .so 的函数入口，比对内存字节与磁盘字节。
// regions 必须是 /proc/self/maps 的全量解析结果（顺序不重要）。
// budgetMs 是硬预算（建议 28ms）；超时设 truncated=1 并返回已扫结果。
InlineScanResult scanInlineHooks(const std::vector<MapRegion> &regions, long long budgetMs);

// 检查本 so（liblitmusprobe.so）的 .rela.plt：每个 GOT 槽的落点必须落在某个可执行映射里。
// budgetMs 建议 8ms。
GotScanResult checkGotPlt(const std::vector<MapRegion> &regions, long long budgetMs);

}  // namespace litmus

#endif  // LITMUS_ELF_SCAN_H

```

`canary/jni/litmus_probe.cpp`:

```cpp
// Litmus 检测内核 · native 探针
//
// 契约：docs/04-契约.md §4.5 —— snapshot() 返回单个 JSON 对象字符串。
// 分工：本文件只做**机械取证**，不做判定。判定在 io.litmus.core.probes 里。
//
// 已实现：maps 统计（anonExec/rwx/memfd/deleted 精化）/ TracerPid / 线程名 / vdso 大小 /
//         syscall 时延 / inline hook 字节比对 / GOT 落点检查
//
// 新增字段全部是「加子对象 / 加子键」，已有字段名一个都没动；见文件末尾的字段清单注释。

#include <jni.h>

#include <android/log.h>
#include <dirent.h>
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <sys/mman.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <time.h>
#include <unistd.h>

#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

#include "elf_scan.h"

#define LOG_TAG "litmus-probe"
#define LOGI(...) __android_log_print(ANDROID_LOG_INFO, LOG_TAG, __VA_ARGS__)
#define LOGE(...) __android_log_print(ANDROID_LOG_ERROR, LOG_TAG, __VA_ARGS__)

namespace {

constexpr const char *kVersion = "0.2.0";

// 单次 snapshot 的时间切分（总目标 < 40ms）
constexpr long long kInlineBudgetMs = 25;  // 契约要求 inlineHook 扫描 < 30ms
constexpr long long kGotBudgetMs = 7;

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ JSON 辅助

std::string jsonEscape(const std::string &in) {
    std::string out;
    out.reserve(in.size() + 8);
    for (char c : in) {
        switch (c) {
            case '"': out += "\\\""; break;
            case '\\': out += "\\\\"; break;
            case '\n': out += "\\n"; break;
            case '\r': out += "\\r"; break;
            case '\t': out += "\\t"; break;
            default:
                if (static_cast<unsigned char>(c) < 0x20) {
                    char buf[8];
                    snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "\\u%04x", static_cast<unsigned char>(c));
                    out += buf;
                } else {
                    out += c;
                }
        }
    }
    return out;
}

std::string quote(const std::string &s) { return "\"" + jsonEscape(s) + "\""; }

std::string num(long long v) {
    char buf[32];
    snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "%lld", v);
    return std::string(buf);
}

std::string strArray(const std::vector<std::string> &v, size_t limit = 8) {
    std::string out = "[";
    for (size_t i = 0; i < v.size() && i < limit; i++) {
        if (i) out += ",";
        out += quote(v[i]);
    }
    out += "]";
    return out;
}

// {count, samples}——契约里已有的形态，原样保留。
std::string countSamplesJson(int count, const std::vector<std::string> &samples) {
    return "{\"count\":" + num(count) + ",\"samples\":" + strArray(samples) + "}";
}

// {count, samples, so, dex, apk}——memfd / deleted 的精化计数（只加键，不改已有键）。
std::string countSamplesTypedJson(int count, const std::vector<std::string> &samples, int so,
                                  int dex, int apk) {
    return "{\"count\":" + num(count) + ",\"samples\":" + strArray(samples) +
           ",\"so\":" + num(so) + ",\"dex\":" + num(dex) + ",\"apk\":" + num(apk) + "}";
}

// 顶层对象拼装器：key() 自动处理逗号，从结构上消灭尾逗号。
class JObj {
  public:
    JObj() {
        s_.reserve(4096);
        s_ += '{';
    }

    JObj &key(const char *k) {
        if (s_.size() > 1) s_ += ',';
        s_ += '"';
        s_ += k;
        s_ += "\":";
        return *this;
    }

    JObj &raw(const std::string &v) {
        s_ += v;
        return *this;
    }

    std::string finish() {
        s_ += '}';
        return s_;
    }

  private:
    std::string s_;
};

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ 文件读取

bool readFile(const char *path, std::string &out, size_t cap = 4u * 1024 * 1024) {
    int fd = open(path, O_RDONLY | O_CLOEXEC);
    if (fd < 0) return false;
    out.clear();
    char buf[8192];
    ssize_t n;
    while ((n = read(fd, buf, sizeof(buf))) > 0) {
        out.append(buf, static_cast<size_t>(n));
        if (out.size() > cap) break;
    }
    close(fd);
    return true;
}

std::vector<std::string> splitLines(const std::string &s) {
    std::vector<std::string> lines;
    size_t start = 0;
    while (start < s.size()) {
        size_t pos = s.find('\n', start);
        if (pos == std::string::npos) {
            lines.push_back(s.substr(start));
            break;
        }
        lines.push_back(s.substr(start, pos - start));
        start = pos + 1;
    }
    return lines;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ /proc/self/status

int readTracerPid() {
    std::string status;
    if (!readFile("/proc/self/status", status, 256 * 1024)) return -1;
    for (const auto &line : splitLines(status)) {
        if (line.compare(0, 9, "TracerPid") == 0) {
            size_t colon = line.find(':');
            if (colon == std::string::npos) return -1;
            return atoi(line.c_str() + colon + 1);
        }
    }
    return -1;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ maps

bool parseMapsLine(const std::string &line, litmus::MapRegion &r) {
    unsigned long long start = 0, end = 0, off = 0, inode = 0;
    char perms[8] = {0};
    char dev[16] = {0};
    int pathOff = 0;
    int n = sscanf(line.c_str(), "%llx-%llx %7s %llx %15s %llu %n", &start, &end, perms, &off,
                   dev, &inode, &pathOff);
    if (n < 6) return false;
    r.start = start;
    r.end = end;
    r.offset = off;
    r.perms = perms;
    r.readable = r.perms.find('r') != std::string::npos;
    r.writable = r.perms.find('w') != std::string::npos;
    r.executable = r.perms.find('x') != std::string::npos;
    // dev 是 "major:minor"（十六进制），inode 是十进制；两个一起用来确认
    // 「磁盘上这个路径还是被 mmap 的那一个文件」，避免包更新/overlay 造成的假阳性。
    unsigned int dmaj = 0, dmin = 0;
    if (sscanf(dev, "%x:%x", &dmaj, &dmin) == 2) {
        r.dev = (static_cast<uint64_t>(dmaj) << 32) | static_cast<uint64_t>(dmin);
    }
    r.ino = inode;
    r.path.clear();
    if (pathOff > 0 && static_cast<size_t>(pathOff) < line.size()) {
        r.path = line.substr(static_cast<size_t>(pathOff));
        size_t b = r.path.find_first_not_of(' ');
        r.path = (b == std::string::npos) ? std::string() : r.path.substr(b);
    }
    r.deleted = r.path.size() > 10 && r.path.compare(r.path.size() - 10, 10, " (deleted)") == 0;
    return true;
}

std::string hexRange(uint64_t s, uint64_t e) {
    char buf[48];
    snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "%llx-%llx", static_cast<unsigned long long>(s),
             static_cast<unsigned long long>(e));
    return buf;
}

struct MapsStats {
    int anonExecCount = 0;
    long long anonExecBytes = 0;
    std::vector<std::string> anonExecSamples;
    int rwxCount = 0;
    std::vector<std::string> rwxSamples;
    int memfdCount = 0;
    std::vector<std::string> memfdSamples;
    int memfdSo = 0;
    int memfdDex = 0;
    int memfdApk = 0;
    int deletedCount = 0;
    std::vector<std::string> deletedSamples;
    int deletedSo = 0;
    int deletedDex = 0;
    int deletedApk = 0;
    long long vdsoSize = 0;
};

struct MapsReport {
    MapsStats st;
    std::vector<litmus::MapRegion> regions;  // 全量，供 ELF 扫描用
};

bool pathHas(const std::string &p, const char *token) { return p.find(token) != std::string::npos; }

// 只读一次 /proc/self/maps：既算统计，也留全量记录给 inline hook / GOT 扫描。
MapsReport scanMaps() {
    MapsReport rep;
    std::string content;
    if (!readFile("/proc/self/maps", content)) return rep;
    rep.regions.reserve(512);

    const auto &lines = splitLines(content);
    MapsStats &st = rep.st;
    for (const auto &line : lines) {
        litmus::MapRegion r;
        if (!parseMapsLine(line, r)) continue;
        rep.regions.push_back(r);

        const bool x = r.executable;
        const bool w = r.writable;

        if (x && w) {
            st.rwxCount++;
            if (st.rwxSamples.size() < 8) st.rwxSamples.push_back(hexRange(r.start, r.end) + " " + r.perms);
        }

        if (x && r.path.empty()) {
            st.anonExecCount++;
            st.anonExecBytes += static_cast<long long>(r.end - r.start);
            if (st.anonExecSamples.size() < 8)
                st.anonExecSamples.push_back(hexRange(r.start, r.end) + " " + r.perms);
        }

        if (x && pathHas(r.path, "memfd:")) {
            st.memfdCount++;
            if (pathHas(r.path, ".so")) st.memfdSo++;
            if (pathHas(r.path, "dex")) st.memfdDex++;
            if (pathHas(r.path, ".apk")) st.memfdApk++;
            if (st.memfdSamples.size() < 8) st.memfdSamples.push_back(r.path.substr(0, 100));
        }

        if (r.deleted) {
            st.deletedCount++;
            if (pathHas(r.path, ".so")) st.deletedSo++;
            if (pathHas(r.path, "dex")) st.deletedDex++;
            if (pathHas(r.path, ".apk")) st.deletedApk++;
            if (st.deletedSamples.size() < 8) st.deletedSamples.push_back(r.path.substr(0, 120));
        }

        if (r.path == "[vdso]") st.vdsoSize = static_cast<long long>(r.end - r.start);
    }
    return rep;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ 线程名

std::vector<std::string> readThreadNames(int *countOut) {
    std::vector<std::string> names;
    int count = 0;
    DIR *d = opendir("/proc/self/task");
    if (!d) {
        if (countOut) *countOut = -1;
        return names;
    }
    struct dirent *ent;
    while ((ent = readdir(d)) != nullptr) {
        if (ent->d_name[0] == '.') continue;
        count++;
        std::string commPath = std::string("/proc/self/task/") + ent->d_name + "/comm";
        std::string comm;
        if (readFile(commPath.c_str(), comm, 4096) && names.size() < 256) {
            while (!comm.empty() && (comm.back() == '\n' || comm.back() == '\r')) comm.pop_back();
            names.push_back(comm);
        }
    }
    closedir(d);
    if (countOut) *countOut = count;
    return names;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ syscall 时延
//
// 用 openat("/dev/null") 做基准：必走真实 syscall，不进 vDSO。
// hook 掉 libc open 会把这段时延抬高/抖动，是框架无关的行为侧不变量。

struct TimingStats {
    int n = 0;
    long long medianNs = 0;
    long long p99Ns = 0;
    long long jitterNs = 0;
};

long long nowNs() {
    struct timespec ts;
    clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC, &ts);
    return static_cast<long long>(ts.tv_sec) * 1000000000LL + ts.tv_nsec;
}

int cmpLongLong(const void *a, const void *b) {
    long long x = *static_cast<const long long *>(a);
    long long y = *static_cast<const long long *>(b);
    return x < y ? -1 : (x > y ? 1 : 0);
}

TimingStats measureSyscall(int iterations) {
    TimingStats st;
    if (iterations <= 0) return st;
    std::vector<long long> samples;
    samples.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(iterations));
    for (int i = 0; i < iterations; i++) {
        long long t0 = nowNs();
        int fd = open("/dev/null", O_RDONLY | O_CLOEXEC);
        if (fd >= 0) close(fd);
        long long t1 = nowNs();
        samples.push_back(t1 - t0);
    }
    qsort(samples.data(), samples.size(), sizeof(long long), cmpLongLong);
    st.n = static_cast<int>(samples.size());
    st.medianNs = samples[samples.size() / 2];
    st.p99Ns = samples[(samples.size() * 99) / 100];
    st.jitterNs = st.p99Ns - st.medianNs;
    return st;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ 汇总

// 契约 §4.5 的字段一个不少；新增内容全部是「子对象内的新键」，见 §4.5 扩展说明。
std::string buildSnapshotJson() {
    const long long t0 = nowNs() / 1000000LL;

    const int tracerPid = readTracerPid();
    const MapsReport maps = scanMaps();
    int threadCount = -1;
    const std::vector<std::string> threadNames = readThreadNames(&threadCount);
    const TimingStats tm = measureSyscall(200);  // 先测时延，避免被下面的 mmap 搅热

    const litmus::InlineScanResult ih = litmus::scanInlineHooks(maps.regions, kInlineBudgetMs);
    const litmus::GotScanResult gc = litmus::checkGotPlt(maps.regions, kGotBudgetMs);

    const MapsStats &st = maps.st;

    JObj j;
    j.key("ok").raw("true");
    j.key("abi").raw(quote(
#if defined(__aarch64__)
        "arm64-v8a"
#elif defined(__arm__)
        "armeabi-v7a"
#elif defined(__x86_64__)
        "x86_64"
#elif defined(__i386__)
        "x86"
#else
        "unknown"
#endif
        ));
    j.key("pid").raw(num(static_cast<long long>(getpid())));
    j.key("tracerPid").raw(num(tracerPid));
    j.key("vdsoSize").raw(num(st.vdsoSize));

    j.key("anonExec").raw("{\"count\":" + num(st.anonExecCount) + ",\"bytes\":" +
                          num(st.anonExecBytes) + ",\"samples\":" + strArray(st.anonExecSamples) +
                          "}");
    j.key("rwx").raw(countSamplesJson(st.rwxCount, st.rwxSamples));
    j.key("memfd").raw(countSamplesTypedJson(st.memfdCount, st.memfdSamples, st.memfdSo,
                                             st.memfdDex, st.memfdApk));
    j.key("deleted").raw(countSamplesTypedJson(st.deletedCount, st.deletedSamples, st.deletedSo,
                                               st.deletedDex, st.deletedApk));
    j.key("threads").raw("{\"count\":" + num(threadCount) + ",\"names\":" +
                         strArray(threadNames, 64) + "}");

    // inlineHook：libs=真正比对了符号的 .so 数，tramp=命中里带经典跳板形态的个数，
    // skipped=打不开/解析失败的 .so 数，truncated=1 表示撞上限（结果不完整），ms=耗时
    j.key("inlineHook").raw(
        "{\"scanned\":" + num(ih.scanned) + ",\"hits\":" + num(ih.hits) +
        ",\"samples\":" + strArray(ih.samples) + ",\"libs\":" + num(ih.libs) +
        ",\"tramp\":" + num(ih.tramp) + ",\"skipped\":" + num(ih.skipped) +
        ",\"truncated\":" + num(ih.truncated) + ",\"ms\":" + num(ih.ms) + "}");

    // gotCheck：plt=.rela.plt 条目数，nulls=值为 0 的槽（弱符号，不判定），ms=耗时
    j.key("gotCheck").raw(
        "{\"scanned\":" + num(gc.scanned) + ",\"bad\":" + num(gc.bad) +
        ",\"samples\":" + strArray(gc.samples) + ",\"plt\":" + num(gc.plt) +
        ",\"nulls\":" + num(gc.nulls) + ",\"ms\":" + num(gc.ms) + "}");

    j.key("timing").raw("{\"n\":" + num(tm.n) + ",\"medianNs\":" + num(tm.medianNs) +
                        ",\"p99Ns\":" + num(tm.p99Ns) + ",\"jitterNs\":" + num(tm.jitterNs) + "}");

    // 附加：本次 snapshot 的自身耗时（不参与判定，只给 CLI 复核性能用）
    j.key("elapsedMs").raw(num(nowNs() / 1000000LL - t0));

    return j.finish();
}

}  // namespace

extern "C" JNIEXPORT jstring JNICALL Java_io_litmus_core_NativeBridge_version(JNIEnv *env, jclass) {
    return env->NewStringUTF(kVersion);
}

extern "C" JNIEXPORT jstring JNICALL Java_io_litmus_core_NativeBridge_snapshot(JNIEnv *env,
                                                                             jclass) {
    std::string json;
    // 任何异常路径都不能把 Java 侧炸掉：拼装失败就回一个最小合法 JSON。
    json = buildSnapshotJson();
    if (json.empty() || json[0] != '{') {
        json = "{\"ok\":false,\"error\":\"snapshot build failed\"}";
    }
    return env->NewStringUTF(json.c_str());
}
