```java
            char c = src.charAt(i);
            if (c == '`') {
                if (open < 0) {
                    open = plain.length();
                } else {
                    if (plain.length() > open) {
                        spans.add(new int[]{open, plain.length()});
                    }
                    open = -1;
                }
                continue;
            }
            plain.append(c);
        }
        if (open >= 0 && plain.length() > open) {
            spans.add(new int[]{open, plain.length()});
        }
        SpannableStringBuilder sb = new SpannableStringBuilder(plain.toString());
        for (int i = 0; i < spans.size(); i++) {
            int[] r = spans.get(i);
            sb.setSpan(new TypefaceSpan("monospace"), r[0], r[1],
                    Spannable.SPAN_EXCLUSIVE_EXCLUSIVE);
        }
        return sb;
    }

    /** 去掉反引号但不要样式（复制到剪贴板、写日志用），保证「一行纯文本」。 */
    static String stripMd(String src) {
        if (src == null) {
            return "";
        }
        return src.indexOf('`') < 0 ? src : src.replace("`", "");
    }
}

```

`canary/src/io/litmus/canary/VectorDetailSheet.java`:

```java
package io.litmus.canary;

import android.app.Activity;
import android.app.Dialog;
import android.content.ClipData;
import android.content.ClipboardManager;
import android.content.Context;
import android.graphics.Color;
import android.graphics.Typeface;
import android.graphics.drawable.ColorDrawable;
import android.util.TypedValue;
import android.view.ContextThemeWrapper;
import android.view.Gravity;
import android.view.View;
import android.view.ViewGroup;
import android.view.Window;
import android.view.WindowManager;
import android.widget.LinearLayout;
import android.widget.ScrollView;
import android.widget.TextView;
import android.widget.Toast;

import org.json.JSONObject;

import io.litmus.core.VectorDocs;

/**
 * 单条向量的详情卡：从屏幕底部升起的 28dp 圆角卡（规范 §4）。
 *
 * <p>为什么要它：v0.1 只能看到「{@code rt.maps.file_integrity = TAMPERED}」，
 * 但看不出「这意味着什么、要不要管」。三段文案全部来自 {@link VectorDocs}
 * （由 schema/vectors.json 生成），**UI 里一个字都不硬编码** —— 这样 App 与 CLI 报告
 * 说的永远是同一套话。
 *
 * <p>实现用原生 {@link Dialog} + {@code Window.setGravity(BOTTOM)} + 透明窗口背景，
 * 不引任何 BottomSheet 库，只用系统 Dialog。
 */
final class VectorDetailSheet {

    private VectorDetailSheet() {
    }

    static void show(Activity activity, JSONObject signal) {
        if (activity == null || activity.isFinishing() || signal == null) {
            return;
        }
        final String id = signal.optString("id", "?");
        final String verdict = signal.optString("verdict", "UNKNOWN");
        final String category = signal.optString("category", "");
        final String tier = signal.optString("tier", "-");
        final int weight = signal.optInt("weight", 0);
        final String evidence = signal.optString("evidence", "");

        // 对话框自己带主题：Activity 是浅色 DeviceDefault，弹窗必须显式换成深色，
        // 否则 Dialog 的默认背景/文字是浅色，盖在深色卡上很脏。
        Context dark = new ContextThemeWrapper(activity, android.R.style.Theme_DeviceDefault);
        final Dialog dialog = new Dialog(dark);
        dialog.requestWindowFeature(Window.FEATURE_NO_TITLE);

        int pad = Ui.dp(activity, 20);

        LinearLayout card = new LinearLayout(activity);
        card.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
        card.setBackground(Ui.topRounded(Ui.SURFACE_CONTAINER_LOW, Ui.dp(activity, 28)));
        card.setPadding(pad, Ui.dp(activity, 18), pad, Ui.dp(activity, 12));

        // ---- 头：id + verdict 徽章 ----
        LinearLayout head = new LinearLayout(activity);
        head.setOrientation(LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL);
        head.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER_VERTICAL);

        TextView idView = new TextView(activity);
        idView.setText(id);
        idView.setTextColor(Ui.ON_SURFACE);
        idView.setTextSize(TypedValue.COMPLEX_UNIT_SP, 15);
        idView.setTypeface(Typeface.create("sans-serif-medium", Typeface.NORMAL));
        head.addView(idView, new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0,
                ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, 1f));

        TextView badge = new TextView(activity);
        badge.setText(Ui.verdictZh(verdict) + " · " + verdict);
        badge.setTextColor(Ui.verdictColor(verdict));
        badge.setTextSize(TypedValue.COMPLEX_UNIT_SP, 11);
        badge.setTypeface(Typeface.create("sans-serif-medium", Typeface.NORMAL));
        badge.setLetterSpacing(0.03f);
        int bPadH = Ui.dp(activity, 10);
        int bPadV = Ui.dp(activity, 5);
        badge.setPadding(bPadH, bPadV, bPadH, bPadV);
        badge.setBackground(Ui.rounded(0x1FFFFFFF, Ui.dp(activity, 999)));
        head.addView(badge, new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
                ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT));
        card.addView(head, wrap());

        TextView meta = new TextView(activity);
        meta.setText(Ui.categoryZh(category) + " · tier " + tier + " · 权重 " + weight);
        meta.setTextColor(Ui.OUTLINE);
        meta.setTextSize(TypedValue.COMPLEX_UNIT_SP, 12);
        meta.setLetterSpacing(0.03f);
        LinearLayout.LayoutParams metaLp = wrap();
        metaLp.topMargin = Ui.dp(activity, 6);
        card.addView(meta, metaLp);

        View divider = new View(activity);
        divider.setBackgroundColor(Ui.OUTLINE_VARIANT);
        LinearLayout.LayoutParams divLp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
                ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, Math.max(1, Ui.dp(activity, 1)));
        divLp.topMargin = Ui.dp(activity, 14);
        divLp.bottomMargin = Ui.dp(activity, 6);
        card.addView(divider, divLp);

        // ---- 四段正文：判据 / 证据 / 意味着 / 怎么绕过 ----
        LinearLayout body = new LinearLayout(activity);
        body.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);

        body.addView(block(activity, "判据", VectorDocs.zh(id), Ui.ON_SURFACE_VARIANT));
        body.addView(block(activity, "证据",
                evidence == null || evidence.trim().isEmpty() ? "（这一轮探针没有给出证据串）" : evidence,
                Ui.ON_SURFACE));
        body.addView(block(activity, "意味着", VectorDocs.plain(id), Ui.ON_SURFACE_VARIANT));
        body.addView(block(activity, "怎么绕过", VectorDocs.bypass(id), Ui.ON_SURFACE_VARIANT));

        // 长文案（bypass 可能几百字）要能滚，但不能让卡片顶到屏幕外
        MaxScroll scroll = new MaxScroll(activity, (int) (activity.getResources()
                .getDisplayMetrics().heightPixels * 0.62f));
        scroll.setVerticalScrollBarEnabled(false);
        scroll.addView(body);
        card.addView(scroll, wrap());

        // ---- 底部两个按钮 ----
        LinearLayout buttons = new LinearLayout(activity);
        buttons.setOrientation(LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL);
        LinearLayout.LayoutParams btnRowLp = wrap();
        btnRowLp.topMargin = Ui.dp(activity, 10);

        TextView copy = action(activity, "复制这一条", true);
        copy.setOnClickListener(v -> {
            // I9：剪贴板必须是 `id verdict evidence` 单行（反引号也剥掉，方便贴进报告）
            String line = id + " " + verdict + " " + Ui.stripMd(oneLine(evidence));
            try {
                ClipboardManager cm = (ClipboardManager) activity.getSystemService(Context.CLIPBOARD_SERVICE);
                if (cm != null) {
                    cm.setPrimaryClip(ClipData.newPlainText("litmus-signal", line));
                    Toast.makeText(activity, "已复制这一条：" + Ui.clip(line, 40),
                            Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                Toast.makeText(activity, "复制失败：" + t, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show();
            }
            dialog.dismiss();
        });
        LinearLayout.LayoutParams copyLp = new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0,
                ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, 1f);
        copyLp.rightMargin = Ui.dp(activity, 10);
        buttons.addView(copy, copyLp);

        TextView close = action(activity, "关闭", false);
        close.setOnClickListener(v -> dialog.dismiss());
        buttons.addView(close, new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0,
                ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, 1f));
        card.addView(buttons, btnRowLp);

        dialog.setContentView(card);
        Window w = dialog.getWindow();
        if (w != null) {
            w.setBackgroundDrawable(new ColorDrawable(Color.TRANSPARENT));
            w.setGravity(Gravity.BOTTOM);
            w.addFlags(WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_DIM_BEHIND);
            w.setDimAmount(0.62f);
            w.setLayout(WindowManager.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
                    WindowManager.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT);
        }
        dialog.show();
        if (w != null) {
            // Dialog 的 setLayout 要在 show() 之后才吃到 window，这里再设一次保证满宽贴底
            w.setLayout(WindowManager.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
                    WindowManager.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT);
        }

        // 「从底部升起」：先偏移 30% 屏高，再 220ms 拉回原位（纯 View 动画，无依赖）
        card.setTranslationY(activity.getResources().getDisplayMetrics().heightPixels * 0.30f);
        card.animate().translationY(0f).setDuration(220L).start();
    }

    /** evidence 只留一行（换行会让剪贴板里的一行契约破掉）。 */
    private static String oneLine(String s) {
        if (s == null) {
            return "";
        }
        return s.replace('\n', ' ').replace('\r', ' ').replace('\t', ' ').trim();
    }

    /** 「标签 + 值」一行：标签固定宽度对齐，长文本自动换行。 */
    private static View block(Activity a, String label, String value, int valueColor) {
        LinearLayout row = new LinearLayout(a);
        row.setOrientation(LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL);
        LinearLayout.LayoutParams rowLp = wrap();
        rowLp.topMargin = Ui.dp(a, 10);

        TextView l = new TextView(a);
        l.setText(label);
        l.setTextColor(Ui.OUTLINE);
        l.setTextSize(TypedValue.COMPLEX_UNIT_SP, 12);
        l.setLetterSpacing(0.03f);
        row.addView(l, new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
                Ui.dp(a, 62), ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT));

        TextView v = new TextView(a);
        // 三段文案与证据都来自 Markdown 语料（schema/vectors.json），这里统一走 Ui.md()
        // 把成对反引号渲染成等宽，避免用户看到字面 ` 字符
        v.setText(Ui.md(value == null || value.isEmpty() ? "—" : value));
        v.setTextColor(valueColor);
        v.setTextSize(TypedValue.COMPLEX_UNIT_SP, 13);
        v.setLineSpacing(Ui.dp(a, 2), 1f);
        v.setTypeface(Typeface.create("sans-serif", Typeface.NORMAL));
        row.addView(v, new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, 1f));
        row.setLayoutParams(rowLp);
        return row;
    }

    private static TextView action(Activity a, String text, boolean primary) {
        TextView b = new TextView(a);
        b.setText(text);
        b.setTextSize(TypedValue.COMPLEX_UNIT_SP, 14);
        b.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);
        b.setPadding(0, Ui.dp(a, 12), 0, Ui.dp(a, 12));
        if (primary) {
            b.setTextColor(Ui.ON_PRIMARY_CONTAINER);
            b.setTypeface(Typeface.create("sans-serif-medium", Typeface.NORMAL));
            b.setBackground(Ui.ripple(Ui.PRIMARY_CONTAINER, Ui.dp(a, 999)));
        } else {
            b.setTextColor(Ui.ON_SURFACE_VARIANT);
            b.setBackground(Ui.ripple(0x00000000, Ui.dp(a, 999)));
        }
        b.setClickable(true);
        b.setFocusable(true);
        return b;
    }

    private static LinearLayout.LayoutParams wrap() {
        return new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
                ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT);
    }

    /** 高度上限受控的 ScrollView：Dialog 是 WRAP_CONTENT，长文案不能把卡片顶出屏幕。 */
    private static final class MaxScroll extends ScrollView {
        private final int maxHeight;

        MaxScroll(Activity a, int maxHeight) {
            super(a);
            this.maxHeight = maxHeight;
        }

        @Override
        protected void onMeasure(int widthMeasureSpec, int heightMeasureSpec) {
            int size = MeasureSpec.getSize(heightMeasureSpec);
            int mode = MeasureSpec.getMode(heightMeasureSpec);
            if (mode == MeasureSpec.UNSPECIFIED || size > maxHeight) {
                heightMeasureSpec = MeasureSpec.makeMeasureSpec(maxHeight, MeasureSpec.AT_MOST);
            }
            super.onMeasure(widthMeasureSpec, heightMeasureSpec);
        }
    }
}

```

`canary/src/io/litmus/core/AppSnapshot.java`:

```java
package io.litmus.core;

import android.content.Context;
import android.content.pm.ApplicationInfo;
import android.content.pm.PackageInfo;
import android.content.pm.PackageManager;
import android.content.pm.Signature;
import android.content.pm.SigningInfo;
import android.os.Build;
import android.os.Process;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.security.MessageDigest;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.Enumeration;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.zip.ZipEntry;
import java.util.zip.ZipFile;

/**
 * 包/进程/签名的一次性快照。契约见 docs/04-契约.md §1.4。
 *
 * <p>一次采样、全局复用：所有 pkg.* 向量必须基于同一份快照判定，否则会出现
 * 「签名对得上但哈希对不上」这种自相矛盾的报告。
 */
@SuppressWarnings("deprecation")
public final class AppSnapshot {

    public final String packageName;
    public final int pid;
    public final int uid;
    public final String processName;
    public final String codePath;
    public final String dataDir;
    public final boolean debuggable;
    public final int targetSdk;
    public final String installer;

    /** GET_SIGNATURES 路径（旧 flags），每项为证书 DER 的 SHA-256 小写 hex。 */
    public final List<String> installCerts;
    /** GET_SIGNING_CERTIFICATES → apkContentsSigners。 */
    public final List<String> signingCerts;
    /** getSigningCertificateHistory()。 */
    public final List<String> certHistory;
    public final boolean multipleSigners;

    /** codePath 文件内容 SHA-256（CLI 平面用它对比原始包哈希）。 */
    public final String apkSha256;
    /** ZipEntry 指纹，算法见契约 §4.4（Java / Python 必须逐字节一致）。 */
    public final String zipFingerprint;
    /** classes*.dex 条目数与总大小，用于粗判 dex 是否被增删。 */
    public final int dexCount;
    public final long dexBytes;
    /** appComponentFactory / appClass 的原始值（被打包器改过就是痕迹）。 */
    public final String appComponentFactory;
    public final String appClass;

    private AppSnapshot(Context c) throws Exception {
        final PackageManager pm = c.getPackageManager();
        this.packageName = c.getPackageName();
        this.pid = Process.myPid();
        this.uid = Process.myUid();
        this.codePath = c.getApplicationInfo().sourceDir;
        this.dataDir = c.getApplicationInfo().dataDir;
        this.processName = currentProcessName();

        final ApplicationInfo ai = c.getApplicationInfo();
        this.debuggable = (ai.flags & ApplicationInfo.FLAG_DEBUGGABLE) != 0;
        this.targetSdk = ai.targetSdkVersion;
        this.appComponentFactory = ai.appComponentFactory == null ? "" : ai.appComponentFactory;
        this.appClass = ai.className == null ? "" : ai.className;

        PackageInfo pi = pm.getPackageInfo(packageName,
                PackageManager.GET_SIGNATURES | PackageManager.GET_SIGNING_CERTIFICATES);

        List<String> legacy = new ArrayList<>();
        if (pi.signatures != null) {
            for (Signature s : pi.signatures) {
                legacy.add(sha256Hex(s.toByteArray()));
            }
        }
        this.installCerts = Collections.unmodifiableList(legacy);

        List<String> v3 = new ArrayList<>();
        List<String> hist = new ArrayList<>();
        boolean multi = false;
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.P) {
            SigningInfo si = pi.signingInfo;
            if (si != null) {
                Signature[] signers = si.getApkContentsSigners();
                if (signers != null) {
                    for (Signature s : signers) {
                        v3.add(sha256Hex(s.toByteArray()));
                    }
                }
                Signature[] history = si.getSigningCertificateHistory();
                if (history != null) {
                    for (Signature s : history) {
                        hist.add(sha256Hex(s.toByteArray()));
                    }
                }
                multi = si.hasMultipleSigners();
            }
        } else {
            v3.addAll(legacy);
        }
        this.signingCerts = Collections.unmodifiableList(v3);
        this.certHistory = Collections.unmodifiableList(hist);
        this.multipleSigners = multi;

        this.installer = safeInstaller(pm, packageName);

        String sha = "";
        try {
            sha = sha256File(new File(codePath));
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        }
        this.apkSha256 = sha;

        String fp = "";
        int dc = -1;
        long db = 0L;
        try {
            File apk = new File(codePath);
            ZipFile zf = new ZipFile(apk);
            try {
                StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
                Enumeration<? extends ZipEntry> en = zf.entries();
                while (en.hasMoreElements()) {
                    ZipEntry e = en.nextElement();
                    sb.append(e.getName()).append('|')
                            .append(String.format("%08x", e.getCrc())).append('|')
                            .append(e.getSize()).append('|')
                            .append(e.getCompressedSize()).append('|')
                            .append(e.getMethod()).append('\n');
                    String nm = e.getName();
                    if (nm.startsWith("classes") && nm.endsWith(".dex")) {
                        dc = (dc < 0 ? 0 : dc) + 1;
                        db += e.getSize();
                    }
                }
                fp = sha256Hex(sb.toString().getBytes("UTF-8"));
            } finally {
                zf.close();
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        }
        this.zipFingerprint = fp;
        this.dexCount = dc;
        this.dexBytes = db;
    }

    public static AppSnapshot capture(Context c) throws Exception {
        return new AppSnapshot(c);
    }

    private static String currentProcessName() {
        try {
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.P) {
                return android.app.Application.getProcessName();
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        }
        return "";
    }

    private static String safeInstaller(PackageManager pm, String pkg) {
        try {
            String s = pm.getInstallerPackageName(pkg);
            return s == null ? "" : s;
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            return "";
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ 工具

    public static String sha256Hex(byte[] data) {
        try {
            MessageDigest md = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256");
            return hex(md.digest(data));
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            return "";
        }
    }

    public static String sha256File(File f) throws IOException {
        MessageDigest md;
        try {
            md = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256");
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new IOException(e);
        }
        InputStream in = new java.io.FileInputStream(f);
        try {
            byte[] buf = new byte[64 * 1024];
            int n;
            while ((n = in.read(buf)) > 0) {
                md.update(buf, 0, n);
            }
        } finally {
            in.close();
        }
        return hex(md.digest());
    }

    public static String hex(byte[] b) {
        if (b == null) {
            return "";
        }
        char[] digits = "0123456789abcdef".toCharArray();
        char[] out = new char[b.length * 2];
        for (int i = 0; i < b.length; i++) {
            out[i * 2] = digits[(b[i] >> 4) & 0xf];
            out[i * 2 + 1] = digits[b[i] & 0xf];
        }
        return new String(out);
    }
}
```

`canary/src/io/litmus/core/BuildInfo.java`:

```java
package io.litmus.core;

import android.content.Context;
import android.content.res.AssetManager;
import android.util.Log;

import java.io.InputStream;
import java.util.Properties;

/**
 * 构建期烘进 APK 的期望值。
 *
 * <p>为什么不是代码生成：签名证书 SHA-256 在**构建前**就已知（签名密钥固定），
 * 所以可以烘进去；而 APK 自身的 SHA-256 烘不进自己（签完才产生），那一项交给 CLI 平面判。
 * 见 docs/04-契约.md §4.1 与 docs/02-探针目录.md。
 *
 * <p>资产文件：{@code assets/litmus-build.properties}，由 {@code canary/build.ps1} 生成。
 * 用户重打包时若忘了改它，pkg.* 系列就会报 TAMPERED —— 这正是我们要的。
 */
public final class BuildInfo {

    public static final String ASSET = "litmus-build.properties";

    public static volatile boolean inited = false;
    public static String version = "0.0.0-unknown";
    public static String flavor = "unknown";
    public static String buildUtc = "";
    public static String kitId = "";
    public static boolean hasNative = false;

    /** 原始（未过签）证书 SHA-256：GET_SIGNING_CERTIFICATES 路径的期望值。 */
    public static String expectedCertSha256 = "";
    /** 旧 flags（GET_SIGNATURES）路径的期望值：轮换包里是 lineage 最老的那张证书。 */
    public static String expectedLegacyCertSha256 = "";
    /** getSigningCertificateHistory() 的期望集合，JSON 数组字面量。 */
    public static String expectedCertHistoryJson = "[]";
    public static int expectedSignerCount = 1;

    private BuildInfo() {
    }

    public static synchronized void init(Context c) {
        if (inited) {
            return;
        }
        inited = true;
        AssetManager am = c.getApplicationContext().getAssets();
        Properties p = new Properties();
        try {
            InputStream in = am.open(ASSET);
            try {
                p.load(in);
            } finally {
                in.close();
            }
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            Log.w("LitmusBuildInfo", "cannot read " + ASSET + ": " + t);
            return;
        }
        version = p.getProperty("litmus.version", version);
        flavor = p.getProperty("litmus.flavor", flavor);
        buildUtc = p.getProperty("litmus.buildUtc", buildUtc);
        kitId = p.getProperty("litmus.kitId", kitId);
        hasNative = Boolean.parseBoolean(p.getProperty("litmus.hasNative", "false"));
        expectedCertSha256 = p.getProperty("litmus.expectedCertSha256", "");
        expectedLegacyCertSha256 = p.getProperty("litmus.expectedLegacyCertSha256", "");
        expectedCertHistoryJson = p.getProperty("litmus.expectedCertHistory", "[]");
        try {
            expectedSignerCount = Integer.parseInt(p.getProperty("litmus.expectedSignerCount", "1"));
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        }
    }
}

```

`canary/src/io/litmus/core/Facts.java`:

```java
package io.litmus.core;

import org.json.JSONArray;
import org.json.JSONObject;

import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.TreeMap;

/**
 * 原始事实袋子。值是「观测值」，不是「判定」。
 *
 * <p>契约见 docs/04-契约.md §4.1 的 {@code facts} 字段。CLI 平面靠这些值做进程外复核
 * （典型：pkg.codepath.hash、pkg.zip.fingerprint —— 期望值烘不进 APK 自身，只能由 CLI 判）。
 */
public final class Facts {

    private final TreeMap<String, Object> map = new TreeMap<>();

    public Facts put(String key, Object value) {
        if (key != null && value != null) {
            map.put(key, value);
        }
        return this;
    }

    public boolean has(String key) {
        return map.containsKey(key);
    }

    public Object get(String key) {
        return map.get(key);
    }

    public Collection<String> keys() {
        return map.keySet();
    }

    public JSONObject toJson() {
        JSONObject o = new JSONObject();
        for (java.util.Map.Entry<String, Object> e : map.entrySet()) {
            try {
                Object v = e.getValue();
                if (v instanceof Collection) {
                    JSONArray a = new JSONArray();
                    for (Object item : (Collection<?>) v) {
                        a.put(item);
                    }
                    o.put(e.getKey(), a);
                } else {
                    o.put(e.getKey(), v);
                }
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                // 事实字段写失败不影响整体
            }
        }
        return o;
    }
}

```

`canary/src/io/litmus/core/LitmusContext.java`:

```java
package io.litmus.core;

import android.content.Context;

/**
 * 一次检测运行的上下文。
 *
 * <p>契约见 docs/04-契约.md §1.3。{@code appSnap} / {@code nat} 都是**一次采样、全体复用**，
 * 保证同一轮里所有向量看到的是同一时刻的世界（否则 rt.* 系列会互相矛盾）。
 */
public final class LitmusContext {

    public final Context app;
    public final AppSnapshot appSnap;
    public final NativeSnapshot nat;
    public final Facts facts;

    public LitmusContext(Context app, AppSnapshot appSnap, NativeSnapshot nat, Facts facts) {
        this.app = app;
        this.appSnap = appSnap;
        this.nat = nat;
        this.facts = facts == null ? new Facts() : facts;
    }

    public boolean hasNative() {
        return nat != null && nat.ok;
    }
}

```

`canary/src/io/litmus/core/LitmusRunner.java`:

```java
package io.litmus.core;

import android.content.Context;
import android.os.Build;
import android.os.SystemClock;
import android.util.Base64;
import android.util.Log;

import org.json.JSONArray;
import org.json.JSONObject;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileOutputStream;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat;
import java.util.Date;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.TimeZone;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * 一次检测运行：采样 → 跑全部向量 → 组装 report → 落盘 + logcat。
 *
 * <p>契约见 docs/04-契约.md §4.1 / §4.3。**不能**在主线程跑（native 采样约 5~30ms，
 * 向量合计 < 200ms，但 rt.timing 自带 200 次采样，放后台线程更稳）。
 */
public final class LitmusRunner {

    public static final String TAG = "LitmusReport";
    public static final String PREFIX = "LITMUS_REPORT ";
    public static final String CHUNK_PREFIX = "LITMUS_REPORT_CHUNK ";
    public static final String PATH_PREFIX = "LITMUS_REPORT_PATH ";
    /** logcat 单行安全上限（契约 §4.3：4000 字节）。 */
    private static final int LOGCAT_LIMIT = 3800;

    private LitmusRunner() {
    }

    public static File outputDir(Context c) {
        File base = c.getExternalFilesDir(null);
        if (base == null) {
            base = c.getFilesDir();
        }
        File dir = new File(base, "litmus");
        //noinspection ResultOfMethodCallIgnored
        dir.mkdirs();
        return dir;
    }

    public static JSONObject run(Context c) {
        final long t0 = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime();
        final String runId = UUID.randomUUID().toString().replace("-", "").substring(0, 16);

        BuildInfo.init(c);

        AppSnapshot snap = null;
        String snapError = "";
        try {
            snap = AppSnapshot.capture(c);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            snapError = t.getClass().getSimpleName() + ": " + t.getMessage();
            Log.w(TAG, "AppSnapshot failed: " + snapError);
        }

        NativeSnapshot nat = null;
        try {
            nat = NativeSnapshot.parse(NativeBridge.snapshot());
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            Log.w(TAG, "native snapshot unavailable: " + t);
        }

        Facts facts = new Facts();
        if (snap != null) {
            facts.put("apk.sha256", snap.apkSha256)
                    .put("apk.codePath", snap.codePath)
                    .put("apk.zip.fingerprint", snap.zipFingerprint)
                    .put("apk.dex.count", snap.dexCount)
                    .put("apk.dex.bytes", snap.dexBytes)
                    .put("cert.install", snap.installCerts)
                    .put("cert.signing", snap.signingCerts)
                    .put("cert.history", snap.certHistory)
                    .put("apk.multipleSigners", snap.multipleSigners)
                    .put("apk.installer", snap.installer)
                    .put("app.componentFactory", snap.appComponentFactory)
                    .put("app.class", snap.appClass)
                    .put("app.debuggable", snap.debuggable)
                    .put("app.targetSdk", snap.targetSdk);
        }
        if (nat != null) {
            facts.put("native.abi", nat.abi)
                    .put("native.pid", nat.pid)
                    .put("native.tracerPid", nat.tracerPid)
                    .put("native.threadCount", nat.threadCount)
                    .put("native.anonExecCount", nat.anonExecCount)
                    .put("native.rwxCount", nat.rwxCount)
                    .put("native.memfdCount", nat.memfdCount)
                    .put("native.deletedCount", nat.deletedCount)
                    .put("native.inlineHits", nat.inlineHits)
                    .put("native.gotBad", nat.gotBad);
        }

        LitmusContext ctx = new LitmusContext(c, snap, nat, facts);
        JSONArray signals = new JSONArray();

        List<Probe> probes = ProbeRegistry.all();
        for (Probe p : probes) {
            final long ps = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime();
            final JSONObject[] holder = new JSONObject[1];
            Probe.Sink sink = new Probe.Sink() {
                @Override
                public void emit(String verdict, double confidence, String evidence) {
                    if (holder[0] != null) {
                        return;  // 契约：一个 Probe 只 emit 一次
                    }
                    JSONObject s = new JSONObject();
                    try {
                        s.put("id", p.id());
                        s.put("category", p.category());
                        s.put("verdict", verdict);
                        s.put("confidence", Math.round(confidence * 1000d) / 1000d);
                        s.put("weight", p.weight());
                        s.put("tier", p.tier());
                        s.put("evidence", evidence == null ? "" : evidence);
                        s.put("ms", SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() - ps);
                    } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                    }
                    holder[0] = s;
                }
            };
            try {
                p.run(ctx, sink);
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                if (holder[0] == null) {
                    sink.error("probe threw " + t.getClass().getSimpleName() + ": " + t.getMessage());
                }
            }
            if (holder[0] == null) {
                sink.unknown("probe emitted nothing");
            }
            signals.put(holder[0]);
        }

        JSONObject summary = Score.summarize(signals);

        JSONObject report = new JSONObject();
        try {
            report.put("schema", "litmus.report/1");

            JSONObject producer = new JSONObject();
            producer.put("name", "io.litmus.canary");
            producer.put("version", BuildInfo.version);
            producer.put("flavor", BuildInfo.flavor);
            producer.put("buildUtc", BuildInfo.buildUtc);
            producer.put("kitId", BuildInfo.kitId);
            producer.put("hasNative", nat != null && nat.ok);
            report.put("producer", producer);

            JSONObject run = new JSONObject();
            run.put("id", runId);
            run.put("collectedAt", iso8601());
            run.put("durationMs", SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() - t0);
            run.put("passes", 1);
            run.put("probeCount", probes.size());
            run.put("snapshotError", snapError);
            report.put("run", run);

            report.put("app", appJson(c, snap));
            report.put("device", deviceJson());
            report.put("facts", facts.toJson());
            report.put("signals", signals);
            report.put("summary", summary);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            Log.e(TAG, "assemble report failed", t);
        }
        return report;
    }

    /** 落盘 + logcat。返回写出的文件（可能为 null）。 */
    public static File publish(Context c, JSONObject report) {
        String json = report.toString();
        File out = null;
        try {
            String runId = report.optJSONObject("run") == null ? "unknown"
                    : report.optJSONObject("run").optString("id", "unknown");
            out = new File(outputDir(c), "report-" + runId + ".json");
            FileOutputStream fos = new FileOutputStream(out);
            try {
                fos.write(json.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
            } finally {
                fos.close();
            }
            Log.i(TAG, PATH_PREFIX + out.getAbsolutePath());
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            Log.w(TAG, "write report failed: " + t);
        }

        byte[] utf8 = json.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        if (utf8.length <= LOGCAT_LIMIT) {
            Log.i(TAG, PREFIX + json);
        } else {
            String b64 = Base64.encodeToString(utf8, Base64.NO_WRAP);
            int chunk = 2800;
            int total = (b64.length() + chunk - 1) / chunk;
            for (int i = 0; i < total; i++) {
                int from = i * chunk;
                int to = Math.min(b64.length(), from + chunk);
                Log.i(TAG, CHUNK_PREFIX + (i + 1) + "/" + total + " " + b64.substring(from, to));
            }
        }
        return out;
    }

    private static JSONObject appJson(Context c, AppSnapshot snap) {
        JSONObject o = new JSONObject();
        try {
            o.put("package", c.getPackageName());
            o.put("pid", android.os.Process.myPid());
            o.put("uid", android.os.Process.myUid());
            o.put("process", snap == null ? "" : snap.processName);
            o.put("codePath", snap == null ? "" : snap.codePath);
            o.put("debuggable", snap != null && snap.debuggable);
            o.put("targetSdk", snap == null ? 0 : snap.targetSdk);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        }
        return o;
    }

    private static JSONObject deviceJson() {
        JSONObject o = new JSONObject();
        try {
            o.put("manufacturer", Build.MANUFACTURER);
            o.put("model", Build.MODEL);
            o.put("brand", Build.BRAND);
            o.put("fingerprint", Build.FINGERPRINT);
            o.put("sdk", Build.VERSION.SDK_INT);
            o.put("release", Build.VERSION.RELEASE);
            JSONArray abis = new JSONArray();
            if (Build.SUPPORTED_ABIS != null) {
                for (String a : Build.SUPPORTED_ABIS) {
                    abis.put(a);
                }
            }
            o.put("abis", abis);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        }
        return o;
    }

    private static String iso8601() {
        SimpleDateFormat f = new SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss'Z'", Locale.US);
        f.setTimeZone(TimeZone.getTimeZone("UTC"));
        return f.format(new Date());
    }
}

```

`canary/src/io/litmus/core/NativeBridge.java`:

```java
package io.litmus.core;

import android.util.Log;

/**
 * JNI 桥。契约见 docs/04-契约.md §1.4 / §4.5。
 *
 * <p>加载失败（无 native 库 / 不支持的 ABI）不是错误：上层 {@link LitmusRunner} 会把
 * {@code nat} 置 null，所有依赖 native 的 Probe 自行降级成 UNKNOWN。
 *
 * <p><b>为什么要 catch Throwable</b>：同一个 APK 可能只带部分 ABI 的 .so，也可能被用户
 * 删掉 lib/ 目录。静态块里漏抓就是整个进程崩在类初始化上——比「少 7 条向量」严重得多。
 */
public final class NativeBridge {

    private static final String TAG = "LitmusNative";
    private static final boolean LOADED;

    static {
        boolean ok = false;
        try {
            System.loadLibrary("litmusprobe");
            ok = true;
            Log.i(TAG, "liblitmusprobe loaded");
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            // UnsatisfiedLinkError / ExceptionInInitializerError 都要吞掉，走纯 Java 降级
            Log.w(TAG, "liblitmusprobe unavailable: " + t);
        }
        LOADED = ok;
    }

    public static boolean isLoaded() {
        return LOADED;
    }

    public static native String version();

    /** 一次性快照，JSON 字符串。见 NativeSnapshot。 */
    public static native String snapshot();

    private NativeBridge() {
    }
}

```

`canary/src/io/litmus/core/NativeSnapshot.java`:

```java
package io.litmus.core;

import org.json.JSONArray;
import org.json.JSONObject;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * native 侧一次性快照的 Java 视图。见 docs/04-契约.md §4.5。
 *
 * <p>解析必须<b>容忍缺字段</b>：不同 ABI / 不同 ROM 上子对象可能整体缺失。
 */
public final class NativeSnapshot {

    public final boolean ok;
    public final String error;
    public final String abi;
    public final int pid;
    public final int tracerPid;
    public final long vdsoSize;

    /** 匿名可执行映射。 */
    public final int anonExecCount;
    public final long anonExecBytes;
    public final List<String> anonExecSamples;

    /** RWX 段。 */
    public final int rwxCount;
    public final List<String> rwxSamples;

    /** memfd 可执行。 */
    public final int memfdCount;
    public final List<String> memfdSamples;

    /** maps 里带 (deleted) 的 file-backed 段。 */
    public final int deletedCount;
    public final List<String> deletedSamples;

    /** 线程数与线程名。 */
    public final int threadCount;
    public final List<String> threadNames;

    /** inline hook 扫描：扫描了多少个函数入口，命中多少个。 */
    public final int inlineScanned;
    public final int inlineHits;
    public final List<String> inlineSamples;

    /** GOT/PLT 落点检查。 */
    public final int gotScanned;
    public final int gotBad;
    public final List<String> gotSamples;

    /** 系统调用耗时统计（ns）。 */
    public final int timingN;
    public final long timingMedianNs;
    public final long timingP99Ns;
    public final long timingJitterNs;

    private NativeSnapshot(JSONObject o) {
        ok = o.optBoolean("ok", false);
        error = o.optString("error", "");
        abi = o.optString("abi", "");
        pid = o.optInt("pid", -1);
        tracerPid = o.optInt("tracerPid", -1);
        vdsoSize = o.optLong("vdsoSize", 0L);

        JSONObject anon = o.optJSONObject("anonExec");
        anonExecCount = anon == null ? -1 : anon.optInt("count", -1);
        anonExecBytes = anon == null ? 0L : anon.optLong("bytes", 0L);
        anonExecSamples = readList(anon, "samples");

        JSONObject rwx = o.optJSONObject("rwx");
        rwxCount = rwx == null ? -1 : rwx.optInt("count", -1);
        rwxSamples = readList(rwx, "samples");

        JSONObject memfd = o.optJSONObject("memfd");
        memfdCount = memfd == null ? -1 : memfd.optInt("count", -1);
        memfdSamples = readList(memfd, "samples");

        JSONObject deleted = o.optJSONObject("deleted");
        deletedCount = deleted == null ? -1 : deleted.optInt("count", -1);
        deletedSamples = readList(deleted, "samples");

        JSONObject threads = o.optJSONObject("threads");
        threadCount = threads == null ? -1 : threads.optInt("count", -1);
        threadNames = readList(threads, "names");

        JSONObject ih = o.optJSONObject("inlineHook");
        inlineScanned = ih == null ? -1 : ih.optInt("scanned", -1);
        inlineHits = ih == null ? -1 : ih.optInt("hits", -1);
        inlineSamples = readList(ih, "samples");

        JSONObject got = o.optJSONObject("gotCheck");
        gotScanned = got == null ? -1 : got.optInt("scanned", -1);
        gotBad = got == null ? -1 : got.optInt("bad", -1);
        gotSamples = readList(got, "samples");

        JSONObject t = o.optJSONObject("timing");
        timingN = t == null ? 0 : t.optInt("n", 0);
        timingMedianNs = t == null ? 0L : t.optLong("medianNs", 0L);
        timingP99Ns = t == null ? 0L : t.optLong("p99Ns", 0L);
        timingJitterNs = t == null ? 0L : t.optLong("jitterNs", 0L);
    }

    public static NativeSnapshot parse(String json) {
        if (json == null || json.trim().isEmpty()) {
            return null;
        }
        try {
            return new NativeSnapshot(new JSONObject(json));
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    private static List<String> readList(JSONObject o, String key) {
        List<String> out = new ArrayList<>();
        if (o == null) {
            return out;
        }
        JSONArray a = o.optJSONArray(key);
        if (a == null) {
            return out;
        }
        for (int i = 0; i < a.length() && out.size() < 32; i++) {
            String s = a.optString(i, "");
            if (!s.isEmpty()) {
                out.add(s);
            }
        }
        return out;
    }
}
```

`canary/src/io/litmus/core/Probe.java`:

```java
package io.litmus.core;

/**
 * 一条检测向量。
 *
 * <p>契约见 docs/04-契约.md §1.2。实现类放在 {@code io.litmus.core.probes} 下，按类别分文件。
 *
 * <p>硬约束：
 * <ul>
 *   <li>一个 Probe 只能 emit 一次；</li>
 *   <li>不得把异常抛给 Runner（Runner 会兜底转成 ERROR，但那样就丢失了 evidence）；</li>
 *   <li>不得阻塞超过 50ms（rt.timing 除外）。</li>
 * </ul>
 */
public interface Probe {

    /** 稳定 id，见 docs/04-契约.md §3。 */
    String id();

    /** 六类之一，必须与 id 前缀一致。 */
    String category();

    /** 1..5，越大越重要。默认 3。 */
    default int weight() {
        return 3;
    }

    /**
     * 证据强度：
     * <ul>
     *   <li>A = 不变量/密码学，可以下 TAMPERED；</li>
     *   <li>B = 交叉视图，可以下 TAMPERED 但应有 2 条以上证据；</li>
     *   <li>C = 特征匹配，<b>只允许 SUSPECT</b>。</li>
     * </ul>
     */
    default String tier() {
        return "B";
    }

    void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) throws Exception;

    /** 结论出口。 */
    interface Sink {
        void emit(String verdict, double confidence, String evidence);

        default void clean(String ev) {
            emit(Signal.CLEAN, 0.95, ev);
        }

        default void clean(String ev, double conf) {
            emit(Signal.CLEAN, conf, ev);
        }

        default void suspect(String ev, double conf) {
            emit(Signal.SUSPECT, conf, ev);
        }

        default void tampered(String ev, double conf) {
            emit(Signal.TAMPERED, conf, ev);
        }

        default void unknown(String ev) {
            emit(Signal.UNKNOWN, 0.0, ev);
        }

        default void error(String ev) {
            emit(Signal.ERROR, 0.0, ev);
        }
    }
}

```

`canary/src/io/litmus/core/ProbeRegistry.java`:

```java
package io.litmus.core;

import io.litmus.core.probes.EnvironmentProbes;
import io.litmus.core.probes.InjectionProbes;
import io.litmus.core.probes.PackageIntegrityProbes;
import io.litmus.core.probes.PrivilegeProbes;
import io.litmus.core.probes.RuntimeIntegrityProbes;
import io.litmus.core.probes.VirtualizationProbes;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * 向量注册表。契约见 docs/04-契约.md §1.4 / §1.5。
 *
 * <p>顺序 = 报告里的顺序；按类别排列，方便人读。新增类别才需要动这里。
 */
public final class ProbeRegistry {

    private ProbeRegistry() {
    }

    public static List<Probe> all() {
        List<Probe> out = new ArrayList<>(64);
        out.addAll(RuntimeIntegrityProbes.list());
        out.addAll(InjectionProbes.list());
        out.addAll(PackageIntegrityProbes.list());
        out.addAll(PrivilegeProbes.list());
        out.addAll(VirtualizationProbes.list());
        out.addAll(EnvironmentProbes.list());
        return out;
    }

    public static int count() {
        return all().size();
    }
}
```

`canary/src/io/litmus/core/Score.java`:

```java
package io.litmus.core;

import org.json.JSONArray;
import org.json.JSONObject;

import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 本地摘要评分。契约见 docs/04-契约.md §2 与 docs/03-评分模型.md。
 *
 * <p><b>CLI 是权威</b>：这里只给 App 界面一个即时数字，CLI 会用自己的实现重算一遍
 * （含基线归一与盲区判定）。两边公式必须一致，改公式请同时改 {@code cli/litmus_score.py}。
 */
public final class Score {

    /** 六类聚合权重，契约 §2.1。 */
    private static final Map<String, Double> CATEGORY_WEIGHT = new LinkedHashMap<>();

    static {
        CATEGORY_WEIGHT.put("runtime-integrity", 0.24);
        CATEGORY_WEIGHT.put("injection", 0.22);
        CATEGORY_WEIGHT.put("package-integrity", 0.20);
        CATEGORY_WEIGHT.put("privilege", 0.14);
        CATEGORY_WEIGHT.put("virtualization", 0.10);
        CATEGORY_WEIGHT.put("environment", 0.10);
    }

    private Score() {
    }

    /**
     * @param signals Array of signal objects（字段见契约 §4.1）
     * @return {@code {"total","clean","suspect","tampered","unknown","error","flaky","score","categories":[…]}}
     */
    public static JSONObject summarize(JSONArray signals) {
        int clean = 0, suspect = 0, tampered = 0, unknown = 0, error = 0, flaky = 0;

        Map<String, double[]> acc = new LinkedHashMap<>();  // category -> {pollutedWeight, countedWeight}
        for (String k : CATEGORY_WEIGHT.keySet()) {
            acc.put(k, new double[]{0d, 0d});
        }

        JSONObject catCounts = new JSONObject();
        for (int i = 0; i < signals.length(); i++) {
            JSONObject s = signals.optJSONObject(i);
            if (s == null) {
                continue;
            }
            String verdict = s.optString("verdict", Signal.UNKNOWN);
            String cat = s.optString("category", "");
            int w = Math.max(1, Math.min(5, s.optInt("weight", 3)));

            switch (verdict) {
                case Signal.CLEAN: clean++; break;
                case Signal.SUSPECT: suspect++; break;
                case Signal.TAMPERED: tampered++; break;
                case Signal.ERROR: error++; break;
                case Signal.FLAKY: flaky++; break;
                default: unknown++; break;
            }

            boolean countable = !Signal.UNKNOWN.equals(verdict)
                    && !Signal.ERROR.equals(verdict)
                    && !Signal.FLAKY.equals(verdict);
            if (!countable || !acc.containsKey(cat)) {
                continue;
            }
            double[] a = acc.get(cat);
            a[1] += w;
            if (Signal.TAMPERED.equals(verdict)) {
                a[0] += w;
            } else if (Signal.SUSPECT.equals(verdict)) {
                a[0] += w * 0.5;
            }
            try {
                JSONObject c = catCounts.optJSONObject(cat);
                if (c == null) {
                    c = new JSONObject();
                    c.put("tampered", 0);
                    c.put("suspect", 0);
                    c.put("clean", 0);
                    c.put("total", 0);
                    catCounts.put(cat, c);
                }
                c.put("total", c.optInt("total") + 1);
                if (Signal.TAMPERED.equals(verdict)) {
                    c.put("tampered", c.optInt("tampered") + 1);
                } else if (Signal.SUSPECT.equals(verdict)) {
                    c.put("suspect", c.optInt("suspect") + 1);
                } else if (Signal.CLEAN.equals(verdict)) {
                    c.put("clean", c.optInt("clean") + 1);
                }
                // 契约外的 verdict 不归类：宁可少计数，也不要把它算成 CLEAN 抬高分数。
                // 这类值只可能来自实现 bug，出现时应当显眼地「不干净也不脏」，而不是悄悄加分。
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {
            }
        }

        JSONArray categories = new JSONArray();
        double weightedSum = 0d;
        double weightTotal = 0d;
        for (Map.Entry<String, Double> e : CATEGORY_WEIGHT.entrySet()) {
            String cat = e.getKey();
            double a = e.getValue();
            double[] v = acc.get(cat);
            double counted = v[1];
            double catScore = counted <= 0 ? -1d : 100d * (1d - v[0] / counted);
            if (catScore >= 0) {
                weightedSum += a * catScore;
                weightTotal += a;
            }
            JSONObject c = catCounts.optJSONObject(cat);
            JSONObject o = new JSONObject();
            try {
                o.put("id", cat);
                o.put("weight", a);
                o.put("score", catScore < 0 ? JSONObject.NULL : round1(catScore));
                o.put("tampered", c == null ? 0 : c.optInt("tampered"));
                o.put("suspect", c == null ? 0 : c.optInt("suspect"));
                o.put("clean", c == null ? 0 : c.optInt("clean"));
                o.put("total", c == null ? 0 : c.optInt("total"));
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {
            }
            categories.put(o);
        }

        double score = weightTotal <= 0 ? -1d : weightedSum / weightTotal;

        JSONObject out = new JSONObject();
        try {
            out.put("total", signals.length());
            out.put("clean", clean);
            out.put("suspect", suspect);
            out.put("tampered", tampered);
            out.put("unknown", unknown);
            out.put("error", error);
            out.put("flaky", flaky);
            out.put("score", score < 0 ? JSONObject.NULL : round1(score));
            out.put("categories", categories);
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        }
        return out;
    }

    public static double round1(double v) {
        return Math.round(v * 10d) / 10d;
    }
}

```

`canary/src/io/litmus/core/Signal.java`:

```java
package io.litmus.core;

/**
 * 单条检测结论。不可变。
 *
 * <p>契约见 docs/04-契约.md §1.1。一个 {@link Probe} 只产出一条 Signal。
 */
public final class Signal {

    /** 未发现篡改。 */
    public static final String CLEAN = "CLEAN";
    /** 有痕迹，但证据不足以下定论。 */
    public static final String SUSPECT = "SUSPECT";
    /** 确认被改。 */
    public static final String TAMPERED = "TAMPERED";
    /** 本设备 / 本版本测不了（不扣分，单独统计）。 */
    public static final String UNKNOWN = "UNKNOWN";
    /** 探针自身异常（不扣分，单独统计）。 */
    public static final String ERROR = "ERROR";
    /** 多次采样结论不一致（不扣分，标记该检测不可靠）。 */
    public static final String FLAKY = "FLAKY";

    public final String id;
    public final String category;
    public final String verdict;
    public final double confidence;
    public final int weight;
    public final String tier;
    public final String evidence;
    public final long ms;

    public Signal(String id, String category, String verdict, double confidence,
                  int weight, String tier, String evidence, long ms) {
        this.id = id;
        this.category = category;
        this.verdict = verdict;
        this.confidence = clamp(confidence);
        this.weight = weight < 1 ? 1 : (weight > 5 ? 5 : weight);
        this.tier = tier == null ? "B" : tier;
        this.evidence = trim(evidence);
        this.ms = ms;
    }

    /** 污染度：参与加权的扣分值。 */
    public double pollution() {
        switch (verdict) {
            case TAMPERED:
                return 1.0;
            case SUSPECT:
                return 0.5;
            default:
                return 0.0;
        }
    }

    /** 是否计入加权分母。UNKNOWN / ERROR / FLAKY 一律不计。 */
    public boolean countable() {
        return !UNKNOWN.equals(verdict) && !ERROR.equals(verdict) && !FLAKY.equals(verdict);
    }

    /** 是否算「被抓到」。CLI 的盲区判定用这个。 */
    public boolean detected() {
        return TAMPERED.equals(verdict);
    }

    private static double clamp(double v) {
        if (Double.isNaN(v)) {
            return 0.0;
        }
        return v < 0.0 ? 0.0 : (v > 1.0 ? 1.0 : v);
    }

    private static String trim(String s) {
        if (s == null) {
            return "";
        }
        String one = s.replace('\n', ' ').replace('\r', ' ').replace('\t', ' ').trim();
        return one.length() <= 300 ? one : one.substring(0, 297) + "...";
    }

    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return id + "=" + verdict + "(" + confidence + ") " + evidence;
    }
}

```

`canary/src/io/litmus/core/VectorDocs.java`:

```java
package io.litmus.core;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 向量的人话文案（构建期由 tools/gen_vector_docs.py 从 schema/vectors.json 生成）。
 *
 * <p><b>不要手工编辑本文件</b>：改 schema/vectors.json 里对应条目的
 * {@code zh} / {@code plain} / {@code bypass}，然后重跑生成器。
 *
 * <p>App 详情卡与 CLI 报告读的是同一份文案，这是「说法一致」的工程保证。
 */
public final class VectorDocs {

    private static final Map<String, String[]> DOC = new HashMap<>();

    static {
        DOC.put("rt.tracerpid",

        DOC.put("virt.emulator.hardware",
                new String[]{"CPU 核数/传感器集合/GPU renderer 三者不得同时落在「模拟器典型值」区间", "核数、传感器和显卡凑成了模拟器的典型组合。真机上这三样几乎不会同时这么整齐。", "伪造传感器是系统级改动，比改属性难得多；只改一两样，剩下的会落在 virt.emulator.props 或 files 那条。"});
        DOC.put("virt.vapp",
                new String[]{"maps/路径中不得出现 VirtualApp 系宿主包特征（宿主 `codePath`、`/data/data/<host>/virtual/`）", "路径里出现了容器宿主的包。多开方案把客应用塞在宿主数据目录里跑，路径会替它说话。", "换宿主包名或换容器实现能绕开本条，但进程名与 uid 归属仍会错位——见 virt.vapp.binder。"});
        DOC.put("virt.multi.user",
                new String[]{"用户/工作资料数与 `userId` 偏移必须等于默认单用户形态", "设备上有多用户或工作资料。这本身是系统功能，只是它和双开共用同一套身份机制，容易误伤。", "不需要绕，关掉工作资料即可；它和 priv.mounts 一样看设备形态而非包，所以必须靠基线判读。"});
        DOC.put("virt.vapp.binder",
                new String[]{"`Process.myUid()` 必须等于 PMS 记录的自身 uid，且自调用 Binder 路径的 `getCallingUid()` 与之一致——容器代客调用必然让 uid 归属错位", "进程身份和包管理记录对不上。容器代客调用系统服务时 uid 归属必然错位，这是容器最难藏的一处。", "要让 uid 对齐就得真跑在自己进程里，也就是不用容器；单改一边读数会踩 priv.proc.uid 的同一处校验。"});
        DOC.put("env.debuggable",
                new String[]{"`ApplicationInfo.FLAG_DEBUGGABLE` 必须为 0——这是清单里的位，改了就是改了", "这个包被标成了可调试。重打包时常为看日志顺手打开它，但它写死在清单里，改了就是改了。", "关掉它要重签整个包，签名与指纹两条跟着一起红（撞 pkg.signature）；留着就只能等别的向量先绿。"});
        DOC.put("env.developer",
                new String[]{"开发者选项与 `adb_enabled` 必须为关（`Settings.Global` 三条键交叉）", "开发者选项或 USB 调试开着。这是使用姿势而非包的问题，通常只用来解释分数。", "关掉开关就没了，不需要技术手段；留着时调试器真接上去，会先在 rt.tracerpid 留下非 0。"});
        DOC.put("env.proxy",
                new String[]{"全局 HTTP 代理与 VPN `TRANSPORT_VPN` 不得同时成立", "设备上挂着代理或 VPN。抓包与公司网络都会命中，它解释的是流量为什么不走直连。", "关代理即可，不必改包；改成 hook 读取反而要在可执行内存里落代码，撞 rt.maps.anon_exec。"});
        DOC.put("env.capture",
                new String[]{"已启用的无障碍服务/投屏录屏不得包含非期望项", "有别的程序在看这一屏。无障碍、投屏、录屏都会命中，正常辅助工具同样会触发。", "关掉对应服务即可；这条与包内改动无关，换成 hook 读取只会平白撞上 rt.maps.anon_exec。"});
        DOC.put("env.overlay",
                new String[]{"`TYPE_APPLICATION_OVERLAY` 悬浮窗授权不得对本应用生效", "有悬浮窗权限挂着。游戏加速器、录屏按钮都会申请，单看它说明不了什么。", "关权限即可，不涉及包内改动；它只把环境有异物记一笔，判读价值低于 pkg.signature 这类硬证据。"});
    }

    private VectorDocs() {
    }

    private static String get(String id, int idx) {
        if (id == null) {
            return "";
        }
        String[] v = DOC.get(id);
        return (v == null || idx >= v.length || v[idx] == null) ? "" : v[idx];
    }

    /** 一句话判据。 */
    public static String zh(String id) {
        return get(id, 0);
    }

    /** 人话解释：这条红了意味着什么（没有时回落判据）。 */
    public static String plain(String id) {
        String s = get(id, 1);
        return s.isEmpty() ? zh(id) : s;
    }

    /** 怎么绕过 / 绕过会踩到哪条别的向量。 */
    public static String bypass(String id) {
        String s = get(id, 2);
        return s.isEmpty()
                ? "暂无已整理的绕过路径；按「不变量」思路，要让它变绿必须同时让相关等式重新成立。"
                : s;
    }

    public static int size() {
        return DOC.size();
    }
}

```

`canary/src/io/litmus/core/probes/EnvironmentProbes.java`:

```java
package io.litmus.core.probes;

import android.accessibilityservice.AccessibilityServiceInfo;
import android.content.ContentResolver;
import android.os.Build;
import android.provider.Settings;
import android.view.accessibility.AccessibilityManager;

import io.litmus.core.LitmusContext;
import io.litmus.core.BuildInfo;
import io.litmus.core.Probe;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * 环境面（应用自身的暴露属性，与设备无关）。
 *
 * <p>只有 {@code env.debuggable} 是 tier A（清单里的硬事实，debuggable 的 release 包一定被
 * 重打包过）；其余是 tier C 的弱信号，只出 CLEAN / SUSPECT / UNKNOWN。
 *
 * <p>干净基线：CI 里靶包是 adb 装到开着「USB 调试」的干净机上，所以 USB adb 与开发者选项
 * **不算痕迹**；只有「无线调试」这种额外暴露面才给弱 SUSPECT。
 */
public final class EnvironmentProbes {

    private EnvironmentProbes() {
    }

    public static List<Probe> list() {
        List<Probe> out = new ArrayList<>(8);
        out.add(new Debuggable());
        out.add(new DeveloperOptions());
        out.add(new ProxyProbe());
        out.add(new CaptureProbe());
        out.add(new OverlayProbe());
        return out;
    }

    /**
     * env.debuggable —— 应用是否带 FLAG_DEBUGGABLE。tier A：这是清单里的硬事实。
     *
     * <p>唯一豁免：构建期 flavor 就是 dev/debug 的包（BuildInfo 里写明），那种包本来就
     * debuggable，只给 SUSPECT，避免开发态自打脸。
     */
    static final class Debuggable implements Probe {
        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "env.debuggable";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "environment";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 3;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "A";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                if (ctx.appSnap == null) {
                    sink.unknown("snapshot unavailable");
                    return;
                }
                String flavor = Ev.nz(BuildInfo.flavor).toLowerCase();
                boolean devBuild = flavor.contains("dev") || flavor.contains("debug");
                if (!ctx.appSnap.debuggable) {
                    sink.clean("not debuggable (sdk=" + Build.VERSION.SDK_INT + ")");
                } else if (devBuild) {
                    sink.suspect("FLAG_DEBUGGABLE set on flavor=" + Ev.nz(BuildInfo.flavor), 0.4);
                } else {
                    sink.tampered("FLAG_DEBUGGABLE set on flavor=" + Ev.nz(BuildInfo.flavor)
                            + " (sdk=" + Build.VERSION.SDK_INT + ")", 0.95);
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("env.debuggable: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * env.developer —— 开发者选项 / adb 暴露面。tier C。
     *
     * <p>USB adb 是靶包安装与测试的**前提**，不能当痕迹（否则干净机永远拿不到满分）；
     * 只有无线调试（adb_wifi_enabled / adb tcp port）才给弱 SUSPECT。
     */
    static final class DeveloperOptions implements Probe {
        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "env.developer";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "environment";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 2;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "C";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                int adb = globalInt(ctx.app.getContentResolver(), "adb_enabled");
                int dev = globalInt(ctx.app.getContentResolver(), "development_settings_enabled");
                int wifiAdb = globalInt(ctx.app.getContentResolver(), "adb_wifi_enabled");
                ctx.facts.put("env.adb_enabled", adb)
                        .put("env.development_settings", dev)
                        .put("env.adb_wifi", wifiAdb);

                if (adb < 0 && dev < 0 && wifiAdb < 0) {
                    sink.unknown("Settings.Global unreadable");
                } else if (wifiAdb == 1) {
                    sink.suspect("wireless adb enabled (adb=" + adb + " dev=" + dev + ")", 0.5);
                } else {
                    sink.clean("adb=" + adb + " dev=" + dev + " wifiAdb=" + wifiAdb
                            + " (usb adb is test baseline)");
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("env.developer: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    /** env.proxy —— 全局 HTTP 代理 / java proxy 属性。tier C，权重 2。 */
    static final class ProxyProbe implements Probe {
        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "env.proxy";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "environment";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 2;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "C";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                String global = "";
                boolean readable = false;
                try {
                    ContentResolver cr = ctx.app.getContentResolver();
                    String v = Settings.Global.getString(cr, Settings.Global.HTTP_PROXY);
                    global = Ev.nz(v).trim();
                    readable = true;
                } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                    // 读不到就只看 java 属性
                }
                String host = Ev.nz(System.getProperty("http.proxyHost")).trim();
                String port = Ev.nz(System.getProperty("http.proxyPort")).trim();
                ctx.facts.put("env.http_proxy", global).put("env.proxy_host", host);

                boolean globalSet = !global.isEmpty() && !":0".equals(global) && !"0".equals(global);
                if (!host.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.suspect("java proxy=" + Ev.clip(host + ":" + port, 60), 0.5);
                } else if (globalSet) {
                    sink.suspect("global http_proxy=" + Ev.clip(global, 60), 0.5);
                } else if (!readable) {
                    sink.unknown("Settings.Global.HTTP_PROXY unreadable");
                } else {
                    sink.clean("no proxy (global=" + Ev.clip(global, 20) + ")");
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("env.proxy: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * env.capture —— 无障碍 / 录屏类辅助服务。tier C，权重 2。
     *
     * <p>无障碍服务能读屏并模拟点击，是自动化取证/外挂的主要通道。
     */
    static final class CaptureProbe implements Probe {
        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "env.capture";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "environment";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 2;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "C";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                AccessibilityManager am = (AccessibilityManager) ctx.app
                        .getSystemService(android.content.Context.ACCESSIBILITY_SERVICE);
                if (am == null) {
                    sink.unknown("AccessibilityManager unavailable");
                    return;
                }
                List<AccessibilityServiceInfo> list =
                        am.getEnabledAccessibilityServiceList(AccessibilityServiceInfo.FEEDBACK_ALL_MASK);
                int n = list == null ? 0 : list.size();
                String first = "";
                if (n > 0 && list.get(0) != null) {
                    first = Ev.nz(list.get(0).getId());
                }
                ctx.facts.put("env.a11y.enabled", n)
                        .put("env.a11y.first", Ev.clip(first, 90));

                if (n > 0) {
                    sink.suspect("a11y services=" + n + " e0=" + Ev.clip(first, 70), 0.4);
                } else {
                    sink.clean("no accessibility service enabled");
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("env.capture: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    /** env.overlay —— 悬浮窗权限。tier C，权重 2。 */
    static final class OverlayProbe implements Probe {
        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "env.overlay";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "environment";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 2;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "C";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                boolean can = Settings.canDrawOverlays(ctx.app);
                ctx.facts.put("env.overlay.can", can);
                if (can) {
                    sink.suspect("SYSTEM_ALERT_WINDOW granted", 0.45);
                } else {
                    sink.clean("no overlay permission");
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("env.overlay: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ 工具

    private static int globalInt(ContentResolver cr, String key) {
        try {
            return Settings.Global.getInt(cr, key, -1);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            return -1;
        }
    }
}

```

`canary/src/io/litmus/core/probes/InjectionProbes.java`:

```java
package io.litmus.core.probes;

import io.litmus.core.LitmusContext;
import io.litmus.core.Probe;

import java.io.File;
import java.net.InetSocketAddress;
import java.net.Socket;
import java.net.SocketException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Set;

/**
 * 注入痕迹（框架痕迹面）。tier C 为主，**只允许 SUSPECT**。
 *
 * <p>为什么 tier C 不能下 TAMPERED：这些是「特征匹配」，误报率天然高
 * （用户装了 LSPosed 但没启用模块、或某个正常应用恰好起了同名线程）。
 * 真正的判定要靠 {@link RuntimeIntegrityProbes} 的不变量。
 *
 * <p>本文件里只有 {@code inj.elf.inline} / {@code inj.proc.env} 是 tier B（交叉视图/硬痕迹），
 * 其余 C 级向量一律 CLEAN / SUSPECT / UNKNOWN，绝不 TAMPERED。
 */
public final class InjectionProbes {

    private InjectionProbes() {
    }

    public static List<Probe> list() {
        List<Probe> out = new ArrayList<>(8);
        out.add(new ElfInline());
        out.add(new ProcEnv());
        out.add(new ModulePaths());
        out.add(new ModuleMarker());
        out.add(new RwxSegment());
        out.add(new FridaPort());
        out.add(new FridaSocket());
        return out;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ B

    /**
     * inj.elf.inline —— ARM64 inline hook trampoline 字节扫描（native inlineHook）。
     *
     * <p>B 级交叉视图：函数入口的原始字节都在磁盘 ELF 里，内存里前几条指令被改成跳转就是
     * 硬痕迹。native 侧未实现（scanned=0）时必须 UNKNOWN，不能当干净。
     */
    static final class ElfInline implements Probe {
        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "inj.elf.inline";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "injection";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 4;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "B";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                if (ctx.nat == null || !ctx.nat.ok || ctx.nat.inlineScanned < 0) {
                    sink.unknown("native snapshot unavailable");
                    return;
                }
                int scanned = ctx.nat.inlineScanned;
                int hits = ctx.nat.inlineHits;
                String sample = ctx.nat.inlineSamples.isEmpty()
                        ? "" : " e0=" + Ev.clip(ctx.nat.inlineSamples.get(0), 90);
                if (scanned <= 0) {
                    sink.unknown("inlineHook scanned=0 (native side not implemented)");
                } else if (hits > 0) {
                    sink.tampered("inline-hook hits=" + hits + "/" + scanned + sample, 0.85);
                } else {
                    sink.clean("inline scan " + scanned + " entries, 0 hit", 0.8);
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("inj.elf.inline: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * inj.proc.env —— /proc/self/environ 里的 LD_PRELOAD / CLASSPATH 之类。
     *
     * <p>B 级：环境变量是进程启动时的定值，重打包的 wrapper 脚本只能靠它布防；一旦读到
     * LD_PRELOAD 指向外部可写目录，就是「先加载我的库再交给 ART」。
     */
    static final class ProcEnv implements Probe {
        private static final String[] KEYS = {
                "LD_PRELOAD", "LD_LIBRARY_PATH", "LD_AUDIT", "CLASSPATH", "BOOTCLASSPATH",
        };

        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "inj.proc.env";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "injection";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 3;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "B";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                String raw = ProcFs.readText("/proc/self/environ", 256 * 1024);
                if (raw == null || raw.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.unknown("/proc/self/environ unreadable or empty");
                    return;
                }
                List<String> entries = new ArrayList<>();
                int from = 0;
                while (from < raw.length() && entries.size() < 256) {
                    int e = raw.indexOf('\0', from);
                    if (e < 0) {
                        e = raw.length();
                    }
                    if (e > from) {
                        entries.add(raw.substring(from, e));
                    }
                    from = e + 1;
                }
                if (entries.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.unknown("/proc/self/environ parsed 0 entries");
                    return;
                }

                List<String> seen = new ArrayList<>();
                String preload = "";
                String otherHit = "";
                for (String kv : entries) {
                    int eq = kv.indexOf('=');
                    String k = eq < 0 ? kv : kv.substring(0, eq);
                    String v = eq < 0 ? "" : kv.substring(eq + 1).trim();
                    for (String want : KEYS) {
                        if (!want.equals(k)) {
                            continue;
                        }
                        if (v.isEmpty()) {
                            break;
                        }
                        seen.add(k);
                        if ("LD_PRELOAD".equals(k)) {
                            preload = v;
                        } else if (otherHit.isEmpty()) {
                            otherHit = k + "=" + Ev.clip(v, 90);
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                }
                ctx.facts.put("inj.proc.env.count", entries.size()).put("inj.proc.env.hit", seen);

                String dataDir = ctx.appSnap == null ? "" : Ev.nz(ctx.appSnap.dataDir);
                if (!preload.isEmpty()) {
                    if (Ev.isBadRoot(preload, dataDir)) {
                        sink.tampered("LD_PRELOAD=" + Ev.clip(preload, 120), 0.85);
                    } else {
                        sink.suspect("LD_PRELOAD=" + Ev.clip(preload, 120), 0.7);
                    }
                } else if (!otherHit.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.suspect(otherHit, 0.6);
                } else {
                    sink.clean("environ n=" + entries.size() + ", no LD_*/CLASSPATH set");
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("inj.proc.env: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ C（只许 SUSPECT）

    /**
     * inj.module.paths —— 已加载 .so 的路径白名单外命中。
     *
     * <p>tier C：加载器只从 /system、/apex、/vendor 和 app 的 codePath 里映射 so。出现别处
     * （/data/local/tmp、sdcard、别人家的 data 目录）就是有人自己拉了一份。
     */
    static final class ModulePaths implements Probe {
        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "inj.module.paths";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "injection";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 4;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "C";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                List<ProcFs.Region> maps = ProcFs.mapsOrNull();
                if (maps == null) {
                    sink.unknown("/proc/self/maps unreadable");
                    return;
                }
                Set<String> libs = new HashSet<>();
                List<String> outside = new ArrayList<>();
                for (ProcFs.Region r : maps) {
                    if (!r.fileBacked() || ProcFs.isPseudoPath(r.path) || !r.path.endsWith(".so")) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    if (!libs.add(r.path)) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    if (!Ev.isSystemLibPath(r.path) && outside.size() < 4) {
                        outside.add(r.path);
                    }
                }
                ctx.facts.put("inj.module.libs", libs.size()).put("inj.module.outside", outside);

                if (libs.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.unknown("no file-backed .so mapping found");
                } else if (!outside.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.suspect("libs=" + libs.size() + " outside=" + Ev.clipList(outside, 2), 0.6);
                } else {
                    sink.clean("libs=" + libs.size() + " all under whitelist root");
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("inj.module.paths: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    /** inj.module.marker —— 已加载库的**文件名**命中 hook 引擎特征。tier C。 */
    static final class ModuleMarker implements Probe {
        private static final String[] MARKS = {
                "frida", "gum-js", "libgadget", "gadget", "substrate", "xposed", "lsposed",
                "sandhook", "dobby", "libwhale", "whale", "riru", "zygisk", "magisk",
                "libepic", "libpine", "libyuki", "inlinehook", "libhookzz", "bhook",
        };

        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "inj.module.marker";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "injection";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 4;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "C";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                List<ProcFs.Region> maps = ProcFs.mapsOrNull();
                if (maps == null) {
                    sink.unknown("/proc/self/maps unreadable");
                    return;
                }
                Set<String> libs = new HashSet<>();
                List<String> hits = new ArrayList<>();
                for (ProcFs.Region r : maps) {
                    if (ProcFs.isPseudoPath(r.path) || !r.path.endsWith(".so")) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    if (!libs.add(r.path)) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    String name = Ev.base(r.path).toLowerCase();
                    for (String m : MARKS) {
                        if (name.contains(m)) {
                            if (hits.size() < 4) {
                                hits.add(Ev.base(r.path));
                            }
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                }
                ctx.facts.put("inj.module.marker", hits);
                if (libs.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.unknown("no mapped .so to match");
                } else if (!hits.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.suspect("libs=" + libs.size() + " marker=" + Ev.clipList(hits, 3), 0.6);
                } else {
                    sink.clean("libs=" + libs.size() + " no engine marker in names");
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("inj.module.marker: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * inj.elf.rwx —— 同时可写可执行的内存段。
     *
     * <p>tier C。必须排除 ART 的 JIT code cache：它在很多 ROM 上就是 rwx 且有名字
     * （{@code [anon:dalvik-jit-code-cache]} / {@code /dev/ashmem/dalvik-jit-code-cache}），
     * 不过滤的话干净机 100% 命中。
     */
    static final class RwxSegment implements Probe {
        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "inj.elf.rwx";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "injection";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 3;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "C";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                List<ProcFs.Region> maps = ProcFs.mapsOrNull();
                int suspicious;
                int art = 0;
                String sample = "";
                if (maps != null) {
                    suspicious = 0;
                    for (ProcFs.Region r : maps) {
                        if (!r.exec() || !r.writable()) {
                            continue;
                        }
                        String lower = r.path.toLowerCase();
                        if (lower.contains("dalvik") || lower.contains("jit")
                                || lower.contains("art-image") || lower.contains("app-image")) {
                            art++;
                            continue;
                        }
                        suspicious++;
                        if (sample.isEmpty()) {
                            sample = r.hexRange() + " " + r.perms
                                    + (r.path.isEmpty() ? "" : " " + Ev.clip(r.path, 40));
                        }
                    }
                } else if (ctx.nat != null && ctx.nat.rwxCount >= 0) {
                    suspicious = ctx.nat.rwxCount;
                    sample = ctx.nat.rwxSamples.isEmpty()
                            ? "" : Ev.clip(ctx.nat.rwxSamples.get(0), 60);
                } else {
                    sink.unknown("maps unreadable + native rwx unavailable");
                    return;
                }
                ctx.facts.put("inj.rwx.count", suspicious).put("inj.rwx.artExcluded", art);
                String ev = "rwx=" + suspicious + (art > 0 ? " (art-jit excluded=" + art + ")" : "")
                        + (sample.isEmpty() ? "" : " e0=" + sample);

                if (suspicious == 0) {
                    sink.clean(ev);
                } else if (suspicious <= 2) {
                    sink.suspect(ev, 0.45);
                } else {
                    sink.suspect(ev, 0.6);
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("inj.elf.rwx: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * inj.frida.port —— 本机 27042/27043 是否在监听。tier C。
     *
     * <p>只连 loopback，connect 超时 15ms；关闭端口的 connect 会立刻 ECONNREFUSED，实测
     * 单端口 < 1ms，两端口合计远低于 50ms 预算。
     */
    static final class FridaPort implements Probe {
        private static final int[] PORTS = {27042, 27043};

        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "inj.frida.port";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "injection";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 3;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "C";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                List<Integer> open = new ArrayList<>();
                boolean denied = false;
                for (int port : PORTS) {
                    Socket s = new Socket();
                    try {
                        s.connect(new InetSocketAddress("127.0.0.1", port), 15);
                        open.add(port);
                    } catch (SocketException se) {
                        String m = String.valueOf(se.getMessage()).toLowerCase();
                        if (m.contains("denied") || m.contains("eacces") || m.contains("eperm")) {
                            denied = true;
                        }
                    } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                        // 超时/拒绝都算端口未开
                    } finally {
                        ProcFs.close(s);
                    }
                }
                ctx.facts.put("inj.frida.openPorts", open);
                if (denied && open.isEmpty()) {
                    // 没有 INTERNET 权限时 connect 直接 EACCES，别把它当干净
                    sink.unknown("loopback connect denied (no INTERNET / seccomp)");
                } else if (!open.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.suspect("tcp loopback open on " + open.size() + " port(s) e0=" + open.get(0), 0.6);
                } else {
                    sink.clean("tcp 27042/27043 closed");
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("inj.frida.port: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * inj.frida.socket —— frida 的 unix socket / 落地文件痕迹。tier C。
     *
     * <p>三条路取证，任何一条命中即可：/data/local/tmp 下的 frida 落地文件、maps 里的
```
