```java
     * frida/gum 映射名、/proc/self/net/unix 里的 socket 名（后者多数 ROM 上读不到，
     * 读不到就只用前两条，不影响干净机给 CLEAN）。
     */
    static final class FridaSocket implements Probe {
        private static final String[] ARTIFACTS = {
                "/data/local/tmp/frida-server",
                "/data/local/tmp/re.frida.server",
                "/data/local/tmp/linjector",
                "/data/local/tmp/libgadget.so",
                "/data/local/tmp/frida-gadget.so",
                "/data/local/tmp/frida-agent.so",
        };

        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "inj.frida.socket";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "injection";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 3;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "C";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                List<String> hits = new ArrayList<>();

                for (String p : ARTIFACTS) {
                    try {
                        if (new File(p).exists() && hits.size() < 4) {
                            hits.add("file:" + Ev.base(p));
                        }
                    } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                        // stat 被拒就跳过
                    }
                }

                List<ProcFs.Region> maps = ProcFs.mapsOrNull();
                int mapHits = 0;
                if (maps != null) {
                    for (ProcFs.Region r : maps) {
                        String lower = r.path.toLowerCase();
                        if (lower.contains("frida") || lower.contains("libgadget") || lower.contains("gum-js")) {
                            mapHits++;
                            if (hits.size() < 4) {
                                hits.add("map:" + Ev.clip(Ev.base(r.path), 40));
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                boolean unixReadable = false;
                String unix = ProcFs.readText("/proc/self/net/unix", 512 * 1024);
                if (unix != null) {
                    unixReadable = true;
                    String lower = unix.toLowerCase();
                    if (lower.contains("frida") || lower.contains("gum-js") || lower.contains("gmain")) {
                        if (hits.size() < 4) {
                            hits.add("unix:socket");
                        }
                    }
                }

                ctx.facts.put("inj.frida.hits", hits).put("inj.frida.unixReadable", unixReadable);
                if (!hits.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.suspect("frida trace=" + Ev.clipList(hits, 3), 0.55);
                } else {
                    sink.clean("no frida file/map/unix-socket trace (unix="
                            + (unixReadable ? "readable" : "denied") + ")");
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("inj.frida.socket: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }
}

```

`canary/src/io/litmus/core/probes/PackageIntegrityProbes.java`:

```java
package io.litmus.core.probes;

import android.content.pm.InstallSourceInfo;
import android.content.pm.PackageManager;
import android.os.Build;

import io.litmus.core.LitmusContext;
import io.litmus.core.AppSnapshot;
import io.litmus.core.BuildInfo;
import io.litmus.core.Probe;

import org.json.JSONArray;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.zip.ZipEntry;
import java.util.zip.ZipFile;

/**
 * 包完整性。tier A 主力：签名链与内容哈希是密码学的，改了就一定不成立。
 *
 * <p>注意分工：{@code pkg.codepath.hash} 与 {@code pkg.zip.fingerprint} 的**期望值烘不进
 * APK 自身**（签完才有），所以这两个向量在 App 侧只产 UNKNOWN + facts，由 CLI 平面判。
 * 见 docs/04-契约.md §4.1。
 *
 * <p>所有期望值来自 {@link BuildInfo}（构建期烘进 assets）或 {@link AppSnapshot}，
 * 本文件**不含任何硬编码的哈希**。
 */
public final class PackageIntegrityProbes {

    private PackageIntegrityProbes() {
    }

    public static List<Probe> list() {
        List<Probe> out = new ArrayList<>(12);
        out.add(new SignatureLegacy());
        out.add(new SigningV3());
        out.add(new CertHistory());
        out.add(new CodePathHash());
        out.add(new ZipFingerprint());
        out.add(new ManifestProbe());
        out.add(new DexProbe());
        out.add(new Installer());
        out.add(new ProcName());
        return out;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ 签名（A）

    /** pkg.signature —— GET_SIGNATURES 全量比对。A 级。 */
    static final class SignatureLegacy implements Probe {
        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "pkg.signature";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "package-integrity";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 5;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "A";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                if (ctx.appSnap == null) {
                    sink.unknown("snapshot unavailable");
                    return;
                }
                String expect = Ev.nz(BuildInfo.expectedLegacyCertSha256);
                List<String> actual = safe(ctx.appSnap.installCerts);
                if (expect.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.unknown("no baked expectation (dev build), actual=" + hexList(actual));
                    return;
                }
                if (actual.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.unknown("no legacy signature returned");
                    return;
                }
                if (actual.size() == 1 && expect.equalsIgnoreCase(actual.get(0))) {
                    sink.clean("cert=" + shortHex(actual.get(0)));
                } else {
                    sink.tampered("expect=" + shortHex(expect) + " actual=" + hexList(actual), 1.0);
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("pkg.signature: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * pkg.signing.v3 —— apkContentsSigners 全量 + hasMultipleSigners。A 级。
     *
     * <p>重打包几乎必然换证书；换多张证书（多个 signer）也是痕迹。
     */
    static final class SigningV3 implements Probe {
        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "pkg.signing.v3";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "package-integrity";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 5;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "A";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                if (ctx.appSnap == null) {
                    sink.unknown("snapshot unavailable");
                    return;
                }
                String expect = Ev.nz(BuildInfo.expectedCertSha256);
                List<String> actual = safe(ctx.appSnap.signingCerts);
                if (expect.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.unknown("no baked expectation (dev build), signers=" + actual.size());
                    return;
                }
                if (actual.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.unknown("no apkContentsSigners returned");
                    return;
                }
                int expectCount = BuildInfo.expectedSignerCount <= 0 ? 1 : BuildInfo.expectedSignerCount;
                if (ctx.appSnap.multipleSigners && expectCount == 1) {
                    sink.tampered("multipleSigners=true signers=" + hexList(actual), 1.0);
                    return;
                }
                if (actual.size() != expectCount) {
                    sink.tampered("signerCount expect=" + expectCount + " actual=" + actual.size()
                            + " " + hexList(actual), 1.0);
                    return;
                }
                boolean hit = false;
                for (String s : actual) {
                    if (expect.equalsIgnoreCase(s)) {
                        hit = true;
                        break;
                    }
                }
                if (hit) {
                    sink.clean("v3 signer=" + shortHex(actual.get(0)) + " count=" + actual.size());
                } else {
                    sink.tampered("expect=" + shortHex(expect) + " actual=" + hexList(actual), 1.0);
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("pkg.signing.v3: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * pkg.cert.history —— getSigningCertificateHistory 全量。A 级。
     *
     * <p>密钥轮换时历史链是固定的；被换过签名的人拿不到原链。期望集合来自 BuildInfo 里的
     * JSON 字面量（构建期写入），不是硬编码。
     */
    static final class CertHistory implements Probe {
        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "pkg.cert.history";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "package-integrity";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 3;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "A";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                if (ctx.appSnap == null) {
                    sink.unknown("snapshot unavailable");
                    return;
                }
                if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < Build.VERSION_CODES.P) {
                    sink.unknown("SigningInfo requires API 28+");
                    return;
                }
                Set<String> expect = new HashSet<>();
                try {
                    JSONArray a = new JSONArray(Ev.nz(BuildInfo.expectedCertHistoryJson).trim());
                    for (int i = 0; i < a.length() && expect.size() < 8; i++) {
                        String s = a.optString(i, "");
                        if (!s.isEmpty()) {
                            expect.add(s.toLowerCase());
                        }
                    }
                } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                    // 解析失败按「没有期望值」处理
                }
                List<String> actual = safe(ctx.appSnap.certHistory);
                if (expect.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.unknown("no baked history expectation (dev build), history=" + actual.size());
                    return;
                }
                Set<String> got = new HashSet<>();
                for (String s : actual) {
                    got.add(Ev.nz(s).toLowerCase());
                }
                if (actual.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.unknown("certHistory empty (non-rotated or pruned)");
                } else if (got.equals(expect)) {
                    sink.clean("history=" + actual.size() + " matches baked set");
                } else {
                    // 措辞要点：先给「期望 vs 实际」的哈希，再给条数。否则一眼看去像「数量不符」，
                    // 实际这里最常见的是「条数相同、证书被换」。
                    sink.tampered("expect=" + hexList(new ArrayList<>(expect))
                            + " actual=" + hexList(actual)
                            + " (n=" + expect.size() + "/" + actual.size() + ")", 0.95);
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("pkg.cert.history: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ 内容哈希（CLI 判）

    /**
     * pkg.codepath.hash —— codePath 内容哈希。
     *
     * <p>APK 自己的 sha256 烘不进自己（签完才有），所以 App 侧只报 UNKNOWN + facts，
     * 期望值由 CLI 从 build-info.json 取（契约 §4.1 / §4.2）。
     */
    static final class CodePathHash implements Probe {
        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "pkg.codepath.hash";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "package-integrity";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 5;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "A";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                if (ctx.appSnap == null) {
                    sink.unknown("snapshot unavailable");
                    return;
                }
                String sha = Ev.nz(ctx.appSnap.apkSha256);
                ctx.facts.put("apk.sha256", sha).put("apk.codePath", Ev.nz(ctx.appSnap.codePath));
                if (sha.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.unknown("codePath unreadable: " + Ev.clip(ctx.appSnap.codePath, 120));
                } else {
                    sink.unknown("sha256=" + shortHex(sha) + " (CLI compares, codePath="
                            + Ev.base(ctx.appSnap.codePath) + ")");
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("pkg.codepath.hash: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    /** pkg.zip.fingerprint —— ZipEntry 指纹，算法见契约 §4.4。同样只产 UNKNOWN + facts。 */
    static final class ZipFingerprint implements Probe {
        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "pkg.zip.fingerprint";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "package-integrity";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 5;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "A";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                if (ctx.appSnap == null) {
                    sink.unknown("snapshot unavailable");
                    return;
                }
                String fp = Ev.nz(ctx.appSnap.zipFingerprint);
                ctx.facts.put("apk.zip.fingerprint", fp);
                if (fp.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.unknown("zip unreadable: " + Ev.base(ctx.appSnap.codePath));
                } else {
                    sink.unknown("zipFp=" + shortHex(fp) + " (CLI compares)");
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("pkg.zip.fingerprint: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ 清单 / dex

    /**
     * pkg.manifest —— appComponentFactory / appClass 是否被改。
     *
     * <p>B 级不变量：PackageManager 报的 Application 类名是**清单里的值**，而运行时真正跑
     * 的是当前对象。两者不符 = 有人在装载阶段把 Application 换掉了（这是最常见的注入入口）。
     * 允许子类（有的安全 SDK 会派生一层）。
     */
    static final class ManifestProbe implements Probe {
        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "pkg.manifest";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "package-integrity";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 4;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "B";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                if (ctx.appSnap == null) {
                    sink.unknown("snapshot unavailable");
                    return;
                }
                String declared = Ev.nz(ctx.appSnap.appClass);
                String factory = Ev.nz(ctx.appSnap.appComponentFactory);
                String runtime = "";
                try {
                    runtime = ctx.app.getApplicationContext().getClass().getName();
                } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                    // 拿不到就不判类名
                }
                ctx.facts.put("app.class", declared).put("app.runtimeClass", runtime)
                        .put("app.componentFactory", factory);

                if (!declared.isEmpty() && !runtime.isEmpty() && !declared.equals(runtime)) {
                    if (isSubclass(declared, runtime)) {
                        sink.clean("appClass=" + Ev.base(declared) + " runtime subclass ok", 0.85);
                    } else {
                        sink.tampered("manifest appClass=" + declared + " runtime=" + runtime, 0.9);
                    }
                    return;
                }
                // ApplicationInfo.appComponentFactory 是 API 28 才有的字段
                if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.P && factory.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.suspect("appComponentFactory empty in ApplicationInfo "
                            + "(manifest attr dropped?)", 0.45);
                    return;
                }
                if (declared.isEmpty() && runtime.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.unknown("neither manifest nor runtime appClass available");
                } else {
                    sink.clean("appClass=" + Ev.base(declared) + " factory="
                            + (factory.isEmpty() ? "none" : Ev.base(factory)));
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("pkg.manifest: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }

        private static boolean isSubclass(String declared, String runtime) {
            try {
                Class<?> a = Class.forName(declared);
                Class<?> b = Class.forName(runtime);
                return a.isAssignableFrom(b);
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                return false;
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * pkg.dex —— classes*.dex 数量与完整性。
     *
     * <p>B 级：dex 数量来自快照里的 zip 目录；这里再验一遍每个 dex 的魔数（{@code dex\n}）。
     * 加固/加壳器常把 dex 换成自有格式或塞空壳，魔数会对不上。
     */
    static final class DexProbe implements Probe {
        private static final int MAX_CHECK = 6;

        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "pkg.dex";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "package-integrity";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 4;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "B";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                AppSnapshot snap = ctx.appSnap;
                if (snap == null) {
                    sink.unknown("snapshot unavailable");
                    return;
                }
                int count = snap.dexCount;
                ctx.facts.put("apk.dex.count", count).put("apk.dex.bytes", snap.dexBytes);
                if (count < 0) {
                    sink.unknown("zip unreadable: " + Ev.base(snap.codePath));
                    return;
                }
                if (count == 0) {
                    sink.suspect("no classes*.dex in " + Ev.base(snap.codePath), 0.6);
                    return;
                }

                List<String> entries = new ArrayList<>();
                int badMagic = 0;
                ZipFile zf = null;
                try {
                    zf = new ZipFile(new File(snap.codePath));
                    java.util.Enumeration<? extends ZipEntry> en = zf.entries();
                    while (en.hasMoreElements()) {
                        ZipEntry e = en.nextElement();
                        String nm = e.getName();
                        if (!nm.startsWith("classes") || !nm.endsWith(".dex")) {
                            continue;
                        }
                        if (entries.size() < 8) {
                            entries.add(nm + "|" + e.getSize() + "|" + String.format("%08x", e.getCrc()));
                        }
                        if (entries.size() <= MAX_CHECK && !magicOk(zf, e)) {
                            badMagic++;
                        }
                    }
                } finally {
                    ProcFs.close(zf);
                }
                ctx.facts.put("apk.dex.entries", entries);

                if (badMagic > 0) {
                    sink.tampered("dex magic broken on " + badMagic + " entries "
                            + Ev.clipList(entries, 2), 0.9);
                } else {
                    sink.clean("dex=" + count + " bytes=" + snap.dexBytes + " magic ok");
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("pkg.dex: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }

        private static boolean magicOk(ZipFile zf, ZipEntry e) {
            InputStream in = null;
            try {
                in = zf.getInputStream(e);
                byte[] b = new byte[8];
                int off = 0;
                while (off < 8) {
                    int n = in.read(b, off, 8 - off);
                    if (n <= 0) {
                        return false;
                    }
                    off += n;
                }
                // 0x64 0x65 0x78 0x0a = "dex\n"
                return b[0] == 0x64 && b[1] == 0x65 && b[2] == 0x78 && b[3] == 0x0a;
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                return true;   // 读失败不算「魔数坏」
            } finally {
                ProcFs.close(in);
            }
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ 安装来源 / 进程名

    /**
     * pkg.installer —— installerPackageName。tier C。
     *
     * <p>adb / 侧载安装的 installer 是 null，这在本项目里是**正常基线**（靶包就是 adb 装的），
     * 所以 null 不能算痕迹；只有「来源是个不认识的三方包名」才给弱 SUSPECT。
     */
    static final class Installer implements Probe {
        private static final String[] KNOWN = {
                "com.android.vending", "com.google.android.packageinstaller",
                "com.android.packageinstaller", "com.android.shell",
                "com.google.android.gsf", "com.miui.packageinstaller",
                "com.samsung.android.packageinstaller", "com.huawei.appmarket",
                "com.oppo.market", "com.heytap.market", "com.bbk.appstore",
        };

        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "pkg.installer";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "package-integrity";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 2;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "C";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                if (ctx.appSnap == null) {
                    sink.unknown("snapshot unavailable");
                    return;
                }
                PackageManager pm = ctx.app.getPackageManager();
                String pkg = ctx.appSnap.packageName;
                String installer = null;
                String initiating = "";
                try {
                    if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.R) {
                        InstallSourceInfo info = pm.getInstallSourceInfo(pkg);
                        if (info != null) {
                            installer = info.getInstallingPackageName();
                            try {
                                initiating = Ev.nz(info.getInitiatingPackageName());
                            } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                                // 拿不到 initiating 不影响判定
                            }
                        }
                    } else {
                        installer = pm.getInstallerPackageName(pkg);
                    }
                } catch (Throwable t) {
                    sink.unknown("install source query failed: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
                    return;
                }
                ctx.facts.put("apk.installer", Ev.nz(installer))
                        .put("apk.installerInitiator", initiating);

                if (installer == null || installer.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.clean("installer=null (adb/sideload baseline)");
                    return;
                }
                for (String k : KNOWN) {
                    if (k.equals(installer)) {
                        sink.clean("installer=" + installer);
                        return;
                    }
                }
                sink.suspect("installer=" + Ev.clip(installer, 80)
                        + (initiating.isEmpty() ? "" : " initiator=" + Ev.clip(initiating, 60)), 0.4);
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("pkg.installer: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * pkg.proc.name —— /proc/self/cmdline 与 PackageManager 报的包名是否一致。B 级。
     *
     * <p>多开容器/改名共存最常见的手法是把包名换掉，进程名却还留着容器自己的名字。
     * 正常应用（含 :子进程）两者恒等。
     */
    static final class ProcName implements Probe {
        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "pkg.proc.name";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "package-integrity";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 3;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "B";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                if (ctx.appSnap == null) {
                    sink.unknown("snapshot unavailable");
                    return;
                }
                String pkg = Ev.nz(ctx.appSnap.packageName);
                String proc = Ev.nz(ctx.appSnap.processName);
                String cmd = "";
                String raw = ProcFs.readText("/proc/self/cmdline", 8192);
                if (raw != null) {
                    int z = raw.indexOf('\0');
                    cmd = (z >= 0 ? raw.substring(0, z) : raw).trim();
                }
                if (proc.isEmpty()) {
                    proc = cmd;
                }
                ctx.facts.put("proc.name", proc).put("proc.cmdline", cmd);

                String procBase = baseOf(proc);
                String cmdBase = baseOf(cmd);
                if (pkg.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.unknown("package name unavailable");
                } else if (procBase.isEmpty() && cmdBase.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.unknown("process name unreadable");
                } else if (pkg.equals(procBase) || pkg.equals(cmdBase)) {
                    sink.clean("proc=" + Ev.clip(proc, 60) + " pkg=" + pkg);
                } else {
                    sink.suspect("pkg=" + pkg + " proc=" + Ev.clip(procBase, 50)
                            + " cmdline=" + Ev.clip(cmdBase, 50), 0.6);
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("pkg.proc.name: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }

        private static String baseOf(String procName) {
            if (procName == null || procName.isEmpty()) {
                return "";
            }
            int c = procName.indexOf(':');
            return (c > 0 ? procName.substring(0, c) : procName).trim();
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ 工具

    static String shortHex(String hex) {
        if (hex == null) {
            return "";
        }
        return hex.length() <= 16 ? hex : hex.substring(0, 16) + "…";
    }

    static String hexList(List<String> list) {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder("[");
        int n = 0;
        for (String s : list) {
            if (n >= 4) {
                sb.append(",..");
                break;
            }
            if (n > 0) {
                sb.append(',');
            }
            sb.append(shortHex(s));
            n++;
        }
        return sb.append(']').toString();
    }

    static List<String> safe(List<String> l) {
        return l == null ? new ArrayList<String>() : l;
    }

    /** 供其它包内 probe 复用：把 AppSnapshot 的证书列表规范化。 */
    static List<String> normalized(AppSnapshot s) {
        return s == null ? new ArrayList<String>() : new ArrayList<>(safe(s.installCerts));
    }
}

```

`canary/src/io/litmus/core/probes/PrivilegeProbes.java`:

```java
package io.litmus.core.probes;

import android.content.pm.PackageManager;
import android.os.Build;

import io.litmus.core.LitmusContext;
import io.litmus.core.Probe;

import java.io.File;
import java.lang.reflect.Method;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * 提权面（设备平面，Momo 那套）。tier C 为主，SUSPECT 封顶。
 *
 * <p>关键设计：**干净基线**。很多 ROM / 模拟器天生「脏」（ro.debuggable=1、
 * /system 是 overlay 挂载），直接判 TAMPERED 会让分数全是红的 —— 这正是 Momo 被诟病的点。
 * 所以这里只报事实 + 弱结论，由 CLI 的 baseline 归一处理（见 docs/03-评分模型.md §4）。
 *
 * <p>唯一例外是 {@code priv.proc.uid}：uid 区间是硬不变量（应用不可能是 root/system），
 * 所以它是 tier A 并且允许 TAMPERED。
 */
public final class PrivilegeProbes {

    private PrivilegeProbes() {
    }

    public static List<Probe> list() {
        List<Probe> out = new ArrayList<>(10);
        out.add(new ProcUid());
        out.add(new SuBinary());
        out.add(new Magisk());
        out.add(new KernelPatch());
        out.add(new PropsProbe());
        out.add(new Mounts());
        out.add(new Selinux());
        return out;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ A：不变量

    /**
     * priv.proc.uid —— 进程 uid 区间与多视图一致性。
     *
     * <p>A 级不变量：普通应用（走 zygote fork 的 untrusted_app）uid 一定 >= 10000；
     * uid 0/1000/2000/1013 只可能来自 root、系统进程或运行时提权。三处读数还不一致，
     * 说明有人在伪造 /proc 视图。
     */
    static final class ProcUid implements Probe {
        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "priv.proc.uid";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "privilege";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 5;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "A";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                int uid = android.os.Process.myUid();
                int snapUid = ctx.appSnap == null ? -1 : ctx.appSnap.uid;
                int statusUid = ProcFs.statusInt("Uid");
                ctx.facts.put("priv.uid.java", uid)
                        .put("priv.uid.snapshot", snapUid)
                        .put("priv.uid.status", statusUid);

                if (uid < 10000) {
                    sink.tampered("uid=" + uid + " (<10000: root/system/shell)", 1.0);
                } else if (statusUid >= 0 && statusUid != uid) {
                    sink.tampered("uid java=" + uid + " status=" + statusUid + " (views disagree)", 0.95);
                } else if (snapUid >= 0 && snapUid != uid) {
                    sink.suspect("uid java=" + uid + " snapshot=" + snapUid, 0.6);
                } else {
                    sink.clean("uid=" + uid + " (app range, views agree)");
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("priv.proc.uid: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ C：特征匹配

    /** priv.su —— 常见 su 路径是否存在且可执行。tier C：用户可能只是装了 Magisk 未授权。 */
    static final class SuBinary implements Probe {
        private static final String[] CANDIDATES = {
                "/system/bin/su", "/system/xbin/su", "/sbin/su", "/su/bin/su",
                "/data/local/su", "/data/local/xbin/su", "/data/local/bin/su",
                "/system/sd/xbin/su", "/system/bin/failsafe/su", "/data/adb/ksu/bin/su",
                "/debug_ramdisk/su", "/data/adb/su", "/cache/su",
        };

        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "priv.su";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "privilege";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 3;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "C";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                List<String> hit = new ArrayList<>();
                for (String p : CANDIDATES) {
                    try {
                        File f = new File(p);
                        if (f.exists() && f.canExecute() && hit.size() < 4) {
                            hit.add(p);
                        }
                    } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                        // 无权限 stat 就跳过，不算痕迹
                    }
                }
                ctx.facts.put("priv.su", hit);
                if (hit.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.clean("no su binary in " + CANDIDATES.length + " common paths");
                } else {
                    sink.suspect("su=" + Ev.clipList(hit, 3), 0.55);
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("priv.su: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * priv.magisk —— Magisk 目录 / 属性 / 包名。tier C。
     *
     * <p>/data/adb 对普通应用是 EACCES（所以路径检查基本只能拿 false，无害也无用），
     * 真正有效的是**已安装包名**与 /proc/mounts 里的 magisk 挂载源。
     */
    static final class Magisk implements Probe {
        private static final String[] PATHS = {
                "/data/adb/magisk", "/data/adb/modules", "/data/adb/magisk.db",
                "/sbin/.magisk", "/cache/.disable_magisk", "/init.magisk.rc", "/debug_ramdisk",
        };
        private static final String[] PKGS = {
                "com.topjohnwu.magisk", "io.github.huskydg.magisk", "io.github.vvb2060.magisk",
                "com.kingroot.kinguser", "eu.chainfire.supersu", "me.weishu.kernelsu",
                "me.bmax.apatch", "com.rifsxd.ksunext",
        };

        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "priv.magisk";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "privilege";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 3;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "C";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                List<String> hits = new ArrayList<>();
                for (String p : PATHS) {
                    try {
                        if (new File(p).exists() && hits.size() < 4) {
                            hits.add("path:" + Ev.base(p));
                        }
                    } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                        // stat 被拒
                    }
                }

                boolean[] broken = new boolean[1];
                PackageManager pm = ctx.app.getPackageManager();
                for (String pkg : PKGS) {
                    try {
                        pm.getPackageInfo(pkg, 0);
                        if (hits.size() < 4) {
                            hits.add("pkg:" + pkg);
                        }
                    } catch (PackageManager.NameNotFoundException ignored) {
                        // 没装
                    } catch (Throwable t) {
                        broken[0] = true;
                    }
                }

                String mounts = ProcFs.readText("/proc/mounts", 512 * 1024);
                if (mounts != null && mounts.toLowerCase().contains("magisk") && hits.size() < 4) {
                    hits.add("mount:magisk");
                }
                ctx.facts.put("priv.magisk.hits", hits);

                if (!hits.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.suspect("magisk trace=" + Ev.clipList(hits, 3), 0.55);
                } else if (broken[0]) {
                    sink.unknown("package query denied (QUERY_ALL_PACKAGES?)");
                } else {
                    sink.clean("no magisk path/pkg/mount");
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("priv.magisk: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * priv.kernel —— KernelSU/APatch 内核面。/proc/version 是内核对所有进程开放的窗口，
     * 打了 KSU 补丁的内核通常会在版本串里留下痕迹。tier C。
     */
    static final class KernelPatch implements Probe {
        private static final String[] MARKS = {
                "kernelsu", "ksunext", "sukisu", "apatch", "rkernelsu",
        };

        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "priv.kernel";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "privilege";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 3;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "C";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                String ver = ProcFs.readText("/proc/version", 8192);
                if (ver == null) {
                    sink.unknown("/proc/version unreadable");
                    return;
                }
                String lower = ver.toLowerCase();
                String hit = "";
                for (String m : MARKS) {
                    if (lower.contains(m)) {
                        hit = m;
                        break;
                    }
                }
                List<String> dirs = new ArrayList<>();
                for (String p : new String[]{"/data/adb/ksud", "/data/adb/ksu", "/data/adb/apatch"}) {
                    try {
                        if (new File(p).exists() && dirs.size() < 3) {
                            dirs.add(Ev.base(p));
                        }
                    } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                        // 忽略
                    }
                }
                ctx.facts.put("priv.kernel.version", Ev.clip(ver.trim(), 120))
                        .put("priv.kernel.dirs", dirs);

                if (!hit.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.suspect("kernel marker=" + hit + " version="
                            + Ev.clip(ver.trim(), 90), 0.6);
                } else if (!dirs.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.suspect("kernel root dir=" + Ev.clipList(dirs, 2), 0.5);
                } else {
                    sink.clean("kernel version has no ksu/apatch marker");
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("priv.kernel: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * priv.props —— ro.debuggable / ro.secure / service.adb.root。tier C。
     *
     * <p>三处取值来源按可靠性降级：SystemProperties（反射，隐藏 API 可能被拦）→
     * /system/build.prop（多数 ROM 可读）。都拿不到就 UNKNOWN，绝不当成干净。
     */
    static final class PropsProbe implements Probe {
        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "priv.props";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "privilege";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 3;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "C";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                String debug = prop("ro.debuggable");
                String secure = prop("ro.secure");
                String adbRoot = prop("service.adb.root");
                ctx.facts.put("priv.prop.debuggable", debug)
                        .put("priv.prop.secure", secure)
                        .put("priv.prop.adbRoot", adbRoot);

                boolean all = debug.isEmpty() && secure.isEmpty() && adbRoot.isEmpty();
                if (all) {
                    sink.unknown("props unreadable (hidden API blocked + build.prop denied)");
                } else if ("1".equals(debug)) {
                    sink.suspect("ro.debuggable=1 (secure=" + secure + ")", 0.5);
                } else if ("0".equals(secure)) {
                    sink.suspect("ro.secure=0 (debuggable=" + debug + ")", 0.5);
                } else if ("1".equals(adbRoot)) {
                    sink.suspect("service.adb.root=1", 0.55);
                } else {
                    sink.clean("ro.debuggable=" + debug + " ro.secure=" + secure
                            + " service.adb.root=" + adbRoot);
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("priv.props: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ B：交叉视图

    /**
     * priv.mounts —— /proc/mounts 异常挂载。tier B。
     *
     * <p>看两件事：系统分区是不是被 overlay/tmpfs/rw 挂过（Magisk 的 overlay 面具、改装
     * ROM），以及挂载源里有没有 magisk/worker/apatch/ksu 字样。干净机这些分区恒为 ro。
     */
    static final class Mounts implements Probe {
        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "priv.mounts";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "privilege";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 3;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "B";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                String txt = ProcFs.readText("/proc/mounts", 512 * 1024);
                if (txt == null) {
                    sink.unknown("/proc/mounts unreadable");
                    return;
                }
                List<String> anomalies = new ArrayList<>();
                int lines = 0;
                int from = 0;
                while (from < txt.length() && lines < 4096) {
                    int e = txt.indexOf('\n', from);
                    if (e < 0) {
                        e = txt.length();
                    }
                    if (e > from) {
                        lines++;
                        String line = txt.substring(from, e);
                        String[] f = line.split(" ", 4);
                        if (f.length >= 4) {
                            String src = f[0].toLowerCase();
                            String mnt = f[1];
                            String fstype = f[2].toLowerCase();
                            String opts = f[3].toLowerCase();
                            if (src.contains("magisk") || src.contains("worker")
                                    || src.contains("apatch") || src.contains("ksu")) {
                                if (anomalies.size() < 6) {
                                    anomalies.add("src:" + mnt);
                                }
                            } else if (isSystemMount(mnt)) {
                                if ("overlay".equals(fstype) || "tmpfs".equals(fstype)) {
                                    if (anomalies.size() < 6) {
                                        anomalies.add(fstype + ":" + mnt);
                                    }
                                } else if (opts.startsWith("rw")) {
                                    if (anomalies.size() < 6) {
                                        anomalies.add("rw:" + mnt);
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    from = e + 1;
                }
                ctx.facts.put("priv.mounts.lines", lines).put("priv.mounts.anomalies", anomalies);

                boolean suFamily = false;
                for (String a : anomalies) {
                    if (a.startsWith("src:")) {
                        suFamily = true;
                        break;
                    }
                }
                if (anomalies.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.clean("mounts=" + lines + " system/vendor all ro");
                } else if (suFamily) {
                    sink.suspect("su-family mount=" + Ev.clipList(anomalies, 3), 0.75);
                } else {
                    sink.suspect("mount anomalies n=" + anomalies.size() + " "
                            + Ev.clipList(anomalies, 3), 0.6);
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("priv.mounts: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }

        /**
         * 只看**分区根**：/vendor/firmware_mnt 这类子挂载（vfat、rw）在干净 Pixel 上就存在，
         * 拿 startsWith 判会把干净机打成 SUSPECT，所以这里只做精确匹配。
         */
        private static boolean isSystemMount(String mnt) {
            return "/system".equals(mnt) || "/vendor".equals(mnt) || "/product".equals(mnt)
                    || "/system_ext".equals(mnt) || "/odm".equals(mnt)
                    || "/system_root".equals(mnt);
        }
    }

    /**
     * priv.selinux —— getenforce / selinuxfs / 进程域。tier B。
     *
     * <p>permissive 或者 selinuxfs 没挂，等于 MAC 失效；进程域出现 magisk/ksu 说明跑在
     * root 域里。
     */
    static final class Selinux implements Probe {
        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "priv.selinux";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "privilege";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 3;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "B";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                String enforce = null;
                try {
                    Class<?> sc = Class.forName("android.os.SELinux");
                    Method m = sc.getMethod("isSELinuxEnforced");
                    Object v = m.invoke(null);
                    if (v instanceof Boolean) {
                        enforce = ((Boolean) v) ? "1" : "0";
                    }
                } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                    // 隐藏 API 被拦就走文件
                }
                if (enforce == null) {
                    String f = ProcFs.readText("/sys/fs/selinux/enforce", 64);
                    if (f != null && !f.trim().isEmpty()) {
                        enforce = f.trim();
                    }
                }
                String attr = ProcFs.readText("/proc/self/attr/current", 512);
                String domain = attr == null ? "" : attr.trim();
                String mounts = ProcFs.readText("/proc/mounts", 512 * 1024);
                boolean hasSelinuxfs = mounts != null && mounts.contains("selinuxfs");

                ctx.facts.put("priv.selinux.enforce", Ev.nz(enforce))
                        .put("priv.selinux.domain", Ev.clip(domain, 90))
                        .put("priv.selinux.hasSelinuxfs", hasSelinuxfs);

                String lower = domain.toLowerCase();
                if (lower.contains("magisk") || lower.contains(":ksu") || lower.contains("su_domain")) {
                    sink.suspect("process domain=" + Ev.clip(domain, 90), 0.8);
                } else if ("0".equals(enforce)) {
                    sink.suspect("selinux permissive (enforce=0) domain=" + Ev.clip(domain, 60), 0.75);
                } else if (enforce == null && !hasSelinuxfs) {
                    sink.suspect("no selinuxfs in /proc/mounts (SELinux off)", 0.7);
                } else if (enforce == null) {
                    sink.unknown("enforce unreadable (hidden API + /sys denied), selinuxfs present");
                } else {
                    sink.clean("selinux enforcing, domain=" + Ev.clip(domain, 70), 0.9);
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("priv.selinux: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ 属性读取（包内复用）

    private static volatile boolean buildPropLoaded;
    private static volatile String buildPropText;

    /**
     * 读一个系统属性。先试 SystemProperties（反射），失败退 /system/build.prop。
     * 读不到返回 ""（空串），调用方据此判 UNKNOWN。
     */
    static String prop(String key) {
        try {
            Class<?> sp = Class.forName("android.os.SystemProperties");
            Method get = sp.getMethod("get", String.class, String.class);
            Object v = get.invoke(null, key, "");
            if (v instanceof String && !((String) v).isEmpty()) {
                return ((String) v).trim();
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
            // 隐藏 API 被限制：退文件
        }
        return buildProp(key);
    }

    private static String buildProp(String key) {
        if (!buildPropLoaded) {
            buildPropText = ProcFs.readText("/system/build.prop", 1024 * 1024);
            buildPropLoaded = true;
        }
        String txt = buildPropText;
        if (txt == null || txt.isEmpty()) {
            return "";
        }
        String want = key + "=";
        int from = 0;
        while (from < txt.length()) {
            int e = txt.indexOf('\n', from);
            if (e < 0) {
                e = txt.length();
            }
            if (txt.startsWith(want, from)) {
                return txt.substring(from + want.length(), e).trim();
            }
            from = e + 1;
        }
        return "";
    }

    /** 供其它 probe 复用：Build 里有没有 userdebug/test-keys 这类开发态标记。 */
    static boolean isUserdebugBuild() {
        try {
            String tags = Ev.nz(Build.TAGS).toLowerCase();
            String type = Ev.nz(Build.TYPE).toLowerCase();
            return tags.contains("test-keys") || "userdebug".equals(type) || "eng".equals(type);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            return false;
        }
    }
}

```

`canary/src/io/litmus/core/probes/RuntimeIntegrityProbes.java`:

```java
package io.litmus.core.probes;

import io.litmus.core.LitmusContext;
import io.litmus.core.Probe;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileInputStream;
import java.io.RandomAccessFile;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Set;

/**
 * 运行时完整性（tier A/B 的主力，也是「通吃」的核心）。
 *
 * <p>这里的判据全部是**不变量**：不找 Frida/Xposed 的特征，只问「这个进程的内存/系统调用
 * 还符不符合一个未被插桩的进程应有的样子」。因此对任何框架都成立。
 *
 * <p>数据来源两个视图，同一事实两条路径读：
 * <ul>
 *   <li>native 快照（{@link io.litmus.core.NativeSnapshot}，契约 §4.5，一次采样）；</li>
 *   <li>Java 侧自己解析 {@code /proc/self/*}（{@link ProcFs}）。</li>
 * </ul>
 * 两个视图对不上本身就是痕迹（有人只过滤了其中一条路径）。
 *
 * <p>判据阈值全部按「干净 Android 12/13 真机能过」定，宁松不严：拿不准就 SUSPECT/UNKNOWN，
 * 绝不为了抓人把干净机打成 TAMPERED。
 */
public final class RuntimeIntegrityProbes {

    private RuntimeIntegrityProbes() {
    }

    /** 向量顺序 = 报告顺序：先不变量（A/B）后特征（C）。 */
    public static List<Probe> list() {
        List<Probe> out = new ArrayList<>(16);
        out.add(new TracerPid());
        out.add(new ProcPid());
        out.add(new GotIntegrity());
        out.add(new MapFileIntegrity());
        out.add(new MapDeleted());
        out.add(new Memfd());
        out.add(new AnonExec());
        out.add(new MapExecFiles());
        out.add(new Vdso());
        out.add(new Timing());
        out.add(new ThreadName());
        out.add(new StackFrames());
        return out;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ A：不变量

    /**
     * rt.tracerpid —— /proc/self/status 的 TracerPid + native 视图交叉。
     *
     * <p>A 级：ptrace 挂上去就一定非 0，没有任何「正常场景」会写 TracerPid。两条路径读数不一致
     * （一条 0 一条非 0）只有在有人过滤 /proc 时才发生。
     */
    static final class TracerPid implements Probe {
        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "rt.tracerpid";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "runtime-integrity";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 4;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "A";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                int nat = (ctx.nat == null || !ctx.nat.ok) ? -1 : ctx.nat.tracerPid;
                int proc = ProcFs.statusInt("TracerPid");
                ctx.facts.put("rt.tracerpid.proc", proc).put("rt.tracerpid.native", nat);

                if (proc > 0) {
                    sink.tampered("TracerPid=" + proc + " (/proc/self/status)", 0.98);
                } else if (proc == 0) {
                    if (nat > 0) {
                        sink.tampered("status TracerPid=0 but native=" + nat + " (one view filtered)", 0.9);
                    } else {
                        sink.clean("TracerPid=0" + (nat == 0 ? " (status+native agree)" : ""));
                    }
                } else if (nat == 0) {
                    sink.clean("TracerPid=0 (native only; status unreadable)", 0.8);
                } else if (nat > 0) {
                    sink.tampered("native TracerPid=" + nat, 0.95);
                } else {
                    sink.unknown("TracerPid unreadable on both views");
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("rt.tracerpid: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * rt.proc.pid —— 进程号三视图交叉（Java / native / /proc/self/stat）。
     *
     * <p>B 级：正常情况下三者恒等。不一致说明有人替换了 /proc 视图，或 probe 跑在
     * fork 出来的影子进程里。
     */
    static final class ProcPid implements Probe {
        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "rt.proc.pid";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "runtime-integrity";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 2;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "B";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                int javaPid = android.os.Process.myPid();
                int natPid = ctx.nat == null ? -1 : ctx.nat.pid;
                int statPid = ProcFs.statPid();
                ctx.facts.put("rt.proc.pid.java", javaPid)
                        .put("rt.proc.pid.native", natPid)
                        .put("rt.proc.pid.stat", statPid);

                boolean mismatch = (natPid > 0 && natPid != javaPid) || (statPid > 0 && statPid != javaPid);
                if (mismatch) {
                    sink.suspect("pid java=" + javaPid + " native=" + natPid + " stat=" + statPid, 0.65);
                } else if (statPid > 0) {
                    sink.clean("pid=" + javaPid + " (java+stat agree)", 0.9);
                } else {
                    sink.unknown("/proc/self/stat unreadable (pid=" + javaPid + ")");
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("rt.proc.pid: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * rt.got.integrity —— GOT/PLT 落点合法性（native gotCheck）。
     *
     * <p>A 级：GOT 表项被改成指向非目标二进制的地址，是注入的硬痕迹。native 侧尚未实现扫描时
     * scanned=0，这里必须报 UNKNOWN 而不是 CLEAN —— 否则「没测」会被读成「干净」。
     */
    static final class GotIntegrity implements Probe {
        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "rt.got.integrity";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "runtime-integrity";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 4;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "A";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                if (ctx.nat == null || !ctx.nat.ok || ctx.nat.gotScanned < 0) {
                    sink.unknown("native snapshot unavailable");
                    return;
                }
                int scanned = ctx.nat.gotScanned;
                int bad = ctx.nat.gotBad;
                String sample = ctx.nat.gotSamples.isEmpty() ? "" : " e0=" + Ev.clip(ctx.nat.gotSamples.get(0), 90);
                if (scanned <= 0) {
                    sink.unknown("gotCheck scanned=0 (native side not implemented)");
                } else if (bad > 0) {
                    sink.tampered("GOT bad=" + bad + "/" + scanned + sample, 0.95);
                } else {
                    sink.clean("GOT scanned=" + scanned + " bad=0", 0.9);
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("rt.got.integrity: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * rt.maps.file_integrity —— 已映射 .so 的 r-x 段字节 vs 磁盘文件。
     *
     * <p>做法：从 /proc/self/maps 取「可执行 + 文件映射」的段，按 maps 里的 file offset 从
     * /proc/self/mem 读前 192 字节，再从磁盘同一 offset 读同样字节比对。页表是真值，任何
     * 内存补丁（inline hook、热替换、脱壳后改 so）都过不了这一关。
     *
     * <p>降级：Android 15+ 的 exec-only 页 / pkey 会让 /proc/self/mem 读失败，读不到就
     * 跳过；全跳过 → UNKNOWN（不算分），绝不因为读不到就打 TAMPERED。
     */
    static final class MapFileIntegrity implements Probe {
        private static final int MAX_PAGES = 5;
        private static final int BYTES = 192;

        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "rt.maps.file_integrity";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "runtime-integrity";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 4;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "B";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                List<ProcFs.Region> maps = ProcFs.mapsOrNull();
                if (maps == null) {
                    sink.unknown("/proc/self/maps unreadable");
                    return;
                }
                String pkg = ctx.appSnap == null ? "" : Ev.nz(ctx.appSnap.packageName);
                String codeDir = ctx.appSnap == null ? "" : Ev.dirOf(ctx.appSnap.codePath);

                List<ProcFs.Region> picked = new ArrayList<>();
                Set<String> seen = new HashSet<>();
                Set<String> ownPaths = new HashSet<>();
                for (int pass = 0; pass < 2 && picked.size() < MAX_PAGES; pass++) {
                    for (ProcFs.Region r : maps) {
                        if (picked.size() >= MAX_PAGES) {
                            break;
                        }
                        if (!r.exec() || !r.fileBacked()) {
                            continue;
                        }
                        if (!r.path.endsWith(".so") && !r.path.endsWith(".apk")) {
                            continue;
                        }
                        if (pass == 0 && !Ev.isOwnPath(r.path, pkg, codeDir)) {
                            continue;
                        }
                        if (!seen.add(r.path)) {
                            continue;
                        }
                        if (pass == 0) {
                            ownPaths.add(r.path);
                        }
                        picked.add(r);
                    }
                }
                if (picked.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.unknown("no executable file-backed .so/.apk mapping");
                    return;
                }

                RandomAccessFile mem;
                try {
                    mem = new RandomAccessFile("/proc/self/mem", "r");
                } catch (Throwable t) {
                    sink.unknown("/proc/self/mem open denied (" + t.getClass().getSimpleName() + ")");
                    return;
                }

                int checked = 0;
                int bad = 0;
                int ownBad = 0;
                String firstBad = "";
                try {
                    for (ProcFs.Region r : picked) {
                        int len = (int) Math.min((long) BYTES, r.size());
                        if (len <= 0) {
                            continue;
                        }
                        byte[] inMem = new byte[len];
                        if (!ProcFs.readAt(mem, r.start, inMem)) {
                            continue;
                        }
                        byte[] onDisk = ProcFs.readFileAt(r.path, r.offset, len);
                        if (onDisk == null) {
                            continue;
                        }
                        checked++;
                        if (!Arrays.equals(inMem, onDisk)) {
                            bad++;
                            if (ownPaths.contains(r.path)) {
                                ownBad++;
                            }
                            if (firstBad.isEmpty()) {
                                firstBad = Ev.base(r.path) + "+0x" + Long.toHexString(r.offset);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                } finally {
                    ProcFs.close(mem);
                }

                ctx.facts.put("rt.file.integrity.checked", checked)
                        .put("rt.file.integrity.bad", bad)
                        .put("rt.file.integrity.ownBad", ownBad);
                if (checked == 0) {
                    sink.unknown("exec pages unreadable on both sides (exec-only/pkey)");
                } else if (ownBad > 0) {
                    // 自家库的 r-x 段与自家 APK 里的字节不符 —— 只可能是内存被改
                    sink.tampered("own-lib rx!=disk n=" + ownBad + "/" + checked + " e0=" + firstBad, 0.9);
                } else if (bad >= 3) {
                    sink.tampered("system-lib rx!=disk on " + bad + "/" + checked + " e0=" + firstBad, 0.85);
                } else if (bad > 0) {
                    sink.suspect("rx!=disk on " + bad + "/" + checked + " mapping e0=" + firstBad, 0.65);
                } else {
                    sink.clean("rx bytes==disk for " + checked + " mapping(s)", 0.9);
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("rt.maps.file_integrity: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ B：交叉视图

    /**
     * rt.maps.deleted —— maps 里 (deleted) 的 file-backed 段。
     *
     * <p>重打包/热替换的典型形状：进程映射着一份「已删除」的库或 APK（原文件已被换掉）。
     *
     * <p>必须过滤 ART 的伪路径：{@code /dev/ashmem/dalvik-* (deleted)} 与
     * {@code memfd:jit-cache (deleted)} 在**每台**干净机上都存在，不过滤就是 100% 误报。
     */
    static final class MapDeleted implements Probe {
        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "rt.maps.deleted";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "runtime-integrity";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 4;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "B";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                List<ProcFs.Region> maps = ProcFs.mapsOrNull();
                if (maps == null) {
                    sink.unknown("/proc/self/maps unreadable");
                    return;
                }
                String pkg = ctx.appSnap == null ? "" : Ev.nz(ctx.appSnap.packageName);
                String codeDir = ctx.appSnap == null ? "" : Ev.dirOf(ctx.appSnap.codePath);
                String dataDir = ctx.appSnap == null ? "" : Ev.nz(ctx.appSnap.dataDir);

                List<String> hard = new ArrayList<>();   // /data/local/tmp、sdcard、自家 dataDir
                List<String> own = new ArrayList<>();    // 自家 codePath 目录（升级/热替换）
                List<String> other = new ArrayList<>();
                for (ProcFs.Region r : maps) {
                    if (!r.deleted || !r.fileBacked() || ProcFs.isPseudoPath(r.path)) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    if (Ev.isBadRoot(r.path, dataDir)) {
                        hard.add(r.path);
                    } else if (Ev.isOwnPath(r.path, pkg, codeDir)) {
                        own.add(r.path);
                    } else {
                        other.add(r.path);
                    }
                }
                ctx.facts.put("rt.maps.deleted.hard", Ev.clipList(hard, 3))
                        .put("rt.maps.deleted.own", Ev.clipList(own, 3))
                        .put("rt.maps.deleted.other", Ev.clipList(other, 3));

                if (!hard.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.tampered("deleted exec-path mapping n=" + hard.size() + " e0=" + Ev.clip(hard.get(0), 110), 0.85);
                } else if (!own.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.suspect("own codePath deleted-mapped n=" + own.size() + " e0=" + Ev.clip(own.get(0), 110), 0.6);
                } else if (!other.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.suspect("deleted file mapping n=" + other.size() + " e0=" + Ev.clip(other.get(0), 110), 0.5);
                } else {
                    sink.clean("no file-backed (deleted) mapping");
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("rt.maps.deleted: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * rt.memfd —— memfd 可执行加载。
     *
     * <p>memfd 是「无文件注入」的标准载体：payload 不落盘，只在内存里。但 ART 自己的
     * {@code memfd:jit-cache} 是干净的，必须排除，否则干净机全红。
     */
    static final class Memfd implements Probe {
        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "rt.memfd";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "runtime-integrity";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 4;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "B";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                List<ProcFs.Region> maps = ProcFs.mapsOrNull();
                int n;
                int benign = 0;
                List<String> names = new ArrayList<>();
                if (maps != null) {
                    n = 0;
                    for (ProcFs.Region r : maps) {
                        if (!r.exec() || !r.path.contains("memfd:")) {
                            continue;
                        }
                        // memfd 路径不以 '/' 开头，isPseudoPath 一律为真，所以这里必须自己判名字：
                        // ART 的 memfd:jit-cache / app-image 是干净机的常客
                        String lower = r.path.toLowerCase();
                        if (lower.contains("jit") || lower.contains("dalvik")
                                || lower.contains("art-image") || lower.contains("app-image")
                                || lower.contains("gpu")) {
                            benign++;
                            continue;
                        }
                        n++;
                        if (names.size() < 3) {
                            names.add(r.path);
                        }
                    }
                } else if (ctx.nat != null && ctx.nat.memfdCount >= 0) {
                    n = ctx.nat.memfdCount;
                    names.addAll(ctx.nat.memfdSamples.size() > 3
                            ? ctx.nat.memfdSamples.subList(0, 3) : ctx.nat.memfdSamples);
                    benign = -1;
                } else {
                    sink.unknown("maps unreadable + native memfd unavailable");
                    return;
                }
                ctx.facts.put("rt.memfd.count", n).put("rt.memfd.names", Ev.clipList(names, 3));
                String ev = "memfd=" + n + (benign > 0 ? " (art-jit excluded=" + benign + ")" : "")
                        + (names.isEmpty() ? "" : " e0=" + Ev.clip(names.get(0), 90));

                if (n <= 0) {
                    sink.clean(ev);
                } else if (n <= 2) {
                    sink.suspect(ev + " (anonymous exec payload?)", 0.6);
                } else {
                    sink.tampered(ev, 0.8);
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("rt.memfd: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    /** rt.maps.anon_exec —— 匿名可执行映射（hook 引擎的 trampoline 池）。 */
    static final class AnonExec implements Probe {
        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "rt.maps.anon_exec";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "runtime-integrity";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 4;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "B";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                int n;
                long bytes;
                String sample = "";
                List<ProcFs.Region> maps = ProcFs.mapsOrNull();
                if (maps != null) {
                    n = 0;
                    bytes = 0L;
                    for (ProcFs.Region r : maps) {
                        // 真正「无名字」的可执行段；[anon:dalvik-*] 这类有名字的不算
                        if (!r.exec() || !r.path.isEmpty()) {
                            continue;
                        }
                        n++;
                        bytes += r.size();
                        if (sample.isEmpty()) {
                            sample = r.hexRange() + " " + r.perms;
                        }
                    }
                } else if (ctx.nat != null && ctx.nat.anonExecCount >= 0) {
                    n = ctx.nat.anonExecCount;
                    bytes = ctx.nat.anonExecBytes;
                    sample = ctx.nat.anonExecSamples.isEmpty() ? "" : ctx.nat.anonExecSamples.get(0);
                } else {
                    sink.unknown("maps unreadable + native anonExec unavailable");
                    return;
                }
                ctx.facts.put("rt.anon_exec.count", n).put("rt.anon_exec.bytes", bytes);
                String ev = "anon-exec=" + n + " bytes=" + bytes + (sample.isEmpty() ? "" : " e0=" + Ev.clip(sample, 70));

                if (n == 0) {
                    sink.clean(ev);
                } else if (n <= 3 && bytes <= 2L * 1024 * 1024) {
                    // JIT / V8 code range 也就这么大，干净机上允许存在
                    sink.clean(ev + " (jit-like)", 0.8);
                } else if (n <= 8) {
                    sink.suspect(ev, 0.55);
                } else {
                    sink.tampered(ev, 0.8);
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("rt.maps.anon_exec: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * rt.maps.exec_files —— 从「不可能可执行」的路径映射了可执行段。
     *
     * <p>Android 10+ 对 app 可写目录是 W^X 的：{@code /data/local/tmp}、sdcard、自家 data dir
     * 里都不应该出现 r-x 的文件映射。出现即「从外部拉了一份代码执行」。
     */
    static final class MapExecFiles implements Probe {
        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "rt.maps.exec_files";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "runtime-integrity";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 3;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "B";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                List<ProcFs.Region> maps = ProcFs.mapsOrNull();
                if (maps == null) {
                    sink.unknown("/proc/self/maps unreadable");
                    return;
                }
                String pkg = ctx.appSnap == null ? "" : Ev.nz(ctx.appSnap.packageName);
                String dataDir = ctx.appSnap == null ? "" : Ev.nz(ctx.appSnap.dataDir);

                List<String> hard = new ArrayList<>();
                List<String> soft = new ArrayList<>();
                for (ProcFs.Region r : maps) {
                    if (!r.exec() || !r.fileBacked() || ProcFs.isPseudoPath(r.path)) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    if (Ev.isSystemLibPath(r.path)) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    if (Ev.isBadRoot(r.path, dataDir)) {
                        if (!hard.contains(r.path) && hard.size() < 4) {
                            hard.add(r.path);
                        }
                    } else if (!Ev.isOwnPath(r.path, pkg, "")) {
                        if (!soft.contains(r.path) && soft.size() < 4) {
                            soft.add(r.path);
                        }
                    }
                }
                ctx.facts.put("rt.maps.exec.hard", hard).put("rt.maps.exec.soft", soft);

                if (!hard.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.tampered("exec mapping from bad root n=" + hard.size() + " e0=" + Ev.clip(hard.get(0), 110), 0.85);
                } else if (!soft.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.suspect("exec mapping outside whitelist n=" + soft.size() + " e0=" + Ev.clip(soft.get(0), 110), 0.55);
                } else {
                    sink.clean("all exec file mappings under system/codePath", 0.9);
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("rt.maps.exec_files: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * rt.vdso —— vdso 双视图一致性。
     *
     * <p>[vdso] 是内核映射的只读页，大小固定且是页对齐。有人改 syscall 路径（PLT/GOT 或
     * seccomp 注入）时，常常顺带把 /proc/self/maps 也过滤一遍，于是 native 与 Java 两个
     * 视图的 [vdso] 大小对不上。
     */
    static final class Vdso implements Probe {
        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "rt.vdso";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "runtime-integrity";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 3;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "B";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                long natSize = (ctx.nat == null || !ctx.nat.ok) ? 0L : ctx.nat.vdsoSize;
                long javaSize = -1L;
                List<ProcFs.Region> maps = ProcFs.mapsOrNull();
                if (maps != null) {
                    for (ProcFs.Region r : maps) {
                        if ("[vdso]".equals(r.path)) {
                            javaSize = r.size();
                        }
                    }
                }
                ctx.facts.put("rt.vdso.native", natSize).put("rt.vdso.java", javaSize);

                if (natSize <= 0 && javaSize <= 0) {
                    sink.unknown("no [vdso] mapping in either view");
                    return;
                }
                if (natSize > 0 && javaSize > 0 && natSize != javaSize) {
                    sink.suspect("vdso native=" + natSize + "B java=" + javaSize + "B (views disagree)", 0.7);
                    return;
                }
                long size = natSize > 0 ? natSize : javaSize;
                if (size < 4096L || size > 64L * 1024 || (size & 0xfffL) != 0L) {
                    sink.suspect("vdso size=" + size + "B implausible", 0.6);
                } else {
                    sink.clean("vdso=" + size + "B (both views agree)", 0.9);
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("rt.vdso: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * rt.timing —— syscall 耗时抖动。
     *
     * <p>直接读 native 快照里的 200 次 openat 采样（**不自己再采样**，契约 §1.2）。hook 掉
     * libc 的 open/openat 会把中位数抬到微秒甚至毫秒级，或者尾部抖动暴涨。
     *
     * <p>阈值刻意放很宽：干净机 openat 中位数 ~1-3µs，这里卡 2ms，热节流也不会误报。
     */
    static final class Timing implements Probe {
        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "rt.timing";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "runtime-integrity";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 2;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "B";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                if (ctx.nat == null || !ctx.nat.ok || ctx.nat.timingN <= 0) {
                    sink.unknown("native timing not sampled");
                    return;
                }
                long med = ctx.nat.timingMedianNs;
                long p99 = ctx.nat.timingP99Ns;
                long jit = ctx.nat.timingJitterNs;
                int n = ctx.nat.timingN;
                String ev = "openat median=" + med + "ns p99=" + p99 + "ns jitter=" + jit + "ns n=" + n;

                if (med <= 0) {
                    sink.unknown("timing median=0 (clock or seccomp)");
                } else if (med > 2000000L) {
                    sink.suspect(ev + " (median>2ms: libc hook?)", 0.6);
                } else if (p99 > 20000000L) {
                    sink.suspect(ev + " (p99>20ms)", 0.5);
                } else if (jit > 1000000L && jit > 50L * med) {
                    sink.suspect(ev + " (jitter>>median)", 0.45);
                } else {
                    sink.clean(ev, 0.85);
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                sink.error("rt.timing: " + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
            }
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ C：特征（只许 SUSPECT）

    /**
     * rt.thread.name —— 线程名黑名单。
     *
     * <p>tier C：特征匹配，只出 SUSPECT。特征来源是各 hook 引擎自己起的线程名
     * （Frida 的 gum-js-loop/gmain/gdbus/pool-frida、注入器的 linjector…）。
     * 短名用**精确匹配**（gmain 不能拿 contains，否则 ImageMain 之类会误报）。
     */
    static final class ThreadName implements Probe {
        private static final String[] EXACT = {
                "gmain", "gdbus", "gum-js-loop", "gum-js-loop2", "pool-frida", "frida",
                "frida-main", "linjector", "gdbus-proxy", "gum-js",
        };
        private static final String[] CONTAINS = {
                "frida", "gum-js", "linjector", "substrate", "xposed", "lsposed",
                "zygisk", "riru", "sandhook", "dobby",
        };

        @Override
        public String id() {
            return "rt.thread.name";
        }

        @Override
        public String category() {
            return "runtime-integrity";
        }

        @Override
        public int weight() {
            return 3;
        }

        @Override
        public String tier() {
            return "C";
        }

        @Override
        public void run(LitmusContext ctx, Sink sink) {
            try {
                List<String> names;
                if (ctx.nat != null && ctx.nat.ok && !ctx.nat.threadNames.isEmpty()) {
                    names = ctx.nat.threadNames;
                } else {
                    names = ProcFs.taskComms();
                }
                if (names.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.unknown("thread names unavailable");
                    return;
                }
                List<String> hits = new ArrayList<>();
                int renderThread = 0;
                for (String raw : names) {
                    if (raw == null || raw.isEmpty()) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    String nm = raw.trim();
                    String lower = nm.toLowerCase();
                    if ("RenderThread".equals(nm)) {
                        renderThread++;
                    }
                    boolean hit = false;
                    for (String e : EXACT) {
                        if (e.equals(lower)) {
                            hit = true;
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                    if (!hit) {
                        for (String c : CONTAINS) {
                            if (lower.contains(c)) {
                                hit = true;
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if (hit && hits.size() < 4) {
                        hits.add(nm);
                    }
                }
                ctx.facts.put("rt.thread.count", names.size()).put("rt.thread.hits", hits);

                if (!hits.isEmpty()) {
                    sink.suspect("thread names=" + names.size() + " hits=" + Ev.clipList(hits, 4), 0.6);
                } else if (renderThread > 1) {
                    sink.suspect("RenderThread x" + renderThread + " (dup renderer thread)", 0.35);
                } else {
                    sink.clean("thread names=" + names.size() + " no blacklist hit");
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
```
