
`tools/coverage.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""覆盖度审计：目录 vs 实现 vs 攻击面。

回答三个问题：
  1. 目录里有几条、真实现了几条、缺的是哪些
  2. 每个类别里「能下结论的」（tier A/B）和「只能可疑的」（tier C）各占多少
  3. 已知攻击手法（ATTACKS）分别被哪些向量盯着 —— 这张表用来发现"完全没人看"的缺口

ATTACKS 是人工维护的：它代表我们**声称**要防的东西。改这个表比改代码更能反映产品野心，
所以每次加向量后跑一遍，看有没有哪一行是空的。

用法：
    python tools/coverage.py            # 人读表
    python tools/coverage.py --md       # markdown（贴进 docs）
    python tools/coverage.py --json
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import collections
import json
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
VEC = ROOT / "schema" / "vectors.json"
PROBE_DIR = ROOT / "canary" / "src" / "io" / "litmus" / "core" / "probes"

CATEGORIES = [
    ("runtime-integrity", "运行时完整性"),
    ("injection", "注入痕迹"),
    ("package-integrity", "包完整性"),
    ("privilege", "提权面"),
    ("virtualization", "虚拟化/多开/模拟器"),
    ("environment", "环境面"),
]

# 攻击手法 -> 应该盯住它的向量（人工维护：这是「声称的覆盖面」）
ATTACKS: list[tuple[str, str, list[str]]] = [
    ("重打包 / 换签名", "改了 dex 或资源后用自己的证书重签", [
        "pkg.signature", "pkg.signing.v3", "pkg.cert.history",
        "pkg.codepath.hash", "pkg.zip.fingerprint", "pkg.dex", "pkg.manifest",
    ]),
    ("Frida ptrace 注入", "frida-server attach 或 spawn", [
        "rt.tracerpid", "rt.maps.anon_exec", "rt.thread.name", "rt.memfd",
        "inj.frida.port", "inj.frida.socket", "inj.module.paths",
    ]),
    ("Frida gadget 内存加载", "把 agent 放进 memfd / 匿名段，不落盘", [
        "rt.memfd", "rt.maps.anon_exec", "rt.maps.exec_files", "inj.elf.inline",
    ]),
    ("inline hook（Dobby/ShadowHook/whale）", "改写函数入口 16 字节放跳板", [
        "inj.elf.inline", "rt.maps.file_integrity", "rt.got.integrity",
    ]),
    ("PLT/GOT hook", "改 .got 槽指向自己的函数", [
        "rt.got.integrity", "rt.maps.file_integrity", "rt.vdso",
    ]),
    ("Java 层 hook（Xposed/LSPlant）", "改 ArtMethod 入口或 Instrumentation", [
        "rt.art.entrypoint", "rt.stack.frames", "inj.loader.solist",
    ]),
    ("Zygisk / Riru 模块", "进程启动早期注入，无文件痕迹", [
        "rt.maps.file_integrity", "rt.maps.deleted", "inj.module.marker",
        "inj.loader.solist", "inj.dex.inmemory",
    ]),
    ("root / KernelSU / APatch", "su 二进制、内核模块、能力位", [
        "priv.su", "priv.magisk", "priv.kernel", "priv.caps", "priv.props",
        "priv.mounts", "priv.selinux", "priv.proc.uid",
    ]),
    ("模拟器 / 云手机", "qemu 属性、goldfish 设备、GPU/传感器异常", [
        "virt.emulator.props", "virt.emulator.files", "virt.emulator.hardware",
    ]),
    ("多开 / 容器（VirtualApp）", "宿主代跑、uid 错位、进程名异常", [
        "virt.vapp", "virt.vapp.binder", "pkg.proc.name", "virt.multi.user",
    ]),
    ("调试器 attach", "ptrace、JDWP、debuggable 标志", [
        "rt.tracerpid", "env.debuggable", "env.developer",
    ]),
    ("syscall hook / 内核态劫持", "改 syscall 表或 seccomp 过滤", [
        "rt.timing", "rt.vdso", "priv.kernel",
    ]),
    ("DEX 内存加载 / 热修复", "不在磁盘上的 dex 被加载", [
        "inj.dex.inmemory", "pkg.dex", "pkg.codepath.hash",
    ]),
    ("Split APK 篡改", "只替换某个 split", ["pkg.split"]),
]


def load() -> tuple[list[dict], set[str]]:
    data = json.loads(VEC.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    impl: set[str] = set()
    for p in PROBE_DIR.glob("*.java"):
        impl |= set(re.findall(r'String\s+id\s*\(\s*\)\s*\{[^}]*return\s+"([^"]+)"',
                               p.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")))
    return data["vectors"], impl


def main() -> int:
    vectors, impl = load()
    as_json = "--json" in sys.argv
    as_md = "--md" in sys.argv

    by_cat = collections.defaultdict(lambda: collections.Counter())
    for v in vectors:
        c = by_cat[v["category"]]
        c[v["tier"]] += 1
        c["impl" if v["id"] in impl else "todo"] += 1

    if as_json:
        print(json.dumps({
            "catalog": len(vectors), "implemented": len(impl),
            "categories": {k: dict(v) for k, v in by_cat.items()},
            "missing": [v["id"] for v in vectors if v["id"] not in impl],
        }, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
        return 0

    total = len(vectors)
    if as_md:
        print(f"| 类别 | 目录 | 已实现 | 待实现 | tier A | tier B | tier C | 可下结论(A+B) |")
        print("| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |")
        for cid, zh in CATEGORIES:
            c = by_cat[cid]
            n = c["A"] + c["B"] + c["C"]
            print(f"| {zh} (`{cid}`) | {n} | {c['impl']} | {c['todo']} | {c['A']} | {c['B']} | {c['C']} | {c['A']+c['B']} |")
        print(f"| **合计** | **{total}** | **{len(impl)}** | **{total-len(impl)}** | "
              f"**{sum(v['tier']=='A' for v in vectors)}** | **{sum(v['tier']=='B' for v in vectors)}** | "
              f"**{sum(v['tier']=='C' for v in vectors)}** | "
              f"**{sum(v['tier'] in ('A','B') for v in vectors)}** |")
        print()
        print("### 攻击手法覆盖")
        print()
        print("| 手法 | 说明 | 盯着它的向量 | 其中已实现 |")
        print("| --- | --- | ---: | ---: |")
        for name, desc, ids in ATTACKS:
            have = [i for i in ids if i in impl]
            print(f"| {name} | {desc} | {len(ids)} | {len(have)} |")
        return 0

    print(f"目录 {total} 条 / 已实现 {len(impl)} 条 / 缺口 {total - len(impl)} 条\n")
    print(f"{'类别':<20}{'计':>3}{'实现':>5}{'缺':>3}{'A':>4}{'B':>4}{'C':>4}   A+B 占比")
    for cid, zh in CATEGORIES:
        c = by_cat[cid]
        n = c["A"] + c["B"] + c["C"]
        ab = c["A"] + c["B"]
        print(f"{zh:<20}{n:>3}{c['impl']:>5}{c['todo']:>3}{c['A']:>4}{c['B']:>4}{c['C']:>4}   {ab*100//max(n,1)}%")

    print(f"\n{'攻击手法':<28}{'向量':>4}{'已实现':>6}")
    empty = []
    for name, _desc, ids in ATTACKS:
        have = [i for i in ids if i in impl]
        flag = "  ← 缺口" if not have else ""
        print(f"{name:<28}{len(ids):>4}{len(have):>6}{flag}")
        if not have:
            empty.append(name)
    if empty:
        print(f"\n[!] 完全没人盯的手法（{len(empty)}）：{', '.join(empty)}")

    missing = [v["id"] for v in vectors if v["id"] not in impl]
    if missing:
        print(f"\n[!] 目录里有、代码里没有（{len(missing)}）：")
        for m in missing:
            print("    -", m)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

```

`tools/gen_vector_docs.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""从 schema/vectors.json 生成 canary/src/io/litmus/core/VectorDocs.java。

为什么生成而不是手写：App 的详情卡文案、报告的「意味着什么」、文档的判据，
必须永远说的是同一件事。vectors.json 是唯一源，这里是它在 App 侧的投影。

字段：
    zh     一句话判据（来自 docs/02 主表，必填）
    plain  人话解释：这条红了意味着什么（缺失时回落 zh）
    bypass 怎么绕过 / 绕过会踩到哪条别的向量（缺失时给占位提示）

用法：
    python tools/gen_vector_docs.py           # 生成
    python tools/gen_vector_docs.py --check   # 只校验是否需要重新生成（CI 用）
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
VEC = ROOT / "schema" / "vectors.json"
OUT = ROOT / "canary" / "src" / "io" / "litmus" / "core" / "VectorDocs.java"

HEADER = """package io.litmus.core;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 向量的人话文案（构建期由 tools/gen_vector_docs.py 从 schema/vectors.json 生成）。
 *
 * <p><b>不要手工编辑本文件</b>：改 schema/vectors.json 里对应条目的
 * {@code zh} / {@code plain} / {@code bypass}，然后重跑生成器。
 *
 * <p>App 详情卡与 CLI 报告读的是同一份文案，这是「说法一致」的工程保证。
 */
public final class VectorDocs {

    private static final Map<String, String[]> DOC = new HashMap<>();

    static {
"""

FOOTER = """    }

    private VectorDocs() {
    }

    private static String get(String id, int idx) {
        if (id == null) {
            return "";
        }
        String[] v = DOC.get(id);
        return (v == null || idx >= v.length || v[idx] == null) ? "" : v[idx];
    }

    /** 一句话判据。 */
    public static String zh(String id) {
        return get(id, 0);
    }

    /** 人话解释：这条红了意味着什么（没有时回落判据）。 */
    public static String plain(String id) {
        String s = get(id, 1);
        return s.isEmpty() ? zh(id) : s;
    }

    /** 怎么绕过 / 绕过会踩到哪条别的向量。 */
    public static String bypass(String id) {
        String s = get(id, 2);
        return s.isEmpty()
                ? "暂无已整理的绕过路径；按「不变量」思路，要让它变绿必须同时让相关等式重新成立。"
                : s;
    }

    public static int size() {
        return DOC.size();
    }
}
"""

PLACEHOLDER_BYPASS = ""


def jstr(s: str) -> str:
    out = []
    for ch in s:
        if ch == "\\":
            out.append("\\\\")
        elif ch == '"':
            out.append('\\"')
        elif ch == "\n":
            out.append("\\n")
        elif ch == "\t":
            out.append("\\t")
        elif ord(ch) < 0x20:
            out.append("\\u%04x" % ord(ch))
        else:
            out.append(ch)
    return '"' + "".join(out) + '"'


def render(data: dict) -> str:
    lines = [HEADER]
    for v in data["vectors"]:
        vid = v["id"]
        zh = v.get("zh", "")
        plain = v.get("plain", "")
        bypass = v.get("bypass", PLACEHOLDER_BYPASS)
        lines.append(
            f"        DOC.put({jstr(vid)},\n"
            f"                new String[]{{{jstr(zh)}, {jstr(plain)}, {jstr(bypass)}}});\n"
        )
    lines.append(FOOTER)
    return "".join(lines)


def main() -> int:
    check = "--check" in sys.argv
    data = json.loads(VEC.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    content = render(data)

    if check:
        if not OUT.exists():
            print(f"[FAIL] 缺少 {OUT.name}，请运行 python tools/gen_vector_docs.py")
            return 1
        if OUT.read_text(encoding="utf-8") != content:
            print(f"[FAIL] {OUT.name} 与 schema/vectors.json 不同步")
            return 1
        print(f"[OK] {OUT.name} 与 vectors.json 同步（{len(data['vectors'])} 条）")
        return 0

    OUT.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    OUT.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")

    missing_plain = [v["id"] for v in data["vectors"] if not v.get("plain")]
    missing_bypass = [v["id"] for v in data["vectors"] if not v.get("bypass")]
    print(f"已生成 {OUT.relative_to(ROOT)}：{len(data['vectors'])} 条")
    if missing_plain:
        print(f"  ! 缺 plain（会回落 zh）：{len(missing_plain)} 条 → {', '.join(missing_plain[:6])}…")
    if missing_bypass:
        print(f"  ! 缺 bypass（用占位文案）：{len(missing_bypass)} 条")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

```

`tools/lint_vectors.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""向量契约静态检查器。

用途：在 CI 里当门禁，确保「通吃」这条设计承诺不会因为随手加一条特征匹配而退化。

检查项（对应 docs/04-契约.md）：
  L1  id 唯一
  L2  id 前缀与 category 一致（rt./inj./pkg./priv./virt./env.）
  L3  tier 只能是 A/B/C
  L4  weight 在 1..5
  L5  **tier C 的 probe 不得调用 sink.tampered(...)**   ← 硬约束，违反即失败
  L6  id 必须登记在 docs/02-探针目录.md 里（缺失只告警）
  L7  evidence 文案里不许出现换行拼接（'\n'）

用法：
    python tools/lint_vectors.py            # 在仓库根跑
    python tools/lint_vectors.py --strict   # 把 L6 告警也算失败
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import re
import sys
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
PROBE_DIR = ROOT / "canary" / "src" / "io" / "litmus" / "core" / "probes"
CATALOG = ROOT / "docs" / "02-探针目录.md"

CATEGORY_PREFIX = {
    "runtime-integrity": "rt.",
    "injection": "inj.",
    "package-integrity": "pkg.",
    "privilege": "priv.",
    "virtualization": "virt.",
    "environment": "env.",
}

# class X implements Probe ... 直到下一个顶层 class 声明
CLASS_SPLIT = re.compile(r"(?m)^\s*(?:static\s+)?(?:final\s+)?class\s+(\w+)[^{]*implements\s+Probe\b")


@dataclass
class Vector:
    cls: str
    file: str
    id: str = ""
    category: str = ""
    tier: str = ""
    weight: str = ""
    calls_tampered: bool = False
    errors: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)


def _match(pattern: str, body: str, default: str = "") -> str:
    m = re.search(pattern, body)
    return m.group(1) if m else default


def parse_file(path: Path) -> list[Vector]:
    text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    parts = CLASS_SPLIT.split(text)
    # parts = [preamble, name1, body1, name2, body2, ...]
    out: list[Vector] = []
    for i in range(1, len(parts) - 1, 2):
        name, body = parts[i], parts[i + 1]
        v = Vector(cls=name, file=path.name)
        v.id = _match(r'String\s+id\s*\(\s*\)\s*\{[^}]*return\s+"([^"]+)"', body)
        v.category = _match(r'String\s+category\s*\(\s*\)\s*\{[^}]*return\s+"([^"]+)"', body)
        v.tier = _match(r'String\s+tier\s*\(\s*\)\s*\{[^}]*return\s+"([^"]+)"', body)
        v.weight = _match(r'int\s+weight\s*\(\s*\)\s*\{[^}]*return\s+(\d+)', body)
        v.calls_tampered = bool(re.search(r"\bsink\.tampered\s*\(", body))
        out.append(v)
    return out


def main() -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--strict", action="store_true", help="L6 缺失目录登记也算失败")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    if not PROBE_DIR.is_dir():
        print(f"[ERR] 找不到 probe 目录：{PROBE_DIR}")
        return 2

    catalog_text = CATALOG.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") if CATALOG.exists() else ""

    vectors: list[Vector] = []
    for path in sorted(PROBE_DIR.glob("*.java")):
        vectors.extend(parse_file(path))

    fatal: list[str] = []
    warn: list[str] = []
    seen: dict[str, str] = {}

    for v in vectors:
        where = f"{v.file}:{v.cls}"
        if not v.id:
            fatal.append(f"L1 {where} 取不到 id()")
            continue
        if v.id in seen:
            fatal.append(f"L1 id 重复 {v.id}（{seen[v.id]} 与 {where}）")
        seen[v.id] = where

        prefix = CATEGORY_PREFIX.get(v.category)
        if prefix is None:
            fatal.append(f"L2 {where} category='{v.category}' 不在六类里")
        elif not v.id.startswith(prefix):
            fatal.append(f"L2 {where} id='{v.id}' 前缀应为 '{prefix}'")

        if v.tier not in ("A", "B", "C"):
            fatal.append(f"L3 {where} tier='{v.tier}' 不是 A/B/C")

        if not v.weight:
            warn.append(f"L4 {where} 没有覆写 weight()（用默认 3）")
        elif not (1 <= int(v.weight) <= 5):
            fatal.append(f"L4 {where} weight={v.weight} 越界")

        if v.tier == "C" and v.calls_tampered:
            fatal.append(f"L5 {where} tier=C 却调用了 sink.tampered() —— 违反契约 §2.2")

        if v.id not in catalog_text:
            msg = f"L6 {where} id='{v.id}' 未登记在 docs/02-探针目录.md"
            (fatal if args.strict else warn).append(msg)

    by_cat: dict[str, list[Vector]] = {}
    for v in vectors:
        by_cat.setdefault(v.category, []).append(v)

    print(f"vectors: {len(vectors)}\n")
    print(f"{'category':<20}{'n':>4}  tier A/B/C")
    for cat in CATEGORY_PREFIX:
        vs = by_cat.get(cat, [])
        a = sum(1 for v in vs if v.tier == "A")
        b = sum(1 for v in vs if v.tier == "B")
        c = sum(1 for v in vs if v.tier == "C")
        print(f"{cat:<20}{len(vs):>4}  {a}/{b}/{c}")

    if warn:
        print(f"\nWARN ({len(warn)}):")
        for w in warn:
            print("  " + w)
    if fatal:
        print(f"\nFAIL ({len(fatal)}):")
        for f in fatal:
            print("  " + f)
        return 1

    print(f"\nOK · {len(vectors)} 条向量全部符合契约")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

```

`tools/make-repack.ps1`:

```ps1
<#
.SYNOPSIS
    模拟「用户修补了靶包」：往 APK 里塞一个文件并用**另一把密钥**重签。

.DESCRIPTION
    这是 Litmus 的核心场景最小复现：不需要 Frida / XPatch / root，
    只要换签名 + 改内容，就应该被 pkg.* 三条 A 级向量和 CLI 平面全部抓住。

    产物用于对照实验：
        litmus.py run --label C1-clean
        pwsh -File tools/make-repack.ps1
        litmus.py run --label C2-repack --baseline runs/C1-clean-*/run.json
    然后看 run.json 里的 score / blindSpots。

.PARAMETER InputApk
    原始靶包（默认 canary/out/litmus-canary-0.1.0.apk）

.PARAMETER OutApk
    产出的「被修补」包（默认 canary/out/demo-repacked.apk）

.PARAMETER Payload
    塞进去的文件内容（默认一行说明文字）
#>
[CmdletBinding()]
param(
    [string]$InputApk = '',
    [string]$OutApk = '',
    [string]$Payload = 'this file was injected by the "user" to simulate a repack'
)

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$RepoRoot = Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent
if (-not $InputApk) { $InputApk = Join-Path $RepoRoot 'canary\out\litmus-canary-0.1.0.apk' }
if (-not $OutApk) { $OutApk = Join-Path $RepoRoot 'canary\out\demo-repacked.apk' }
if (-not (Test-Path $InputApk)) { throw "找不到输入 APK：$InputApk" }

$JdkHome = if ($env:JAVA_HOME) { $env:JAVA_HOME } else { Split-Path (Split-Path (Get-Command javac).Source -Parent) -Parent }
$SdkRoot = if ($env:ANDROID_SDK_ROOT) { $env:ANDROID_SDK_ROOT }
           elseif ($env:ANDROID_HOME) { $env:ANDROID_HOME }
           else { throw '请设置 ANDROID_SDK_ROOT 或 ANDROID_HOME' }
$BuildTools = (Get-ChildItem (Join-Path $SdkRoot 'build-tools') -Directory | Sort-Object Name -Descending | Select-Object -First 1).FullName
$Keytool = Join-Path $JdkHome 'bin\keytool.exe'
$ZipAlign = Join-Path $BuildTools 'zipalign.exe'
$ApkSigner = Join-Path $BuildTools 'apksigner.bat'
$env:JAVA_HOME = $JdkHome

$KeyDir = Join-Path $RepoRoot 'canary\out\keys'
if (-not (Test-Path $KeyDir)) { New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $KeyDir | Out-Null }
$Ks = Join-Path $KeyDir 'repacker.p12'
if (-not (Test-Path $Ks)) {
    Write-Host '==> 生成「用户自己的」签名密钥（与靶包原签名不同）' -ForegroundColor Cyan
    & $Keytool -genkeypair -keystore $Ks -storetype PKCS12 -storepass repack-pw -keypass repack-pw `
        -alias repacker -keyalg RSA -keysize 2048 -validity 10000 `
        -dname "CN=Repacker, O=NotLitmus, C=CN" -noprompt 2>&1 | Out-Null
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'keytool 生成失败' }
}

$work = Join-Path $RepoRoot 'canary\out\demo'
if (Test-Path $work) { Remove-Item $work -Recurse -Force }
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $work | Out-Null
$tampered = Join-Path $work 'tampered.apk'

Write-Host '==> 1/3 往 APK 里注入一个文件（改内容）' -ForegroundColor Cyan
Copy-Item $InputApk $tampered -Force
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.IO.Compression.FileSystem
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.IO.Compression
$zip = [System.IO.Compression.ZipFile]::Open($tampered, [System.IO.Compression.ZipArchiveMode]::Update)
try {
    $old = $zip.GetEntry('assets/injected-by-user.txt')
    if ($old) { $old.Delete() }
    $entry = $zip.CreateEntry('assets/injected-by-user.txt', [System.IO.Compression.CompressionLevel]::Optimal)
    $s = $entry.Open()
    $bytes = [System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8.GetBytes($Payload + "`n")
    $s.Write($bytes, 0, $bytes.Length)
    $s.Dispose()
} finally { $zip.Dispose() }

Write-Host '==> 2/3 zipalign' -ForegroundColor Cyan
$aligned = Join-Path $work 'aligned.apk'
& $ZipAlign -p -f 4 $tampered $aligned 2>&1 | Out-Null
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'zipalign 失败' }

Write-Host '==> 3/3 用另一把密钥重签（改签名）' -ForegroundColor Cyan
if (Test-Path $OutApk) { Remove-Item $OutApk -Force }
& $ApkSigner sign --ks $Ks --ks-key-alias repacker `
    --ks-pass pass:repack-pw --key-pass pass:repack-pw `
    --min-sdk-version 26 --v1-signing-enabled false `
    --v2-signing-enabled true --v3-signing-enabled true --v4-signing-enabled false `
    --out $OutApk $aligned 2>&1 | Out-Null
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { throw 'apksigner 签名失败' }

$origSha = (Get-FileHash -Algorithm SHA256 $InputApk).Hash.ToLowerInvariant()
$newSha = (Get-FileHash -Algorithm SHA256 $OutApk).Hash.ToLowerInvariant()
$origFp = (python (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'zipfp.py') $InputApk).Trim()
$newFp = (python (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'zipfp.py') $OutApk).Trim()

Write-Host ''
Write-Host "原包   sha256=$($origSha.Substring(0,16))…  zipFp=$($origFp.Substring(0,16))…" -ForegroundColor DarkGray
Write-Host "修补包 sha256=$($newSha.Substring(0,16))…  zipFp=$($newFp.Substring(0,16))…" -ForegroundColor DarkGray
Write-Host ''
Write-Host "下一步：" -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host "  adb -s emulator-5554 install -r `"$OutApk`"" -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host "  python cli/litmus.py run --serial emulator-5554 --label C2-repack --no-install --baseline runs/C1-clean-*/run.json" -ForegroundColor Green

```

`tools/sync_vectors.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""把「实现已落地但目录未登记」的向量补进 docs/02 主表，并重建 schema/vectors.json。

背景：探针实现（io.litmus.core.probes）与文档目录是两条并行线，合流时必然出现
「实现有、目录没有」的 id。CLI 的契约告警（向量不在 schema/vectors.json）就是这件事。

做法：以 docs/02-探针目录.md 的主表为**唯一人工可读源**，把新增行插进对应类别的表尾，
再从表里反向生成 vectors.json，保证两边永不漂移。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
DOC = ROOT / "docs" / "02-探针目录.md"
VEC = ROOT / "schema" / "vectors.json"

# id, category, tier, weight, 判据, 依赖, 状态
NEW_ROWS = [
    ("rt.proc.pid", "runtime-integrity", "B", 2,
     "`Process.myPid()` 与 `/proc/self/stat` 里的 pid 必须一致；多个 pid 视图错位说明有进程伪装或 ptrace 侧通道",
     "java", "v0.1 基线"),
    ("rt.maps.exec_files", "runtime-integrity", "B", 3,
     "maps 中可执行 file-backed 段只允许来自 `codePath`、`/system`、`/apex`、`/vendor`；出现 `/data/local/tmp` 等可写目录即可执行代码落盘",
     "native", "v0.1 基线"),
    ("inj.module.marker", "injection", "C", 4,
     "已加载模块/映射路径里出现注入框架标记目录名（`/data/adb/modules`、`/data/local/tmp/`、`riru`、`zygisk` 等）",
     "native", "v0.1 基线"),
    ("pkg.proc.name", "package-integrity", "B", 3,
     "`Application.getProcessName()` 必须是包名本身或以 `<包名>:` 开头的子进程；出现宿主包名说明被容器代跑",
     "java", "v0.1 基线"),
    ("priv.proc.uid", "privilege", "A", 5,
     "`Process.myUid()` 必须落在普通应用 uid 区间且与 PMS 记录一致；容器/双开把 uid 换成宿主或隔离 uid 时该等式不成立",
     "java", "v0.1 基线"),
]

ROW_RE = re.compile(r"^\|\s*`([a-z][a-zA-Z0-9._]*)`\s*\|")


def parse_rows(text: str) -> list[tuple[str, str, str, int, str, str, str]]:
    out = []
    for line in text.splitlines():
        m = ROW_RE.match(line)
        if not m:
            continue
        cols = [c.strip() for c in line.strip().strip("|").split("|")]
        if len(cols) < 7:
            continue
        vid = cols[0].strip("`")
        out.append((vid, cols[1], cols[2], int(cols[3]) if cols[3].isdigit() else 3,
                    cols[4], cols[5], cols[6]))
    return out


def fix_notes(text: str) -> str:
    """把数不清的历史口径统一掉。"""
    text = text.replace("48 行主表", "53 行主表").replace("主表 **48 行**", "主表 **53 行**")
    text = text.replace("48 条", "53 条").replace("48 行", "53 行")
    return text


def main() -> int:
    text = DOC.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    rows = parse_rows(text)
    have = {r[0] for r in rows}
    print(f"目录现有 {len(rows)} 条")

    missing = [r for r in NEW_ROWS if r[0] not in have]
    if missing:
        lines = text.splitlines()
        for vid, cat, tier, weight, why, dep, status in missing:
            prefix = {
                "runtime-integrity": "rt.", "injection": "inj.",
                "package-integrity": "pkg.", "privilege": "priv.",
                "virtualization": "virt.", "environment": "env.",
            }[cat]
            newline = f"| `{vid}` | {cat} | {tier} | {weight} | {why} | {dep} | {status} |"
            # 插到同类别的最后一行之后
            last = -1
            for i, ln in enumerate(lines):
                m = ROW_RE.match(ln)
                if m and ln.lstrip().startswith("| `" + prefix):
                    last = i
            if last < 0:
                print(f"  [!] 找不到 {prefix} 类别的表，跳过 {vid}")
                continue
            lines.insert(last + 1, newline)
            print(f"  [+] 插入 {vid} → 第 {last + 2} 行")
        text = "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
        text = fix_notes(text)
        DOC.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
        rows = parse_rows(text)
    else:
        print("无需插入")

    # 反向生成 vectors.json
    data = json.loads(VEC.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    old = {v["id"]: v for v in data.get("vectors", [])}
    # 人写的解释性字段（plain/bypass/fix）不在 docs 表里，重建时必须原样保留，
    # 否则每同步一次就要重新写 53 条人话。
    PRESERVE = ("plain", "bypass", "fix", "risk")

    def plane(vid: str, cat: str) -> str:
        if vid in old:
            return old[vid].get("plane", "self")
        if cat == "package-integrity" and vid in ("pkg.codepath.hash", "pkg.zip.fingerprint"):
            return "cli"
        if cat in ("privilege", "virtualization", "environment"):
            return "device"
        if vid in ("inj.frida.port", "inj.frida.socket", "inj.module.marker", "inj.module.paths"):
            return "device"
        return "self"

    entries = []
    for (vid, cat, tier, weight, why, _dep, _status) in rows:
        entry = {
            "id": vid, "category": cat, "plane": plane(vid, cat),
            "tier": tier, "weight": weight, "zh": why,
        }
        for k in PRESERVE:
            if vid in old and k in old[vid]:
                entry[k] = old[vid][k]
        entries.append(entry)
    data["vectors"] = entries
    data["count"] = len(entries)
    data["note"] = (
        "本文件由 tools/sync_vectors.py 从 docs/02-探针目录.md 的主表反向生成，"
        "不要手工编辑。目录 = 人工可读的唯一源；契约 §3 的命名规则约束 id 形态。"
    )
    VEC.write_text(json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"vectors.json 已重建：{len(entries)} 条")

    by_cat: dict[str, int] = {}
    for e in entries:
        by_cat[e["category"]] = by_cat.get(e["category"], 0) + 1
    print("  " + " / ".join(f"{k}={v}" for k, v in by_cat.items()))
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

```

`tools/zipfp.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""计算 APK 的 ZipEntry 指纹（契约 docs/04-契约.md §4.4）。

这是**唯一实现**：build.ps1 与 cli/litmus.py 都必须调它，保证逐字节一致。
算法：
    按 central directory 顺序遍历 entry
    line = f"{name}|{crc32:08x}|{size}|{compressedSize}|{method}\\n"
    fingerprint = sha256(utf8(concat(lines))).hexdigest()

用法：
    python tools/zipfp.py <apk>
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import sys
import zipfile


def zip_fingerprint(path: str) -> str:
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with zipfile.ZipFile(path) as z:
        for info in z.infolist():
            line = (
                f"{info.filename}|{info.CRC:08x}|{info.file_size}|"
                f"{info.compress_size}|{info.compress_type}\n"
            )
            h.update(line.encode("utf-8"))
    return h.hexdigest()


def main() -> int:
    if len(sys.argv) != 2:
        print("usage: python tools/zipfp.py <apk>", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    print(zip_fingerprint(sys.argv[1]))
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

```
